The Chevalier hath sworn an oath of submission, but I trust him not. Now I will invest the castle. It shall be my quarters, and thine. The town is quiet?”

“Ay. The people are amazed at thy clemency. All France thinks King Hal an ogre.”

Simon rose.

===Chinese===

“他们自然料不到。那样一个香喷喷的朝廷。那之后就简单了。他们引我去看艾伦，他躺在塔楼里一间雅致的房间里。他受了伤，但我想不重。随后我便到了此地，携夫人作人质。那位骑士已宣誓归降，但我信不过他。眼下我要进驻城堡：它做我的驻地，也做你的。城里安静么？”

“安静。百姓对你的宽仁惊讶不已。全法兰西都当哈尔王（King Hal）是个吃人的妖魔。”

西蒙起身。

===Original===

“I must see Huntingdon. Where is he?”

“By the southern gate. His men bring the baggage into the town. Where wilt thou quarter thy men, Simon?”

“Some here. I make provision for the others this day. Geoffrey, summon a score of thine own men-at-arms, and a score of the men of Beauvallet. I will have thee ride into the castle and make all ready. They will not offer ye resistance, for fear lest I should slay the Countess.”

===Chinese===

“我得去见亨廷登（Huntingdon）。他在哪儿？”

“在南门。他的人正往城里搬运辎重。你打算把人马驻在哪儿，西蒙？”

“一部分驻在这里，其余的今天安排。杰弗里，去召集你自己名下的二十名甲士，再添二十名博瓦莱的人。我要你骑马进堡，把一切备办停当。他们不敢阻拦你，生怕我一剑杀了伯爵夫人。”

===Original===

Geoffrey picked up his cap.

“Thou’lt not ride in thyself?”

“Nay, there is work for me here. Take what arms ye find, Geoffrey, and keep the court under close surveillance. I would confine the Chevalier, but that he submitted. Watch him. I will come later. Take Master Hubert for Alan,” he added. “I trust not their French leech.”

Geoffrey lounged out, yawning.

===Chinese===

杰弗里拾起帽子。

“你不亲自进去？”

“不，这里有事离不开我。见到的兵刃一律收走，杰弗里，再把那一朝的人等严密看管起来。那骑士我本想囚起来，无奈他已归降。盯紧他。我晚些过去。让休伯特老爷（Master Hubert）去照应艾伦，”他补了一句。“他们的法国医士，我信不过。”

杰弗里打着哈欠，懒洋洋地踱了出去。

===Original===

“Heigh-ho! When shall we be quit of this troublesome town, I wonder?” At the door he paused, and looked back at Simon.

“She is lovely enow, lad, but I like not that termagant beauty.”

Simon drew the ink-horn towards him.

“Lovely? Oh, ay!”

===Chinese===

“唉嗨！咱们几时才甩得脱这座麻烦的城池呢，我倒想知道？”到得门口，他停步回头，望了望西蒙。

“她是够美的，小子，可我不喜欢那股子悍泼的艳色。”

西蒙把墨水瓶拉到面前。

“美么？哦，可不是！”

===Original===

“Thou hadst not remarked it?” An impish smile danced across Geoffrey’s mouth. “Take heed lest she slight thee for Alan.”

Simon’s hand travelled slowly across the paper. He laughed.

“Holy Virgin! She would kill Alan with but a look. She will kill me an I watch her not.”

“And dost thou admire the tigress, my brother?”

===Chinese===

“你倒不曾留意？”一丝顽皮的笑意在杰弗里嘴角跳荡。“当心哪，她为了艾伦可要冷落你了。”

西蒙执笔的手在纸上缓缓移过。他笑了。

“圣母在上！她只消一个眼神便能杀了艾伦。我若不留神，她也会要了我的命。”

“这么说，你赏识这头母老虎么，我的兄弟？”

===Original===

“Not I.” He paused in his writing. “She is very brave,” he added reflectively.

“She would have slain thee foully,” Geoffrey said solemnly.

“Ay. She is a woman. Get thee gone, Geoffrey, and summon thy men.”

“Oh, I go, I go! I leave thee to dream of thine Amazon.”

===Chinese===

“才不呢。”他笔下停了一停。“她非常勇敢，”他若有所思地补上一句。

“她本会卑鄙地杀了你，”杰弗里一本正经地说。

“是啊。她是女人。去办你的差去吧，杰弗里，把你的人召集起来。”

“哦，这就走，这就走！留你在这儿，梦你的亚马逊悍女去。”

===Original===

Simon smiled.

“Ye leave me to quarter my men,” he said.

===Chinese===

西蒙一笑。

“你留下我，是要我安顿人马。”他说。

## VI / 第六章

===Original===
How the Lady Margaret Could Not Stab Him

===Chinese===
玛格丽特夫人如何刺他不成

===Original===
The Lady Margaret sat on a raised dais, looking out of the window on to the bleak gardens of her castle. A fire burned at the far end of the chamber, and by it were gathered some four or five of her ladies, chattering together, and stitching at a length of canvas. The Lady Margaret sat with head averted and resting on her slender hand. She was dressed all in dull yellow, and her black hair lay over her shoulders in two great braids. A gold net covered her head and hung down to below her knees. Presently she sighed, and turned impatiently.

“Get ye gone, get ye gone!” she commanded petulantly. “Your silly chattering goes through my head. Jeanne, stay with me.”

The ladies departed softly, taking their work with them. The little lady who had smiled upon Geoffrey that day in the justice-house seated herself by the table, and looked up at her mistress gravely.

Margaret plucked nervously at her gown with fingers that quivered. Her delicate nostrils were a little dilated, and the long black eyes were troubled.

===Chinese===
玛格丽特夫人（the Lady Margaret）坐在高台之上，望着窗外城堡里一片萧瑟的园子。房间尽头燃着火，四五个女官围坐火边，叽叽喳喳地闲谈，往一幅长长的绣布上飞针走线。玛格丽特夫人偏着头，纤手支颐。她通身暗黄衣裙，一头黑发编成两条粗辫垂在肩头，金线发网罩着头顶，直垂到膝下。不一会儿，她叹了口气，不耐烦地转过身来。

“都走，都走！”她使着性子呵道。“你们叽叽喳喳，吵得我脑仁生疼。让娜（Jeanne），留下陪我。”

女官们带着针线，轻手轻脚地退了出去。那日在审判厅（justice-house）里对杰弗里（Geoffrey）嫣然一笑的小女官在桌边坐下，神色郑重地仰望她的女主人。

玛格丽特手指微颤，心神不宁地揪着自己的裙衣。精致的鼻翼微微翕张，一双细长的黑眼睛盛满烦乱。

===Original===
“Ay, thou art calm!” she said suddenly, and turned fiercely upon her companion. “Tell me how I may detest this English bully!”

Jeanne folded her hands. A smile hovered about her mouth as she answered.

“Why, Margot, it seems that he is﻿—a man.”

“What mean you? A man! Ay, and an uncouth boor!”

===Chinese===
“是啊，你是够镇定的！”她突然说道，恶狠狠地转向同伴。“告诉我，我怎样才能恨透这个英格兰恶霸！”

让娜双手交叠，唇边噙着一丝笑意答道：

“嗳，玛戈（Margot），依我看哪，他到底是个——男人。”

“你这是什么意思？男人！是啊，一个粗野的莽夫！”

===Original===
“But still a man,” nodded Jeanne de Faucourt. “He hath thy measure, Margot, *chérie*.”

“Ye think he will vanquish me? Ye think that?”

“Why, I know not! Perchance. For till now thou hast known no man.”

Margaret sprang up and came down from the dais.

===Chinese===
“可终究是个男人。”让娜·德·福库尔（Jeanne de Faucourt）点头。“他早摸透了你的斤两，玛戈，亲爱的。”

“你以为他会降住我？你是这么想的？”

“唉，我说不好！也许吧。因为直到如今，你还不曾真正见识过男人。”

玛格丽特霍地起身，走下高台。

===Original===
“Oh, ay, ay! Thou art at one with this bully! Geoffrey of Malvallet hath bewitched thee!”

Jeanne went a rosy red.

“Nay, madame!”

The Countess laughed angrily.

===Chinese===
“哦，是啊，是啊！你竟帮着这恶霸说话！马尔瓦莱（Malvallet）的杰弗里把你迷住了！”

让娜羞得满脸绯红。

“没有的事，夫人！”

伯爵夫人怒极反笑。

===Original===
“Think ye I have no eyes? An Englishman! Thou!”

“He﻿—he is very courtly, Margot,” Jeanne pleaded.

“Very courtly! To march into my domain, disarming my servants, wassailing in my hall at Christmastide! Oh, he charms thine ears with compliments, I make no doubt! Soon ye will desert me entirely!”

“Madame!” Jeanne rose, trembling.

===Chinese===
“你当我眼睛瞎了么？一个英格兰人！你呀！”

“他——他为人很斯文有礼，玛戈。”让娜分辩道。

“好一个斯文有礼！长驱直入我的领地，缴了我仆从的械，圣诞节期还在我大厅里开怀畅饮！哦，他那张甜嘴必是把恭维话说得你心花怒放，这还用疑！要不了多久，你就要把我整个儿抛在脑后了！”

“夫人！”让娜颤巍巍地站起身。

===Original===
Margaret ran to her, and caught her in her arms.

“Nay, I meant it not! I﻿—I am distraught with trouble! Jeanne, I did not say it! It was not me!”

Jeanne thrust her gently into a chair, bending over her and stroking her hands.

“Poor Margot! Poor Margot!” she crooned and drew the proud head to rest on her shoulder.

===Chinese===
玛格丽特奔到她跟前，一把将她搂进怀里。

“不，我不是那个意思！我——我是愁得昏了头！让娜，我没说过那话！不是我说的！”

让娜轻轻扶她在椅上坐了，俯身抚着她的双手。

“可怜的玛戈！可怜的玛戈！”她低声哄慰着，把那颗高傲的头颅拉过来，靠在自己肩上。

===Original===
Margaret clung to her, sobbing for a space, but soon she disengaged herself and dashed her hand across her eyes.

“Cry! I! I﻿—I have seldom done that, Jeannette.”

“Thou art too warlike,” Jeanne chided her, and knelt by the chair. “Margot, Margot, make thy submission! To what avail this tilting against Lord Simon? He hath the advantage of thee in that he is a man, and holds thy lands beyond recall. Be wise, *mignonne*! Be wise!”

“If I could but escape!” Margaret fretted. “If I could but reach Turincel!”

===Chinese===
玛格丽特偎着她抽泣了一阵，不多时便挣开身子，抬手在眼上狠狠一抹。

“哭！我！我——我几乎从不落泪的，小让娜（Jeannette）。”

“你太好斗了。”让娜嗔怪着她，在椅边跪下。“玛戈，玛戈，俯首称臣吧！这样硬顶西蒙（Simon）大人叫阵，又能争得什么好处？他占着一条便宜——他是男人；何况你的领地攥在他手心，再也要不回来。放聪明些，小宝贝！放聪明些！”

“只要我能逃出去！”玛格丽特焦躁地说。“只要我能赶到图兰塞尔（Turincel）！”

===Original===
“Turincel! Why, *chérie*, it is ten leagues distant!”

“What matter? If I could reach it, Fernand de Turincel would aid me! Aid me to throw this Beauvallet out of my land!”

“Yes, Margot, yes, but thou canst not escape, and thou canst not journey ten leagues alone.”

Up went the dark head.

===Chinese===
“图兰塞尔！天哪，亲爱的，那可有十里格之遥！”

“那又何妨？我若能赶到那里，费尔南·德·图兰塞尔（Fernand de Turincel）自会助我！助我把这个博瓦莱（Beauvallet）逐出我的领地！”

“是，玛戈，是，可你逃不出去，你也无法只身跋涉十里格的路。”

那颗黑发头颅倏地扬起。

===Original===
“Ay, but that could I! Why, Jeanne, hast forgotten my strength?”

“But thou art a woman, *chérie*,” Jeanne said gently.

“An Amazon!” Margaret came to her feet, eyes flashing. “He calls me that, the English tyrant! Well, I will show him what an Amazon can do!”

Jeanne sat back on her heels, staring meditatively into the fire.

===Chinese===
“噢，那个我做得到！怎么，让娜，你忘了我的膂力么？”

“可你终究是个女人，亲爱的。”让娜温言道。

“亚马逊悍女！”玛格丽特一跃而起，目光如电。“那英格兰暴君就是这么叫我的！好，我倒要让他瞧瞧，亚马逊悍女能做出什么来！”

让娜跪坐着往后一靠，出神地凝望火光。

===Original===
“He is a strange man, this Lord of Beauvallet,” she remarked. “His men do worship him, yet he is stern and silent. And he is tender with the children.”

“Tender with the children? He would have slain them!”

“Sir Geoffrey told me, no. He is half-brother to Lord Simon, and he says that if any man maltreat a child, Lord Simon’s hand is heavy upon that man.”

“Lies, lies! He is cruel, I tell thee! Cruel!”

===Chinese===
“他真是个奇人，这位博瓦莱大人。”她说。“他麾下的人对他奉若神明，可他冷面寡言。对孩子，他却又温柔得很。”

“对孩子温柔？他不是扬言要杀那些孩子么！”

“杰弗里爵士告诉我，不是的。他是西蒙大人同母异父的哥哥，他说，谁若苛待孩子，西蒙大人决不轻饶那人。”

“撒谎，撒谎！我告诉你，他残忍！残忍！”

===Original===
“Nay, he hath treated thee fairly, Margot.”

The Countess swung round to face her, bosom heaving.

“Thou dost think that? What of this scar I bear upon my breast? Thou didst see him press his sword into my flesh! What of this bruise on my wrist? It is three weeks now since he gripped my arm, but still I bear the marks of his fingers!”

Jeanne looked up at her mistress.

===Chinese===
“不，他待你是公道的，玛戈。”

伯爵夫人霍然转身面向她，胸口剧烈起伏。

“你竟这么想？那我胸口这道疤算什么？你亲眼见他拿剑抵进我的皮肉！那我腕上这块淤青算什么？他抓我的胳膊，到如今三个星期了，指痕还留在我身上！”

让娜抬头望着她的女主人。

===Original===
“I think that scar will always remain,” she said pensively.

“Ay! And so shall I always remember! I will not rest until I have avenged myself! Jeanne, Jeanne, have ye forgotten how he used me, under the eyes of mine own people? Have ye forgotten how he put me to shame in the open street?”

“Nay, none knew thee, and he said naught.”

Up and down the room paced my lady, lashing herself into fury.

===Chinese===
“我看这道疤是要留一辈子了。”她若有所思地说。

“哼！所以我也要记一辈子！此仇不报，我誓不罢休！让娜，让娜，你忘了他在我自己子民的眼皮底下怎样作践我么？你忘了他怎样当街羞辱我么？”

“没有的事，那时无人认得你，他也未曾多言。”

夫人在房里踱来踱去，愈想愈怒，把自己撩拨得火冒三丈。

===Original===
“Would that I had slain his Alan! Thus should I have hurt him! Ay, to the quick! Ah, why did I seek to treat with him?”

“Ye could not have slain Sir Alan. Ye do know that, Margot.”

“That could I! It was his threat that persuaded me! An empty threat, thou sayst! I would I had laughed at it.”

“He would have found another way,” Jeanne said slowly. “He is not easily worsted, Margot.”

===Chinese===
“早知如此，我该杀了他的艾伦！那样才算真正伤了他！对，伤到痛处！唉，我当初何苦要同他讲和？”

“你杀不了艾伦爵士的。你自己心里明白，玛戈。”

“我做得到！是他的威胁逼我就范！你说那是句空话！早知如此，我真该一笑置之。”

“他自会另寻他法。”让娜缓缓地说。“他不是轻易能挫败的人，玛戈。”

===Original===
“We will see!” The black eyes narrowed. “She-devil, he called me!”

A soft knock fell on the door. Jeanne rose to admit the Chevalier. Instantly Margaret’s passion left her. The colour died out of her cheeks, and her mouth took on its haughty curve.

The Chevalier came bowling into the room.

“Sweet cousin, thou art well?”

===Chinese===
“走着瞧！”那双黑眼睛眯细了。“母夜叉，他就是这么叫我的！”

门上响起一记轻叩。让娜起身去迎弗勒里瓦尔骑士（the Chevalier）。玛格丽特的怒火霎时敛尽，双颊血色褪去，嘴角重新挂起倨傲的弧度。

骑士一阵风似的卷进房来。

“好表姐，你安好么？”

===Original===
“Well enough. What want ye, Victor?”

“Always so cold!” he languished. He watched Jeanne withdraw to the window, and came closer to his cousin. “The English bear grows careless, methinks. He sits writing in the hall with none to guard his back. For once his faithful squire is absent.”

She was indifferent, moving away from him.

“I brought thee this, Margot,” the Chevalier said softly. Into her hand he slid a dagger with a jewelled hilt.

===Chinese===
“还好。你有何事，维克托（Victor）？”

“总是这般冷若冰霜！”他矫揉造作地叹道。他望着让娜退到窗边，便凑近表姐。“依我看，那头英格兰熊渐渐大意了。他独自坐在大厅里写字，无人替他护住后背。他那忠心的扈从（squire）难得不在跟前。”

她不置可否，从他身旁走开。

“我给你带来了一样东西，玛戈。”骑士低声说。他往她手心里塞了一把镶宝石柄的匕首。

===Original===
Her lip curled.

“What would ye have me do with it?” She tossed it on to the table.

“Make thyself mistress yet again,” he answered, watching her.

“Stab him in the back? Pah!”

===Chinese===
她撇了撇嘴。

“你要我拿它做什么？”说着把它丢在桌上。

“让你重新做这里的主人。”他答道，眼睛盯住她。

“从背后捅他？呸！”

===Original===
The Chevalier shrugged, spreading out his hands.

“A woman ’gainst a man. What matter?”

She drew herself up, looking scorn upon him.

“Ye grow noisome, Victor. Stab him thyself, if thou wilt.”

===Chinese===
骑士耸耸肩，摊开双手。

“女人对付男人，还讲什么章法？”

她挺直身子，满眼鄙夷地望着他。

“你越来越惹人厌了，维克托。你若有胆，自己去捅。”

===Original===
“Oh, I have submitted!” the Chevalier said nonchalantly. “Else would I surely stab him, and rid this land of his tyranny.” He paused, and shot her a sidelong look. “Thou wert not always so nice, sweet Margot. Perchance thou durst not essay this venture?”

That stung her.

“Durst not! Do ye think I fear Simon of Beauvallet?”

“He is very ruthless,” the Chevalier answered. “But a quick stroke from behind.﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
“哦，我可是已经归顺了！”骑士满不在乎地说。“否则我定会亲手捅了他，替这片土地除掉他的暴政。”他顿了顿，斜睨她一眼。“好玛戈，你从前可没这么多讲究。莫不是，你不敢试这一遭？”

这话刺痛了她。

“不敢！你以为我怕博瓦莱的西蒙么？”

“他素来狠辣无情。”骑士答道。“可只要从背后迅速一击……”

===Original===
“Ah, you sicken me!” she cried. “If I slay him ’twill not be from behind! Get thee gone from my room!”

The Chevalier walked mincingly to the door. He paused by the table as if to pick up the dagger.

“Leave it!” Margaret said sharply.

When he had gone, she swept to the table and hid the dagger in the bottom of her dress.

===Chinese===
“啊，你让我恶心！”她喊道。“我若杀他，绝不从背后下手！给我滚出这房间！”

骑士扭扭捏捏地走向门口，在桌边停了停，像要去拾那把匕首。

“由它搁着！”玛格丽特厉声道。

他走后，她疾步掠到桌前，把匕首藏进裙衣深处。

===Original===
“I would be alone, Jeanne.”

Jeanne rose, and without a word left the room. The door closed behind her, and once again the Lady Margaret fell to pacing the floor. At length she stopped, and drew the dagger from its hiding-place. Then, gathering her skirts close about her, so that they made no sound, she went to the door, and opened it. Before her the stone stairs led down to the great hall. Tiptoeing she approached them, and slowly descended.

In the middle of the hall Simon sat, his back turned towards her, writing. The scratching of his quill on the parchment was the only sound to be heard. He wore no armour, and his back was fair mark for an assassin’s dagger.

The Lady Margaret paused on the bottom step, hardly daring to breathe. Cautiously she stepped down, her little, soft-slippered foot making no sound on the stone floor. Inch by inch she went forward, never taking her eyes from that fair head, her dagger held ready. She meant to creep up to him and to strike him above the heart before he could save himself. Her lips were slightly parted, but her hand was steady, despite the wild beating of her heart. Nearer and nearer she approached until she was but three paces from him.

===Chinese===
“我想独自静一静，让娜。”

让娜起身，一言不发地退出房间。门在她身后合拢，玛格丽特夫人又踱起步来。走了一阵，她终于停住，把匕首从藏处抽出。随后，她拢紧裙裾，不让它发出半点声响，走到门前，拉开门扉。面前一道石阶直下大厅。她蹑着足尖走近石阶，缓缓而下。

大厅正中，西蒙背对着她坐着写字。羽笔划过羊皮纸的沙沙声，是屋里唯一的声音。他未着甲胄，那后背正是刺客匕首的好靶子。

玛格丽特夫人在最末一级台阶上屏息伫立，几乎不敢喘气。她小心翼翼地踏下台阶，软底小鞋落在石板上悄然无声。她一寸一寸向前挪，目光一刻不离那颗俊美的头颅，匕首蓄势待发。她打算蹑到近前，赶他来不及自救，一击刺入他心脏上方。她双唇微启，手却稳稳的，任凭心口狂跳。她越逼越近，直到离他只剩三步。

===Original===
Simon’s hand travelled to and fro across the parchment. He did not lift his head. The silence seemed to grow, and still the Lady Margaret crept on. Then Simon spoke, his voice deep and calm.

“Strike where the neck joins the shoulder, my lady,” he said, and went on writing.

The Lady Margaret started back, letting fall her skirts. Her hand flew to her cheek, and now it was trembling. Her face went white, and her eyes dilated. Of a sudden she had grown cold, and her knees threatened to give way.

Simon signed his name elaborately, and sprinkled sand over the parchment. Then, and then only, did he rise and face the Countess.

===Chinese===
西蒙的手在羊皮纸上来来回回，头也不曾抬起。寂静仿佛越涨越满，玛格丽特夫人仍在一点点挪近。这时西蒙开口了，声音低沉而平静。

“往颈项与肩头相接处刺，夫人。”他说罢，继续写他的字。

玛格丽特夫人猛地倒退，拢着的裙裾散落下来。她的手一下子捂住面颊，此刻竟抖了起来。她面色煞白，双目圆睁。一股寒意蓦地浸透全身，双膝几乎支撑不住。

西蒙一笔一划地签下名字，往羊皮纸上撒了吸墨的细沙。直到这时，直到此刻，他才起身面对伯爵夫人。

===Original===
“Well, why do ye not strike?” he asked her. “I wear no shirt of mail, and I have told ye how to stab. Art thou afraid?” Then, as she did not answer, nor move, he strode forward under her petrified gaze, and folded his arms. “Strike, Margaret of Belrémy.”

With a great effort she pulled herself together, setting her teeth. She lifted her dagger, her eyes riveted on his, but still she did not strike.

“Thy hand trembles,” Simon jibed. He stretched out his arm, and closed his fingers round her wrist. “Here,” he said, and brought her hand to his neck, so that the dagger pricked his tunic. “Push home, my lady.”

“Loose me!” she whispered. “Loose me!”

===Chinese===
“怎么，你为何还不动手？”他问她。“我未着锁子甲，怎么刺也教了你。你怕了么？”见她不答不动，他便在她僵立的注视下大步上前，抱起双臂。“动手啊，贝雷米（Belrémy）的玛格丽特。”

她竭力定住心神，咬紧牙关。她举起匕首，双眼死死钉住他的眼睛，却终究刺不下去。

“你的手在抖。”西蒙讥道。他伸出手臂，五指扣住她的腕子。“刺这里。”他说着，引她的手到自己颈边，刀尖顶上了他的衣襟。“刺进去，夫人。”

“放开我！”她低声说。“放开我！”

===Original===
Simon laughed, releasing her hand. Quickly she stepped back, stumbling over her train. The dagger tinkled to the ground.

“I﻿—I﻿—oh, one day I will do it!”

“Thou wilt never do it now, lady. The time is past, and thy courage forsook thee.”

“No!”

===Chinese===
西蒙放声大笑，松开了她的手。她急急后退，被裙裾绊了一下。匕首叮当一声落在地上。

“我——我——哦，总有一天我会做到的！”

“如今你再也做不成了，夫人。时机已过，你的胆子也弃你而去。”

“不！”

===Original===
“What then?”

“Oh, ye are a devil! a devil! How heard ye mine approach?”

Again he laughed.

“I heard ye not.”

===Chinese===
“那又为何？”

“哦，你是个魔鬼！魔鬼！你怎会听出我走近？”

他又是一阵大笑。

“我并没有听见。”

===Original===
She stared, hands clasped at her breast. Simon looked her over.

“Think ye I would sit alone and unguarded in this place had I not the sense that warns me of danger? I have tested thine honour before, madame, and I take no risks.”

She winced.

“Mine honour? What of thine own, Simon of Beauvallet? What honour hast thou who will threaten a woman?”

===Chinese===
她怔怔望着他，双手在胸前紧握。西蒙上下打量她。

“若非有一种直觉时时警我险厄，你以为我会独自不设防范地坐在这里？夫人，你的信义我早领教过，我不冒无谓的险。”

她像被针扎了一下。

“我的信义？你自己呢，博瓦莱的西蒙？一个以威胁女人为能事的人，还谈什么信义？”

===Original===
“No threat, madame. The scar on thy breast shows whether I lie or not.”

“I will pay ye for that, tenfold!” she cried. “Ye hold me captive, but ye shall see of what stuff Margaret of Belrémy is made! Dearly shall ye rue the day ye sought to pit your strength ’gainst mine!”

Simon stirred the dagger with his foot.

“The means lies there, madame. Take up that plaything and sate your vengeance.”

===Chinese===
“不是威胁，夫人。你胸口那道疤，自会证明我有没有撒谎。”

“这笔账我要十倍讨还！”她喊道。“你囚得住我的人，你且看贝雷米的玛格丽特是何等材料铸成！你胆敢与我一较短长的那一日，保管你悔断肚肠！”

西蒙用脚尖拨了拨那把匕首。

“手段就在那儿，夫人。拾起那件小玩意儿，解你的恨去。”

===Original===
“Nay, I will meet thee on equal terms, milor’! At the head of mine army!”

“Ay, I have heard that ye lead your men into battle. Ye were better occupied in your stitchery, madame.”

She laughed then, and came a step nearer to him.

“Were I so, my lord? Yet I did defeat Umfraville, and would have defeated you, had you not taken Belrémy by a trick!”

===Chinese===
“不！我要同你公平一战，英国老爷！亲率大军，与你相见！”

“是啊，听闻你确曾领兵上阵。夫人，你若去做针线，倒更称职些。”

这一下她反倒笑了，又朝他逼近一步。

“是么，大人？可我确确实实打败过乌姆弗拉维尔（Umfraville），若不是你用诡计袭取贝雷米，我也会打败你！”

===Original===
“It was thy wits against mine, madame, and my wits won the day.”

“A coward’s trick!”

“A ruse, madame, and one that beat you. I could have starved you into submission, but I chose the quickest road as always.”

She flung back her head.

===Chinese===
“那是你的智谋对上我的智谋，夫人，胜出的是我的。”

“懦夫的把戏！”

“是谋略，夫人，而且是胜过你一筹的谋略。我本可以围到你粮尽乞降，但我一如既往，拣了最快的路走。”

她昂起头。

===Original===
“Not yet have I submitted, Lord of Beauvallet!”

“Thou wilt submit.”

“Ye know me not! Ye may do what ye will with me, but ye will kill me before I bend to you!”

“We shall see, madame. There are many things I can do to you, but I think ye are not worth it.”

===Chinese===
“我还没有降，博瓦莱大人！”

“你会降的。”

“你根本不了解我！你要把我怎样都随你，可要我向你屈膝，除非先杀了我！”

“走着瞧吧，夫人。我能施加于你的手段多得很，可我看你还不值当我费那个手脚。”

===Original===
Colour flew into her cheeks.

“Thou insolent! Out of my way!” She caught up her train and would have gone up the stairway had not Alan blocked her path, coming slowly down. His arm still lay in a sling, but the bandage had been removed from his head. He wore his hair long to conceal the scar upon his temple.

“Your pardon, madame.” He came down into the hall, and bowed to her.

Her eyes rested on his wounded arm for a moment, and travelled from there to his forehead.

===Chinese===
血色腾地涌上她的双颊。

“你这无礼之徒！给我让路！”她提起裙裾便要上楼，艾伦却缓缓走下，挡住了去路。他的臂膊还吊在悬带里，头上的绷带已经解去，一头长发披落下来，遮住太阳穴上的疤。

“失礼了，夫人。”他走下大厅，向她一躬身。

她的目光在他受伤的臂膊上停了一停，又移向他额头。

===Original===
“My men strike hard, Sir Alan, is it not so? They leave their mark. A little deeper, and that scar that mars thy beauty would have dispatched thee!”

A swift tread sounded behind her. Simon’s hand descended on her shoulder, pulling her round to face him.

“By the Rood, madame, I am minded to have thine arrogance whipped out of thee! Get thee gone to thine apartment, and let me see you no more today!”

“Simon, Simon!” Alan remonstrated.

===Chinese===
“我的人出手真狠，是也不是，艾伦（Alan）爵士？他们留下了印记。当初再深一分，那道毁你好容貌的疤，就该送你上路了！”

身后响起一串急促的脚步。西蒙的手落在她肩头，把她拽得转过身来。

“圣架（the Rood）在上，夫人，我简直想叫人把你这一身傲气鞭打干净！回你房里去，今日休要再让我看见你！”

“西蒙，西蒙！”艾伦劝阻道。

===Original===
Margaret laughed at him.

“The gentle knight would protect me from the English boor’s wrath! I need no protection, Sir Alan! Had I that dagger now ye were dead a minute since, Lord of Beauvallet! Take thy hand from my shoulder! I go when I will, and how I will, I’ll have you know!”

“Ye go now,” Simon said grimly. “Away with you, or I call my men to carry you to your apartments!”

“Oh, you﻿—you﻿—!” Margaret struck him furiously, on his stern mouth. Then she broke free, and ran quickly up the winding stairway to her chamber.

===Chinese===
玛格丽特冲他嗤笑。

“温柔的骑士想护我周全，免受英格兰莽夫的雷霆之怒！我用不着人护，艾伦爵士！我这会儿手里要有那把匕首，博瓦莱大人，你一分钟前就已是个死人！把手从我肩上拿开！我何时走、怎么走，全凭己意，你给我记住了！”

“你现在就走。”西蒙冷冷道。“快去，不然我叫部下把你抬回房去！”

“哦，你这——你这——！”玛格丽特狠狠一巴掌掴在他紧抿的嘴上。随即挣脱身子，飞快奔上旋梯，逃回自己房中。

===Original===
Alan drew a deep breath, looking at Simon.

“The termagant! Simon, what will you do with her?”

“Conquer her,” Simon answered, and led him to a chair. “Sit, lad. The vixen, to taunt thee so!”

Alan smiled.

===Chinese===
艾伦深深吸了口气，望着西蒙。

“这悍妇！西蒙，你打算拿她怎么办？”

“降服她。”西蒙答道，引他到一张椅前。“坐吧，老弟。这泼辣货，竟敢这般嘲弄你！”

艾伦微微一笑。

===Original===
“I would not be alone with her for untold gold. Yesterday she braved Geoffrey so that he was trembling when he came to me, with anger and fear. He said she would have killed him had she a weapon to hand. She is like a tigress in her fierceness.”

“She hath never met her master﻿—until now. But I will school her.”

Alan looked at him through half-closed eyes. He said nothing, but his smile grew.

Upstairs, Margaret had cast herself into Jeanne’s arms in a fit of wild weeping.

===Chinese===
“纵有金山银海，我也不愿同她独处。昨日她顶撞杰弗里，等他来寻我时，又气又怕，浑身还在打颤。他说，她手边若有兵刃，早把他杀了。她发起凶来，活脱一头母老虎。”

“她从没遇上过能制住她的人——直到如今。不过我会好好调教她。”

艾伦眯起眼睛看他，一言未发，笑意却渐渐深了。

楼上，玛格丽特扑进让娜怀里，恸哭失声。

===Original===
“I could not slay him! I could not slay him! Oh, he is a devil, a devil! He knew that I was there, yet he heard me not! Oh, that I had had the strength to strike home. His fingers on my wrist﻿—ah, was ever a woman so beset?”

“I knew thou couldst not slay him,” Jeanne said calmly. “I saw thee creep down the stairway, but I feared not.”

Margaret sprang away.

“Wait! Wait! I will do it yet, I swear! I will escape﻿—I﻿—” She stopped. “Ah, no! Thou wilt tell Sir Geoffrey. I had forgot.”

===Chinese===
“我下不了手！我杀不了他！哦，他是个魔鬼，魔鬼！他知道我在那儿，却又没听见我的动静！唉，我若有那力气把刀刺个正着……他的手指扣住我的手腕——啊，天下几曾有女人这般受制于人？”

“我早知道你下不了手。”让娜平静地说。“我看见你蹑足下楼，可我并不担心。”

玛格丽特猛地挣开她。

“等着！等着！我总有一天做到，我发誓！我要逃出去——我——”她蓦地收声。“啊，不行！你会去告诉杰弗里爵士。我倒忘了。”

===Original===
“Oh, my dear, my dear!” Jeanne cried, and flung her arms about her. “Would I betray thee? Not for an hundred Sir Geoffreys.”

“He﻿—deems me a creature of no account!” Margaret said tensely. “He scorns me because I am a woman. I will show him what a woman can do!”

===Chinese===
“哦，亲爱的，亲爱的！”让娜嚷道，张开双臂把她搂住。“我会出卖你么？拿一百个杰弗里爵士来换也不换。”

“他——他压根不把我当一回事！”玛格丽特咬着牙说。“只因我是女人，他便轻贱我。我要让他看看，女人究竟能做出什么！”

## VII / 第七章

===Original===
How He Found Geoffrey and Jeanne on the Terrace

===Chinese===
他如何在露台上撞见杰弗里与让娜

===Original===
On the broad terrace Jeanne sat sewing, a fur cloak about her plump form, for although the sun was shining it was but a wintry sun, and the day was frosty. To her came Malvallet, bedight in crimson velvet and gold lacing. Mademoiselle looked up, surveying him.

“Oh, fie!” she murmured and turned her head to gaze pensively at a robin. “The soldier turned popinjay, i’ faith. He shames the sun.” She picked up her needle again.

“This is cruelty,” Geoffrey said mournfully, and sat down upon the parapet, facing her.

“Doubtless he will take a chill,” Mademoiselle sighed. “Such cold stone!” She sent a fleeting glance towards the damp parapet.

===Chinese===
宽阔的露台上，让娜（Jeanne）坐着做针线，丰腴的身子裹着一件毛皮斗篷——日头虽朗朗照着，也不过是隆冬的日头，这一天天寒霜重。马尔瓦莱（Malvallet）朝她走来，一身猩红天鹅绒，滚着金线绦带。小姐抬起头，把他上下打量了一番。

“哦，呸！”她低声咕哝，扭过头去，若有所思地盯住一只知更鸟。“大兵学人做花花公子，凭良心说，他把太阳都羞得没脸了。”她又拈起针来。

“这可真是狠心。”杰弗里哀声说，在护墙上坐下来，正对着她。

“他八成要着凉了。”小姐叹道。“多冷的石头！”她朝那片湿漉漉的护墙匆匆瞟了一眼。

===Original===
“I wonder, will she be sorry?” Geoffrey asked the sky.

“He dreams of his English love,” Mademoiselle nodded sagely.

“In truth, she is unkind today,” Geoffrey said. “She doth not look at me.”

“Oh, she hath no mind to be blinded!”

“Yet every time I do look into her eyes I am blinded and so bemused that I can see naught else forever after.”

“She must be very beautiful,” Mademoiselle said. “This English maid.”

===Chinese===
“不知道她会不会难过？”杰弗里仰面问苍天。

“他惦记着他那英格兰的心上人呢。”小姐煞有介事地点头。

“说真的，她今天真不够意思，”杰弗里说。“一眼也不瞧我。”

“哦，她可不愿意被晃瞎了眼！”

“可我每回望进她的眼睛，就被晃得神魂颠倒，从此一辈子再也看不见旁的东西。”

“那她一定美得不得了。”小姐说。“这位英格兰姑娘。”

===Original===
“Not English yet,” Geoffrey answered. “Please God I will make her so ere long.”

Mademoiselle bit her thread.

“The gentleman is courageous indeed,” she said, and bent again over her work.

For a time there was silence.

===Chinese===
“如今还不算英格兰人呢，”杰弗里答道。“托上帝的福，用不了多久我就会叫她变成英格兰人。”

小姐咬断了线头。

“这位先生端的是胆气过人。”她说着，又俯身做活。

一时间，两人都没了声息。

===Original===
“Jeanne,” Geoffrey said pleadingly.

Mademoiselle started.

“Oh, are ye here still?” she asked in innocent surprise.

Geoffrey came to her side and knelt. He stole one arm about her trim waist.

“Nay, Jeanne!”

“He will certainly be pricked,” Jeanne said, plying her needle faster still.

===Chinese===
“让娜。”杰弗里带着恳求唤道。

小姐吓了一跳。

“咦，你还在这里呀？”她一脸天真地惊问。

杰弗里走到她身旁跪了下来，一条手臂悄悄环上她窈窕的腰身。

“别这样，让娜！”

“他是一定会被扎着的。”让娜说，手里的针使得愈发快了。

===Original===
His right hand imprisoned hers.

“Sweet, thou shalt not torment me. Listen, and I will tell thee of my ladylove.”

Mademoiselle gazed blankly before her. A provocative smile lingered about her lips.

“I might call for help,” she mused.

“Nay, I need none,” Geoffrey answered promptly. “This lady, sweet, is little and lovely. So little that I might hide her in my pocket and forget that she was there.”

“This is English gallantry,” sighed Jeanne. “Poor lady!”

===Chinese===
他的右手把她的手牢牢扣住。

“亲爱的，你不许再折磨我了。听着，我把我的心上人讲给你听。”

小姐怔怔地望着眼前，唇边留着一抹撩人的笑。

“我大可以喊人救命。”她沉吟道。

“不必，我不需要人救。”杰弗里立刻答道。“这位夫人呀，亲爱的，生得又小又可爱。小得我都能把她揣进衣兜，过后就忘了她还在那儿。”

“这就是英格兰式的殷勤。”让娜叹道。“可怜的夫人！”

===Original===
“Not ‘poor,’ Jeanne, for she hath all a man’s heart.”

“Which is so little,” quoth she, “that she slipped it into her bag and forgot that it was there. Heyday!”

“But even though she forgot, being cruel, it still remained, braving her coldness and her tauntings, and waiting very humbly till she should grow kind.”

“A craven, cringing heart, wasting its life.”

“Nay, for although it was humble, it kept a close watch on the lady. And even though she scorned and flouted it, it made solemn oath unto itself that it would devote its whole life to guarding her welfare and her happiness.”

===Chinese===
“谈不上‘可怜’，让娜，因为她拥有一个男子的整颗心。”

“那颗心小得那样，”她说道，“叫她一滑手揣进了荷包，转眼便忘了它还在里头。嚯！”

“可她虽然狠心忘了它，它却仍旧守在那里，顶着她的冷淡与讥嘲，还恭恭敬敬地候着，等她哪一天心软下来。”

“一颗畏缩谄媚的心，白白糟蹋了自己的一辈子。”

“不，它虽然卑微，却把那位夫人盯得紧紧的。哪怕她轻慢它、奚落它，它也郑重对自己起誓，要把一生一世都用来守护她的安康与欢喜。”

===Original===
“Why, then, it was a busy heart, for doubtless it had sworn that oath many a time before.”

“Not so, Jeanne, for before it was asleep.”

“Oh, grammercy, was this its calf-love then?”

“All its love, lady. It knew none before it beheld the little lady with the big blue eyes and the pretty dimples. A French maid, Jeanne, with brown curls and a cruel tongue.”

“A spitfire, forsooth!”

===Chinese===
“哟，这么讲它可真是个忙碌的心，这样的誓它无疑早已发过许多回啦。”

“不是的，让娜，因为先前它一直睡着。”

“哦，多谢开导——这么说，这就是它那点儿孩童的痴恋了？”

“是它全部的爱，夫人。在遇见那位小夫人之前——就是那双又大又蓝的眼睛、那对可爱酒窝的小夫人——它不识爱为何物。一位法兰西姑娘，让娜，棕色的小鬈发，一条狠毒的舌头。”

“好一个火爆性子，真的！”

===Original===
“Just a wilful maid.”

“And French.” Jeanne nodded dreamily. “An enemy. Indeed, I am sorry for this heart.”

Geoffrey’s arm tightened about her.

“The heart is happy enough, Jeanne, but what of its owner! It left him to serve the lady, and now he hath none.”

“It was so little that he would scarce notice its absence,” Jeanne said.

“But he does indeed notice it, and though he would not have it return to him, he would fain have the lady’s heart in its place.”

===Chinese===
“不过是个任性娇纵的小姐罢了。”

“还是法兰西人。”让娜悠悠点头。“是个敌人。说实在的，我真替这颗心难过。”

杰弗里揽着她的手臂收紧了。

“那颗心自己倒快活，让娜，可它的主人又当如何！它撇下他去侍奉夫人，如今他一颗心也不剩了。”

“它那么小，他八成察觉不出自己少了它。”让娜说。

“可他偏偏觉察得一清二楚；他虽不愿那颗心回到自己身上，却巴不得拿夫人的心来补上那个空缺。”

===Original===
“Oh, it would freeze him, sir!”

“He might warm it, sweet.”

“Nay, for he is English, and the lady’s foe. And mayhap the lady’s heart has been given elsewhere.”

Geoffrey rose.

“Now I know why she is cold,” he said. “Her heart was gone already, so that she had none to give this Englishman. So he left her﻿—with his heart.”

===Chinese===
“哦，那颗心会把他冻僵的，先生！”

“他可以把它焐暖呀，亲爱的。”

“不成，他是英格兰人，又是夫人的仇敌。再说，夫人的心说不定早已许给了别人。”

杰弗里站起身。

“如今我可知道她为何这般冷了，”他说。“她的心早已不在了，自然没有心可以给这个英格兰人。于是他也离开了她——把自己的心留在了她那里。”

===Original===
Jeanne inspected her stitchery.

“Perhaps, after all﻿—it was still a virgin heart,” she said softly. “The﻿—the lady’s, I mean.”

Geoffrey came back.

“And might it be won, Jeanne?” he asked.

She bent lower still over her work, and the long lashes veiled her eyes.

“By an English foe, sir?”

===Chinese===
让娜端详起自己的针线活来。

“也许，说到底——那还是一颗不曾许人的心呢。”她柔声说。“我是说——那位夫人的心。”

杰弗里又折了回来。

“那颗心可还赢得来么，让娜？”他问。

她把腰俯得更低，凑向手里的活计，长睫毛垂下来遮住了眼睛。

“让一个英格兰仇敌来赢么，先生？”

===Original===
“By an English lover, Jeanne.”

She poised her needle, looking at it intently.

“Nay. It could not be won.”

“Never?”

“Never. You see, sir, it was a cold, cruel heart, and it repulsed all its suitors. And﻿—and it was a shy heart﻿—but true. So﻿—so one day﻿—it left the lady﻿—very secretly, so that at first she did not know that it had gone, and﻿—and slipped into a man’s pocket. And﻿—when the lady﻿—tried to recall it﻿—it would not come, but nestled down in its hiding-place. But﻿—but it was such a timid little heart, that the man﻿—he was a great, stupid Englishman﻿—never knew that it was in his pocket, but besought the lady to give it to him. He was so blinded, you see﻿—and just an English conqueror.”

===Chinese===
“让一个英格兰情人来赢，让娜。”

她捏着针停在半空，目不转睛地端详。

“不成。它赢不来的。”

“永远不成？”

“永远不成。您瞧，先生，那是一颗又冷又狠的心，所有向它求婚的人一概被它推了回去。而且——而且它是颗害羞的心——可是一片真心。于是——于是有一天——它离开了那位夫人——走得非常隐秘，起初她竟不知道它已经走了，便——便溜进了一个男人的口袋。后来——夫人想——想把心唤回来——它偏不肯回来，只在那藏身之处安安稳稳地伏下了。可是——可是它是那样一个怯生生的小东西，那个男人——那是个又高又蠢的英格兰人——压根儿不知道它在自己口袋里，反倒一个劲儿哀求夫人把它给他。他呀，您瞧，早叫她晃花了眼——不过是个英格兰征服者罢了。”

===Original===
“An English slave,” Geoffrey said, and knelt again, his arms about her. “A suppliant at the little lady’s feet.”

“But he was very strong and masterful withal,” Jeanne murmured, and let her stitchery fall. “And﻿—and clad in crimson velvet which he knew became him well. A conceited popinjay, sir.”

Geoffrey drew her to rest against his shoulder.

“Nay, for he doffed his work-a-day clothes and donned the crimson velvet only to do his lady honour.”

“A peacock preening himself to dazzle the hen,” Jeanne replied, and smoothed her russet gown.

===Chinese===
“一个英格兰的奴隶。”杰弗里说着重新跪下，双臂环住她。“拜倒在小夫人脚下的祈求者。”

“可他到底又壮实又霸道。”让娜低声道，任针线活从膝头滑落。“而且——而且穿着一身猩红天鹅绒，他自己也知道那颜色衬他。一个自负的花花公子，先生。”

杰弗里把她拉过来，让她靠在自己肩头。

“不是的，他脱下平日做工的衣裳，换上那身猩红天鹅绒，只为向他心上人致敬添彩。”

“一只孔雀，精心梳翎，好晃花母鸡的眼。”让娜答道，一面抚平身上那件赤褐色的长裙。

===Original===
“She was such a pretty hen that he decked himself in velvet so as not to show himself a drab fellow beside her loveliness.”

“Oh, I do not think he was ever drab,” Jeanne said into his ear. “In his steel armour with the black plumes in his helm, and the black surcoat floating from his shoulders, and his great sword in hand﻿—he﻿—he was a fine figure.”

“When saw ye me thus, Jeanne?”

“From the tower window, sir. And I hated you. You and your leader, the icy Lord of Beauvallet.”

“And Alan?”

“Alan? Oh﻿—well, he was my lady’s prisoner﻿—and one does not hate a helpless man. And﻿—and indeed he makes pretty love to a maid.” Unseen, she smiled.

===Chinese===
“那只母鸡生得太标致了，他这才披上天鹅绒，免得站在她的美貌旁边，显得自己是个灰扑扑的家伙。”

“哦，我倒不觉得他哪天灰扑扑过。”让娜凑在他耳边说。“钢甲穿在身上，盔上插着黑羽，黑色的战袍自肩头飘拂，手里提着那柄大剑——他——他真是好一副英挺模样。”

“你几时见过我这般模样，让娜？”

“从塔楼的窗子里，先生。我当时恨透了你。恨你，也恨你们那位首领，冷若冰霜的博瓦莱大人。”

“那艾伦呢？”

“艾伦？哦——这个嘛，他是我家夫人的俘虏——谁也不会去恨一个无力还手的人。况且——况且他向姑娘大献殷勤的时候，的确甜得很。”她暗自莞尔，不叫人瞧见。

===Original===
“Doth he so?” Geoffrey turned her face up, a hand beneath her chin. “I will speak with Master Alan. Is his lovemaking so pretty as mine?” He kissed her red lips.

“Prettier by far,” Jeanne retorted, when she could. “For he did not squeeze me brutally, nor take advantage of my loneliness.”

“Why he is but half a man, then,” Geoffrey answered, and kissed her again.

Her bosom rose and fell quickly; she returned his kisses for a while, then struggled to be free of him, her neck and cheeks a rosy red.

“Oh, but we are traitors, both!” she cried, and set her hands on his breast, thrusting him away.

===Chinese===
“他么？”杰弗里抬起她的脸，一只手托在她颏下。“我倒要去会会艾伦老爷。他调情的本事当真赶得上我？”他吻住她的红唇。

“好上许多呢。”让娜缓过气来，反唇相讥。“他既不曾野蛮地乱搂我，也不趁我孤身一人占便宜。”

“嘿，那他岂不算是半条汉子。”杰弗里答道，又吻了她。

她胸脯急促起伏，回了他一阵吻，随即挣着要脱身，脖颈和双颊一片绯红。

“哎呀，我们两个都成了叛徒啦！”她喊道，两手抵住他胸口，把他推开。

===Original===
“Traitors, sweet? Why?”

“Thou to the Lord Beauvallet, I to the Lady Margaret! While these two stay at enmity I must cleave to the one, and thou to the other.”

“The Lady Margaret will make her submission,” Geoffrey said.

“Ah, you do not know her! She hath never bent the knee yet. I have been with her since childhood, and﻿—and I know how strong is her will.”

“Fifteen years have I known Simon,” Geoffrey answered, “and I have yet to see him beaten.”

===Chinese===
“叛徒，亲爱的？这话从何说起？”

“你叛了博瓦莱大人，我叛了玛格丽特夫人！这两位一日不罢兵，我就得依附这一边，你就得依附那一边。”

“玛格丽特夫人终归会俯首称臣的。”杰弗里说。

“唉，你不认得她！她还从未向人屈过膝。我打小就跟着她，而且——而且我知道她的意志有多刚强。”

“我认得西蒙十五年了，”杰弗里答道，“却还没见谁赢过他。”

===Original===
“But now he is pitted ’gainst a woman, and therefore is defenceless, for what weapons can he use? I tell thee, Geoffrey, ever since my lady’s father died, she hath ruled supreme. She will never bend, least of all before an Englishman.”

“In truth, the Lady Margaret is an Amazon,” Geoffrey said ruefully. “I mislike these tigress-women.”

“That is not true!” Jeanne cried hotly. “She is the sweetest, dearest lady! She shows herself tigerish to you, because you seek to conquer her!”

“Not I!” Geoffrey grimaced. “I do not willingly cross her path.”

“She is brave and proud! But to her own people she is, oh, so kind and just!”

===Chinese===
“可这一回他对上的是个女人，便全无防备可言了，他能使的兵器又是什么呢？我告诉你，杰弗里，自打我家夫人的父亲过世，这里便是她一人当家做主。她绝不会低头，尤其是在英格兰人面前，万万不能。”

“说真的，玛格丽特夫人是位亚马逊。”杰弗里苦笑道。“这些母老虎似的女人，我消受不起。”

“才不是这样！”让娜急声道。“她是最温柔、最亲爱不过的夫人！她对你露出虎相，是因为你一心要征服她！”

“我可没有！”杰弗里扮了个鬼脸。“但凡能不撞见她，我绝不存心招惹。”

“她又勇敢又骄傲！可对她自己治下的子民，她是——哦——那样仁慈，那样公正！”

===Original===
“Beshrew me, I am glad that I am not of her people.”

“Sir,” said Jeanne coldly, “loose me!”

Geoffrey kissed her averted cheek.

“Nay, I meant not to anger thee, my dear. The Lady Margaret is what you will. I care not. No woman is aught to me save one.”

Jeanne pushed him away.

“Geoffrey, loose me! Here comes thy lord! Oh, rise, thou great stupid!”

===Chinese===
“谢天谢地，幸而我不是她的子民。”

“先生，”让娜冷冷道，“放开我！”

杰弗里在她偏开的脸颊上吻了一下。

“别恼，我不是有心惹你生气，我的宝贝。玛格丽特夫人随你怎么说都行，我不在乎。天下的女人于我一概无干，只有一个例外。”

让娜把他推开。

“杰弗里，放开我！你的主上过来了！哎呀，快起来呀，你这大笨蛋！”

===Original===
Along the terrace Simon was coming, capless, and Jeanne glanced from his face to Geoffrey’s.

“In truth ye are much alike,” she said. “But the one is ‘*beau*’ and the other is ‘*mal*.’ ”

“We are half-brothers,” Geoffrey told her. He turned to greet Simon. “Hast need of me, lad?”

Simon bowed awkwardly to Mademoiselle.

“Nay. I thought Alan was here. I ask your pardon for my intrusion.”

In the depths of his strange eyes Jeanne saw a twinkle. She blushed, sewing quicker than ever.

===Chinese===
西蒙正沿露台走来，光着头未戴帽，让娜的目光从他的脸移到杰弗里的脸上。

“说真的，你们俩生得真像，”她说。“只是一个是‘*beau*’（俊美），另一个是‘*mal*’（恶劣）呀。”

“我们是同父异母的兄弟。”杰弗里告诉她，随即转身去迎西蒙。“有事要找我么，老弟？”

西蒙生硬地向小姐躬了躬身。

“没有。我只当艾伦在这里。我贸然闯来，还望恕罪。”

让娜瞧见他那双异样的眼睛深处闪过一丝笑意。她红了脸，手里的针线飞得比先前任何时候都快。

===Original===
“I have seen not Alan. What’s amiss?”

“He bears the title, Master of the Horse,” Simon said with heavy sarcasm. “I would have him attend to his affairs.”

Jeanne spoke demurely.

“Methinks Sir Alan is in the western hall, milor’.”

Geoffrey chuckled, for the Lady Margaret’s ladies often sat there.

“Who is the charmer, my Jeanne?” he asked.

===Chinese===
“我没瞧见艾伦呀。出了什么事？”

“他顶着骁骑长这么个头衔，”西蒙话说得讥诮入骨。“我倒要他料理料理自己的差事。”

让娜斯斯文文地开了口。

“依我看，艾伦爵士这会儿在西厅呢，英国老爷。”

杰弗里低低笑了，玛格丽特夫人的女官们常在那儿坐。

“是哪位可人儿呀，我的让娜？”他问。

===Original===
A frown reproved him.

“I believe it is Mademoiselle Yvonne de Vertimaine,” Jeanne answered.

“Wilt fetch him for me, Geoffrey?” Simon said. “Ye will find me here.”

Geoffrey smiled.

“Simon, do ye fear to enter the ladies’ bower?”

“I would not rob you of that sweet delight,” Simon answered. “Go, Geoffrey. I will bear Mademoiselle company.”

===Chinese===
她蹙起眉头，权作申饬。

“我记得是伊冯娜·德·维尔蒂曼（Yvonne de Vertimaine）小姐。”让娜答道。

“你替我把他叫来可好，杰弗里？”西蒙说。“我就在此地等你。”

杰弗里笑了。

“西蒙，你怕踏进女眷的闺阁不成？”

“那样的乐事，我可不能夺你所好。”西蒙答道。“去吧，杰弗里。我陪小姐坐一坐。”

===Original===
“Thank you!” Geoffrey bowed ironically, and sauntered away down the terrace.

Jeanne found her heart beating rather fast. She had been present when Simon had captured her mistress, and she had accompanied Margaret to the English camp. Both of these experiences left her very nervous of Simon. Now he sat down upon the parapet, looking at her.

“So ye have captured my captain’s heart, mademoiselle,” he said slowly.

Jeanne looked at him. He was smiling down at her pleasantly, and she plucked up her courage.

“No, sir. He gave it to me.”

===Chinese===
“多谢美意！”杰弗里煞有介事地一揖，信步沿露台走远了。

让娜觉出自己的心跳得有些快。西蒙擒住她家女主人的时候她在场，玛格丽特前往英格兰营中她也一路随行。这两桩经历都叫她对西蒙怕得不行。此刻他在护墙上坐了下来，望着她。

“这么说，我这位统领的心教你给俘虏啦，小姐。”他慢悠悠地说。

让娜望望他。他正和颜悦色地含笑俯视着她，她便鼓起了勇气。

“不是的，先生。是他自己给了我。”

===Original===
“It is all one. I take it you and he will walk to the altar soon?”

Jeanne shook her head.

“It cannot be, milor’.”

“Ah?”

“I serve the Countess.”

===Chinese===
“那都一样。我猜你们两个不日便要携手上圣坛了吧？”

让娜摇头。

“这断不能够，英国老爷。”

“哦？”

“我是侍奉伯爵夫人的。”

===Original===
“I see,” said Simon. “Yet when I have quelled this turbulent lady, what then?”

“Ye will not do it, milor’,” she said confidently.

“Shall I not? I might ask thine aid.”

She paused in her stitching, and looked him steadily between the eyes.

“Ye would be ill-advised, sir.”

“Oh?” He raised his brows. “Like mistress like maid, is it?”

===Chinese===
“原来如此，”西蒙说。“可待我平服了这位桀骜的夫人，那时又怎样呢？”

“您平服不了的，大人。”她信心十足地说。

“我平不了？那我倒要请你帮把手了。”

她停了停针线，目光直直地望进他两眼之间。

“那样做是失策，先生。”

“哦？”他挑起眉毛。“有其主必有其仆，是也不是？”

===Original===
“Ay, sir.”

“Not all Malvallet’s pleadings will make thee change thy mind?”

“Sir Geoffrey, milor’, would be the last to have me turn traitor.”

“I but ask thy persuasion, lady.”

“You ask in vain, sir.”

“So? Then let me tell thee, mademoiselle, that if the Lady Margaret cannot be persuaded, she may yet be coerced.”

===Chinese===
“正是，先生。”

“任凭马尔瓦莱怎样哀恳，也劝不动你回心转意么？”

“杰弗里爵士最不愿见我做叛徒了，英国老爷。”

“我只请你帮我劝一劝，夫人。”

“您这是白求，先生。”

“是么？那么容我告诉你，小姐：玛格丽特夫人若劝说不服，尚可以强制行事。”

===Original===
“Oh, brave!” Jeanne exclaimed scornfully.

“It is in my power,” Simon said imperturbably, “to execute the Countess. Hast thou thought of that, I wonder?”

“Ye would have all Belrémy about your ears, like hornets,” she answered.

“It would not worry me. If I have not the Lady Margaret’s submission soon, I shall be forced to take stronger measures. Let her take heed, for I mean what I say.”

“I doubt it not.” Jeanne eyed him for a moment. “Yet would ye not slay the Countess, for ye are English, and I have heard that their justice is great.”

===Chinese===
“哦，好威风啊！”让娜讥诮地扬声道。

“处死伯爵夫人，”西蒙神色不动地说，“这权柄正操在我手里。不知你可曾想过这一层？”

“那样全贝雷米的人都会像马蜂一般，铺天盖地扑上您的头顶。”她答道。

“那倒难不倒我。玛格丽特夫人若不早早归降，我便只好采取更严厉的手段。叫她好生当心，我说话是算数的。”

“这个我不疑心。”让娜打量了他片刻。“只是您总不至于杀伯爵夫人——您是英格兰人，听说英格兰人的公道是天下闻名的。”

===Original===
“As ye shall see,” Simon answered grimly.

“And﻿—and do ye war on women?” Jeanne asked.

“Ay, if need be.”

“It is very sad,” she sighed.

===Chinese===
“您日后自会见识。”西蒙冷冷答道。

“那——那您也同妇道人家开战么？”让娜问。

“是，若有必要。”

“真是可悲啊。”她叹道。

## VIII / 第八章

===Original===
How the Lady Margaret Plotted

The Lady Margaret sat with some of her ladies in her audience-chamber. A dark-eyed page was at her feet, playing on a small harp, and Jeanne sat beside her. Margaret lay back at her ease, a splendid figure against the fur-skin that covered her chair. At the far end of the room some gentlemen stood, conversing together; the Chevalier leaned over the back of his cousin’s chair, whispering occasionally in her ear. She paid little heed to his sallies, but now and then jerked her shoulder impatiently, and frowned.

“Art cold today, sweet cousin,” the Chevalier whispered.

“I have not changed, Victor,” she answered curtly. “You weary me.”

===Chinese===
玛格丽特夫人如何定计

玛格丽特夫人带着几名女官坐在觐见厅里。一个黑眼睛的小侍童坐在她脚边弹着小竖琴，让娜（Jeanne）坐在她身旁。玛格丽特闲适地向后倚着，衬着覆椅的毛皮，端的是一段华贵的身姿。屋子另一头立着几位绅士，正在交谈；弗勒里瓦尔骑士（the Chevalier de Fleurival）俯在表姐的椅背后，不时凑到她耳边低语。他打趣的话她全不在意，只是不时烦躁地一耸肩，蹙起眉头。

“今天觉得冷么，亲爱的表姐。”骑士低声说。

“我可没变，维克托（Victor）。”她没好气地答道。“你让我厌烦。”

===Original===
“But one day, fairest, you will change? Shall I never find the way to thy heart?”

“At a distance I might like you better,” she said.

“Cruel, cruel! Ah, Margot, if ye would but smile upon me, what might not we do to oust this English boor?”

Her lip curled.

“I need no help from you, Victor.”

===Chinese===
“可是总有一天，最美的人儿，你会变的吧？我就永远找不到通向你心里的路么？”

“隔着远些，我说不定还喜欢你些。”她说。

“狠心，真狠心！唉，玛戈（Margot），你只要肯对我展颜一笑，咱们还愁赶不走这英格兰粗坯么？”

她的上唇鄙夷地一撇。

“我用不着你帮忙，维克托。”

===Original===
His voice sank lower.

“No, *ma belle*? Yet thou didst not slay him when I gave thee the chance.”

She flushed, tapping her foot on the floor.

“I told you that I would not.”

“And thou didst not essay it?” he purred. “How then came my dagger upon the floor in the great hall?”

===Chinese===
他的声音压得更低。

“不是么，我的美人？可我把机会给了你，你并没有下手杀他。”

她红了脸，拿脚尖笃笃地点着地板。

“我说过我不杀他。”

“你也不曾试一试？”他柔声哄道。“那我的匕首怎么会躺在大厅的地上？”

===Original===
“Oh, go, go!” she said quickly. “I would not kill him, because﻿—because﻿—I will﻿—find a surer way.”

He drew himself upright, still smiling.

“Is it indeed so, Margot? Now I had thought.﻿ ﻿… Ah, well!” Sighing, he strolled out, and the Countess gave a little shiver.

Slowly the colour died from her cheeks. She turned to her page, laying a caressing hand on his shoulder.

“Thy song is joyous today, Léon.”

===Chinese===
“哦，去，去！”她忙说。“我不杀他，是因为——是因为——我要——找一个更有把握的法子。”

他直起身子，笑意未消。

“果真如此么，玛戈？我原还当……唉，罢了！”他叹了口气，踱出门去，伯爵夫人浑身微微一颤。

她颊上的血色慢慢褪尽。她转向小侍童，一只手爱抚地搭上他的肩头。

“莱昂（Léon），你今天的曲子弹得真欢快。”

===Original===
He looked up at her, eyes a-sparkle.

“Yes, madame. I am gay because the English lord hath granted me a pass out of the castle. I go to see my father, without the town.”

The long fingers on his shoulder gripped suddenly. Surprised, he looked up again, into the beautiful face bent over him, and saw it pale, lips slightly parted, and eyes shining.

“Is﻿—no pass needed to leave the town?” Margaret asked softly.

“Nay, madame, for the town hath submitted.”

===Chinese===
他抬头望她，双眼亮晶晶的。

“是，夫人。我高兴，是因为英格兰大人恩准了我一张出城堡的通行证。我要去城外看我父亲。”

搭在他肩上的那双纤长的手指骤然收紧。他吃了一惊，重新抬头，望进俯向自己的那张美丽的脸——脸色发白，双唇微启，眼睛亮亮的。

“出城——出城不要通行证么？”玛格丽特轻声问。

“不要了，夫人，全城已经归降。”

===Original===
He heard the quick intake of her breath, and wondered.

“Léon, when wilt thou go?”

“Tomorrow, madame, if it please you.”

“And﻿—and where is thy﻿—pass?”

He patted his tunic.

===Chinese===
他听见她倏地倒吸一口气，心中纳罕。

“莱昂，你什么时候动身？”

“明天，夫人，只要您愿意。”

“那——那你的——通行证呢？”

他拍了拍身上的短衣。

===Original===
“Safe here, madame. My lord signed it today.”

“Léon﻿—” Margaret spoke in a whisper﻿—“Thou dost love me, is it not so?”

“But yes, madame! I would die﻿—”

“Then come to my room presently﻿—with﻿—with thy pass. And say naught, Léon! Say naught!”

“Yes, madame,” he answered obediently, but his eyes searched her face in mystification.

===Chinese===
“好好儿收在这儿呢，夫人。大人今天刚签的字。”

“莱昂——”玛格丽特耳语般地说——“你是爱我的，对不对？”

“那还用说，夫人！我情愿死——”

“那你一会儿就到我房里来——带着——带着你的通行证。什么也别说，莱昂！什么也别说！”

“是，夫人。”他乖乖应道，眼睛却困惑不解地搜索着她的脸色。

===Original===
She leaned back, and in a moment had called one of the courtiers to her side, laughing gaily, and chattering with him, so that Jeanne glanced at her shrewdly more than once. Presently she rose, brushing her hand across her eyes.

“Ah, now I am tired, and have the migraine! Come with me, Jeanne.” She went out slowly, leaning on Jeanne’s arm. Never a word spake Mademoiselle until the door of my lady’s chamber was closed behind them. Then she turned to Margaret, taking her hand.

“Margot, what dost thou purpose?” she asked anxiously.

Tense fingers clutched at her wrists.

“Jeanne, you swear﻿—you swear to stand my friend?”

===Chinese===
她向后靠去，不多时便唤了一名廷臣到身边，笑着同他喋喋不休，惹得让娜敏锐地朝她望了不止一眼。过了一会儿，她起身，抬手在眼前抹了一下。

“啊，这会儿我累了，头也疼起来了！陪我来，让娜。”她挽着让娜的胳膊，缓缓走出去。直到夫人的房门在两人身后阖上，让娜小姐始终一声不吭。这时她才转向玛格丽特，握住她的手。

“玛戈，你打的什么主意？”她忧心忡忡地问。

绷得紧紧的手指攥住了她的手腕。

“让娜，你发誓——你发誓站在我这一边？”

===Original===
“But, *chérie*! Can you ask?”

“This Geoffrey﻿—” Jealous, suspicious eyes glared into hers﻿—“you would not betray me to him? You would not?”

“Never! Margot, what ails thee? Tell me, please! What said you to Léon?”

“Jeanne﻿—I﻿—I *trust* thee!”

“And so thou mayst.”

===Chinese===
“可是，亲爱的！这还用问么？”

“那个杰弗里——”一双妒忌而多疑的眼睛逼视着她的眼睛——“你不会把我出卖给他吧？你不会吧？”

“决不会！玛戈，你怎么了？求你告诉我！你对莱昂说了什么？”

“让娜——我——我信得过你！”

“你自然信得过。”

===Original===
“Then listen!” Margaret dragged her to a seat. “Léon hath a pass! To go from the castle tomorrow. You see? Tell me now, am I not a little like him?” With a quick movement she was at her looking-glass, gazing close upon herself. “Black eyes, the nose﻿—well, no. Mine is more straight. Lips? Too haughty, Margot dear. No matter. Let us essay a glad smile. Ay, it will suffice. Enough for this Simon. A cap pulled low over my brow. Height?” She drew herself up. “I will measure me ’gainst Léon.” She swept about, clasping her hands, eyes a-brim with triumphant laughter. “Jeanne, shall I not make a pretty page?”

Jeanne started up.

“Margot, what wouldst thou be at?”

“I would go to Fernand de Turincel. Nay, but listen! A pageboy excites no suspicion. Ten leagues. I might find a horse. It shall be given out here that I am sick abed. Even an I walk to Turincel I can reach it within three days. Yes, yes, I can! Oh, Jeanne, shake not thy head!”

“*Chérie*, thou art distraught! Bethink ye, it is all too perilous an emprise for a maid. I could not let thee try it. Ah, *mignonne, mignonne*, I could not!”

===Chinese===
“那就听着！”玛格丽特把她拽到座位上。“莱昂有一张通行证！明天就能出城堡。你明白了么？快说，我是不是有几分像他？”她身子一转，已经到了镜子跟前，贴得近近地端详自己。“黑眼睛；鼻子嘛——嗯，不像，我的更直。嘴唇？太倨傲了，亲爱的玛戈。不要紧，来试一个欢快的笑。喏，成了，尽够了。糊弄这个西蒙足足有余。帽子压低，罩住眉毛。个头呢？”她挺直身子。“我来同莱昂比一比。”她旋过身，双手交握，满眼盛着得胜的笑。“让娜，我扮起小侍童来，是不是挺俊？”

让娜霍地站起来。

“玛戈，你想干什么？”

“我要去投费尔南·德·图兰塞尔（Fernand de Turincel）。别急，听我说！一个小侍童不会惹人起疑。十里格路。我可以寻一匹马。这里只管放风出去，说我卧病在床。就算一路走着去图兰塞尔，三天之内我也赶得到。行，行，准能行！哦，让娜，别摇头呀！”

“亲爱的，你昏了头啦！想想吧，这营生对一个姑娘家来说实在太凶险，我不能由着你去试。啊，小乖乖，小乖乖，我不能啊！”

===Original===
“Thou shalt come with me then! As﻿—as﻿—my sister! Smile, Jeannette! It means escape, and help!”

“But the danger﻿—”

“Pho! Have I not my dagger? If thou art afraid, I’ll not take thee, but go alone. Thou hast sworn to stand my friend.”

“Margot, thou canst not do it!” Jeanne cried. “Would you don boy’s raiment? Margot!”

“That would I!” laughed the Countess, and drew back her skirts to show her tapering foot. Smiling she regarded first it, and then her lady. “Too small, you think? But long, Jeanne. And﻿—and a shapely leg.”

===Chinese===
“那你陪我一道去！当——当——当我姐姐！笑一笑，小让娜（Jeannette）！这一来既能脱身，又能搬救兵！”

“可是这险——”

“呸！我不是带着匕首么？你若害怕，我便不带你，自己去。你发过誓要站在我这一边的。”

“玛戈，你不能这样干！”让娜嚷道。“你要穿上男孩子的衣裳？玛戈！”

“偏要穿！”伯爵夫人大笑，撩起裙裾，露出一只纤巧的脚。她笑吟吟地先看看那只脚，又看看她的女伴。“你嫌太小？可是够长的，让娜。还有——还有一条匀称的小腿呢。”

===Original===
“Margot!” almost wailed Jeanne. “Thou﻿—thou art mad!”

“I was never more sane!﻿—There is Léon! Open, child!”

Jeanne crept to the door, and admitted the handsome page.

“Ah, the good Léon!” Margaret gave him her hand to kiss. “Léon, thou wilt help me?”

“Yes, madame, of course. But I do not understand﻿—”

===Chinese===
“玛戈！”让娜几乎哀号起来。“你——你是疯了！”

“我从来没这么清醒过！——莱昂来了！开门，孩子！”

让娜蹑手蹑脚挪到门边，把俊俏的小侍童放了进来。

“啊，好莱昂！”玛格丽特伸出手去让他吻。“莱昂，你肯帮我么？”

“肯，夫人，当然肯。只是我不明白——”

===Original===
“Am I not about to tell thee? Léon, swear not to divulge what I shall say to any living soul! Not even my cousin. Swear!”

“I swear, madame.”

“Thou sweet boy! I want thy pass. Quick, let me see it!”

He gave it to her, staring. The Countess spread it out.

“The secretary writes plain,” she remarked. “ ‘Léon de Margrute.﻿ ﻿… This by mine order, Simon Beauvallet.’ *Dieu*, what a flourish! Léon, I want this pass! I escape from the castle tomorrow. Thou art in my plot now!”

===Chinese===
“我这不正要告诉你么？莱昂，起个誓，我接下来说的话，决不对任何活人吐露半个字！连我表弟也不例外。发誓！”

“我发誓，夫人。”

“乖孩子！我要你的通行证。快，拿来我瞧瞧！”

他怔怔地递了过去。伯爵夫人把文书展开。

“秘书的字写得真清楚。”她品评道。“‘马尔格吕特的莱昂（Léon de Margrute）……奉我之命放行，西蒙·博瓦莱。’天哪，这一笔花押好不威风！莱昂，这张通行证我要了！我明天就逃出这座城堡。如今你也入了我的局啦！”

===Original===
“But, madame, you cannot﻿—”

“And a suit of thine apparel. Hose, tunic﻿—Oh, I’ll spare thy blushes, Jeannette! Bring me them secretly, Léon, tonight. Ah, Léon, thou wilt do it? I *ask* thy help!”

He bowed.

“Madame, I must obey. But indeed, indeed﻿—”

She covered her ears.

===Chinese===
“可是，夫人，您不能——”

“还有你的一身衣裳。长袜、短衣——哦，我不说下去了，免得小让娜脸红！今晚悄悄给我送来，莱昂。啊，莱昂，你肯办么？我求你帮忙！”

他躬了躬身。

“夫人，我不能不从。只是实在，实在——”

她捂住了耳朵。

===Original===
“I will not listen! Keep close tomorrow, my Léon, so that they shall not wonder at thy presence here. And﻿—and see ye choose me a plain, dark dress, with a cap to set on my head. Go now and fetch it, dear boy! I’ll reward thee for thy pains. Oh, and thou shalt have another pass when I return! No need of it then, perhaps.”

The astonished page retreated. Jeanne sank down on to a chair.

“Margot,” she began weakly, and stopped. “Oh, Margot!”

The Countess picked up a quill and dipped it in the ink.

“See, Jeanne, there is room to add ‘and sister.’ Think you I can copy this fist? Give me parchment!”

===Chinese===
“我不听！明天你只管躲着些，免得人家奇怪你怎么老往这儿跑。还有——还有，替我挑一身素净的深色衣裳，再配一顶戴在头上的帽子。这就去取来，乖孩子！你的辛苦我自有赏。哦，等我回来，再赏你一张新通行证！那时多半也用不着了。”

惊愕的小侍童退了出去。让娜瘫坐进一张椅子里。

“玛戈，”她有气无力地开了口，又停住。“哦，玛戈！”

伯爵夫人拈起一支鹅毛笔，蘸了蘸墨水。

“瞧，让娜，这里还添得下‘及姐姐’几个字。你看我仿得来这笔迹么？把羊皮纸给我！”

===Original===
Jeanne brought it, and watched her mistress practise writing on it. At length Margaret wrote upon the pass, and sat back surveying her handiwork.

“ ’Tis marvellous. Let it dry, Jeanne, my sister. Aha, Simon of Beauvallet, how now?”

“We are not yet escaped,” Jeanne said drily.

“But we shall escape, very early. Look out thine oldest dress, *petite*, and wear a hood and cloak. Oh, I should have written ‘brother,’ and we could have been boys together.”

“Heaven forbid!” Jeanne shuddered.

===Chinese===
让娜取来羊皮纸，看着女主人先在上面临摹练字。末了，玛格丽特才往通行证上落笔，随即往椅背一靠，端详起自己的手笔。

“妙极了。晾干它吧，让娜，我的好姐姐。啊哈，博瓦莱的西蒙，这一回你待怎样？”

“咱们还没有逃出去呢。”让娜淡淡地说。

“可我们准能逃出去，天一亮便动身。把你最旧的一身衣裳找出来，小家伙，戴上风帽，披上斗篷。哦，早知添上‘兄弟’二字就好了，咱们俩还能一块儿做男孩子。”

“老天爷饶了我吧！”让娜打了个哆嗦。

===Original===
The black eyes sparkled.

“Conceive Malvallet’s face of horror! Oh, la, la! In truth, thou art too small for the part, and all a woman. Now I”﻿—she glanced down herself﻿—“I am a thin creature﻿—well, thin enough, and tall. I shall make a comely lad.﻿ ﻿… Enter, Léon! Enter!”

Back into the room came the page. Blushing, he laid a neat bundle on the table.

“I﻿—think﻿—I have forgot naught,” he stammered.

“Thou dear boy!” Margaret kissed him on both cheeks. “There! Keep my secret well, Léon, and thank you, thank you, thank you!”

===Chinese===
那双黑眼睛亮闪闪的。

“你且想想马尔瓦莱那张吓坏了的脸！哎呀呀！说真的，你这副身量扮不来这角色，又满身女儿态。我呢——”她低头打量自己——“我是瘦削身形——嗯，够瘦，个子也高。扮个俊秀少年，保管像样。……进来，莱昂！进来！”

小侍童回到房里，红着脸，把一个齐齐整整的包袱放在桌上。

“我——想是——没落下什么。”他结结巴巴地说。

“好孩子！”玛格丽特在他两边脸颊上各亲了一口。“好了！把我的秘密守严实些，莱昂。谢谢，谢谢，谢谢你！”

===Original===
No sooner had he left the room than she untied the bundle, holding up each garment in turn.

“Oh, the brave hose! See, Jeanne!﻿ ﻿… A cap﻿—the tunic, the﻿—oh, the trunks!” She went off into a peal of laughter, and let them fall. “Go away, Jeannette, into my closet! And﻿—and come when I call!”

Jeanne crawled away into the outer chamber. There followed a long pause, punctuated by gurgles of merriment from within my lady’s chamber. At last Margaret called to her, and she went back into the room.

Before the looking-glass stood a slim stripling in a short brown tunic, a dagger in his belt, and a cap crammed down over his eyes. Long shapely legs were cased in brown hose, and set well apart. Margaret swaggered forward.

“Am I not brave? Sister, I salute thee! These clothes make me smaller, but ’tis no matter. Jeanne, Jeanne, look not so horrified!”

===Chinese===
他前脚出屋，她后脚便解开包袱，把一件件衣裳轮着拎起来端详。

“哦，好神气的长袜！瞧呀，让娜！……一顶帽子——短衣，还有——哦，这短裤！”她迸出一串大笑，衣裳撒手落地。“去去，小让娜，先到我外间去！还有——还有，听我叫你再进来！”

让娜爬也似地躲进外间。接着是好长一阵静默，只有夫人房里咯咯的笑声不时传出来。末了玛格丽特唤她，她才回到房中。

镜前立着一个瘦削的少年郎，一身棕色短衣，腰带里插着匕首，一顶帽子直压到眼睛上。两条修长匀称的腿裹在棕色长袜里，分得很开地站定。玛格丽特大摇大摆地走上前来。

“瞧我威风不威风？姐姐，向你致意！这身衣裳显得我矮了些，可不要紧。让娜，让娜，别摆出这副吓坏了的模样！”

===Original===
“Margot, for God’s sake!” implored Jeanne. “Thy﻿—thy *legs*!”

Margaret inspected them, and cut a caper.

“Said I not that they were shapely? See what a fine calf I have! I must stuff the shoes a little to make them fit, but otherwise it is perfect. The high collar hides my throat, too, which is well. Would it be well to cut my hair, think ye?”

“No!” gasped Jeanne. “A thousand times, no!”

Margaret pulled off her cap, revealing the dark braids bound round and round her shapely little head.

===Chinese===
“玛戈，看在上帝份上！”让娜哀告道。“你——你的腿！”

玛格丽特低头端详一番，还蹦了个跳步。

“我方才怎么说来着，这腿匀称不匀称？瞧我这小腿肚多俊！鞋子得塞点东西才合脚，此外便十全十美了。高领子正好遮住我的脖颈，这也好。依你们看，把头发剪了，是不是更稳妥？”

“不行！”让娜倒抽一口气。“一千个不行！”

玛格丽特摘下帽子，露出缠了一圈又一圈、盘在那颗匀称小脑袋上的深色发辫。

===Original===
“It might be safer,” she reflected. “I cannot wear my cap always, and perhaps it might give rise to suspicion. What was it my father said?﻿—‘See thou dost always set about thine affairs thoroughly, and do not the half only of a thing.’ Give me the scissors!”

“Margot, I implore thee, do not! Thy lovely hair! I﻿—I *will* not countenance it.”

The Countess stood irresolute.

“It﻿—it is﻿—very nice hair,” she said undecidedly. “I doubt it would grow but slowly.”

“Half thy beauty goes with it!” Jeanne said vehemently.

===Chinese===
“剪了也许更稳妥。”她沉吟道。“帽子总不能片刻不离头，没准儿反倒惹人起疑。我父亲那句怎么说的来着？——‘凡你办事，务必做得彻底，切莫只做半截。’剪刀给我！”

“玛戈，我求求你，别剪！你这一头可爱的秀发！我——我决不依。”

伯爵夫人迟疑不决地立着。

“这——这头发——是很好看的。”她拿不定主意地说。“只是恐怕再长起来，慢得很呢。”

“你一半的美色都要跟着它去了！”让娜急切地说。

===Original===
Margaret looked at her seriously.

“Thou dost indeed think that, Jeannette?”

“Yes, yes! Margot, it would be wicked to cut it off!”

“It is to my knees almost. Well, perhaps I will leave it.” On went the cap again. “Wouldst thou know me, Jeanne? Speak truly!”

“Scarcely.” Jeanne walked round her, inspecting. “Thou art suddenly so little. I had thought thee tall.”

===Chinese===
玛格丽特神色郑重地望着她。

“你真是这么想的，小让娜？”

“是的，是的！玛戈，剪掉它可是造孽！”

“头发都快要垂到膝头了。好吧，也许就留着它。”帽子重新扣上。“你还认得我么，让娜？说实话！”

“几乎认不出。”让娜绕着她走了一圈，上下打量。“你忽然变得这样矮小。我原当你是高挑个儿。”

===Original===
“So am I, but this raiment dwarfs me. The face, Jeanne! the face!”

Jeanne stepped back, looking into the Countess’s face with narrowed eyes.

“I should know thee, of course. But mayhap I should need to look twice.”

“Would Simon of Beauvallet know me?”

“They call him the Lynx-Eyed,” Jeanne said dubiously. “And yet﻿—without thy horned headdress, or thy long braids and veil﻿—yes, thou art different.”

===Chinese===
“我本来高，是这身衣裳把我压矮了。脸呢，让娜！脸呢！”

让娜退后一步，眯起眼睛端详伯爵夫人的脸。

“我自然认得你。不过，只怕得多看上两眼才行。”

“博瓦莱的西蒙认得我么？”

“人家管他叫‘锐目’。”让娜迟疑地说。“不过——没有了你那对角的高头饰，没有了长辫和面纱——嗯，你确实大不相同了。”

===Original===
“Summon Hélène,” commanded my lady. “I can trust her, and we will see if she knows me at once.”

Jeanne departed, presently returning with Mademoiselle de Courvonne. Margaret was standing before the fire, arms akimbo, and the long point of her cap drawn down over her shoulder, so that it hid the right side of her face a little.

Mademoiselle cast her a fleeting glance, and on encountering a wicked wink, blushed hotly, and turned her back.

“Where is Madame?” she asked Jeanne. “What does the page here?”

Margaret walked forward, striding nobly, and put her arm about Mademoiselle’s waist. The girl recoiled.

===Chinese===
“传埃莱娜（Hélène）来。”夫人发下话来。“她信得过，咱们倒要看看，她是不是一眼就认出我。”

让娜去了，不多时领着库尔沃娜小姐（Mademoiselle de Courvonne）回来。玛格丽特立在炉火前，双手叉腰，帽子的长尖搭垂过肩，把右半边脸略略遮住。

小姐匆匆瞥了她一眼，正撞上一个促狭的挤眼，登时涨红了脸，扭身背了过去。

“夫人在哪儿？”她问让娜。“这小侍童在这儿做什么？”

玛格丽特迈着堂堂皇皇的大步走上前去，一胳膊搂住小姐的腰。姑娘猛地缩身躲开。

===Original===
“Sirrah!”

“Speak me fair, speak me fair!” Margaret adjured her.

“Madame!” Mademoiselle fell back a pace, hands clasped at her breast. “*Madame!*”

Margaret swept her a bow.

“Am I not a pretty page, sweet chuck?” she smiled.

===Chinese===
“放肆！”

“说句好听的，说句好听的！”玛格丽特向她央告。

“夫人！”小姐倒退一步，双手紧紧交握在胸前。“夫人！”

玛格丽特翩然一躬。

“我是不是个俊俏的小侍童，乖宝贝？”她笑吟吟地问。

===Original===
“*Mon Dieu!*” gasped Hélène. “But﻿—but wherefore?”

Margaret told her, and the lady-in-waiting’s eyes grew rounder and rounder. Before she could exclaim or expostulate, however, a knock fell on the door of the adjoining closet.

“Who﻿—? That is not my cousin’s knock, but a﻿ ﻿… Go, Jeanne!”

Jeanne slipped softly away, closing the door behind her. Margaret tiptoed to it, listening. There came the sound of voices, one deep and forceful.

“Beauvallet!” Margaret slid away from the door. “What can he want?”

===Chinese===
“我的天！”埃莱娜倒抽一口气。“可——可这是为什么呀？”

玛格丽特把缘由对她说了，女官的眼睛越瞪越圆。可她还没来得及惊呼或是劝阻，隔壁内室的门上已落下叩门声。

“谁——？这不是我表弟的敲门声，倒像是……去，让娜！”

让娜悄悄溜出去，随手掩上门。玛格丽特蹑着脚尖走到门边侧耳细听。门外传来人语，其中一个低沉而有力。

“博瓦莱！”玛格丽特从门边闪开。“他能有什么事？”

===Original===
Back came Jeanne, and whispered:

“I have told him that you are abed. Get thee between sheets, madame, quickly!”

“But what doth he want?”

“Naught, I think. He hath not seen you this day.”

Margaret pulled her hair down, and skipped into bed, drawing the clothes up under her chin.

===Chinese===
让娜回来了，低声说：

“我回了他，说您已经睡下。快，夫人，快钻进被窝去！”

“可他有什么事？”

“我想没什么。他今天还没见过您。”

玛格丽特把头发放了下来，一蹦跳上床，把被子拉到下巴底下。

===Original===
“Tell him I am aweary. Why should he wish to see me?”

“I wonder?” said Jeanne, who had her suspicions. She went out again to Simon. “Madame will see you if it is necessary, milor’, but she bids me say that she is aweary.”

“I am sorry to trouble madame,” Simon answered, “but there is that I would say to her.”

“*Eh bien!*” Jeanne shrugged daintily, and allowed him to pass into the Countess’s chamber.

From the great bed Margaret regarded him haughtily.

===Chinese===
“跟他说我乏了。他见我做什么？”

“谁知道呢？”让娜说——她心里自有几分疑心。她又出去见西蒙。“夫人说了，若是要紧事，便见您，英国老爷；不过她托我说，她乏得很。”

“搅扰夫人，甚是不安，”西蒙答道，“只是我有话要对她讲。”

“好吧！”让娜斯文地一耸肩，放他进了伯爵夫人的内室。

玛格丽特从那张大床上倨傲地望着他。

===Original===
“Am I to have no privacy, sir?” she inquired.

Simon, strangely ill-at-ease in these unaccustomed surroundings, bowed, and answered awkwardly.

“I cry your pardon, madame, but I may not see ye tomorrow. I go out to Sal-de-lac, where I shall rest three days. I am come now to say that during mine absence ye will please to keep your rooms. Ye will pardon my discourtesy, but a guard will be set upon these rooms from noon tomorrow, when I depart.”

The Lady Margaret’s eyes flashed dangerously.

“Your insolence passes all bounds, sir!”

===Chinese===
“我连一点清静也不许有么，先生？”她问。

西蒙置身这片全然陌生的境地，说不出的局促，躬了躬身，答话也有些生硬。

“还请夫人恕罪，明天我见不着您了。我要去萨勒德拉克（Sal-de-lac），在那里休整三天。今日前来，是要奉告一句：我不在的这段时日，请您留在自己房中。失礼之处万望海涵——明日正午我动身之时起，这几间房外便要派上卫兵了。”

玛格丽特夫人眼里迸出危险的火星。

“你放肆得没了边，先生！”

===Original===
Simon smiled.

“Mayhap, madame. Your ladies may come to you, but you may not go out.”

“A prisoner in mine own castle! Get thee hence, Lord of Beauvallet!”

But when Simon had gone, she sprang up, flushed and excited.

“It could not be better! It could not be better! Malvallet will command in his absence, and he would not dare to force himself upon me! None will notice mine escape, and all but Hélène here, and﻿—and﻿—Amélie, or Isabelle, must think that I am sick. Oh, it is marvellous, marvellous! We will leave this place at four in the morning, Jeannette, thou and I!”

“God pity me!” Jeanne sighed, and turned her eyes away from the Lady Margaret’s attire with a shudder.

===Chinese===
西蒙微微一笑。

“兴许是，夫人。您的女官可以来见您，您却出不得门。”

“在自己的城堡里做囚犯！滚吧，博瓦莱大人！”

可西蒙前脚刚走，她便一跃而起，满面飞红，兴奋难耐。

“再好不过了！再好不过了！他不在，便由马尔瓦莱号令，他断不敢硬闯到我跟前来！谁也不会觉察我逃走，除了这儿的埃莱娜，还有——还有阿梅莉（Amélie）或者伊莎贝尔（Isabelle），旁人只会当我病着。哦，妙极了，妙极了！明儿一早四点动身，小让娜，就你和我！”

“上帝可怜可怜我吧！”让娜叹了口气，一眼瞥见玛格丽特夫人这身行头，哆嗦着把目光挪开了。

## IX / 第九章

===Original===
How the Lady Margaret Escaped

The Lady Margaret tramped blithely along the high road, a knapsack slung over her shoulder, and a staff in her hand. Beside her trudged Mademoiselle Jeanne, very weary, and very nervous. She wore a kerchief over her curls, and a cloak wrapped round her form. She, too, carried a staff, but whereas the Lady Margaret swung hers boldly and stepped out with a will, Jeanne’s little feet stumbled often over the stones, and she leaned heavily on her staff.

“Sister,” said the Lady Margaret, “how many leagues think ye we have covered?”

“An hundred,” Jeanne answered with feeling.

===Chinese===
玛格丽特夫人如何出逃

玛格丽特夫人（the Lady Margaret）兴冲冲地在大路上大步赶路，肩上挎着行囊，手里拄着一根手杖。让娜小姐（Mademoiselle Jeanne）在旁边步履沉重，又累又慌。她头上包着方巾，遮住鬈发，身上裹着斗篷。她也拄着一根手杖，只是玛格丽特夫人挥杖阔步、走得虎虎有劲，让娜的小脚却时时绊在石头上，整个身子都压在手杖上。

“妹妹，”玛格丽特夫人说，“你猜咱们走了多少里格了？”

“一百里格。”让娜深有感触地答道。

===Original===
“Nay, I think not. Let me see. We did leave Belrémy at half after four﻿—Jeanne, was it not easy? Not one of those great oafs of English guards suspected, and Lord Simon was nowhere to be seen! Then we walked to Balderin, which is two and a half leagues distant from Belrémy, and it was eight of the clock. How long did we spend at the tavern where we breakfasted?”

“Five minutes,” sighed Jeanne.

“Nay, I think it was half-an-hour. Jeanne, did I not swagger well? And﻿—and kissed the wench who served us!”

Jeanne shivered.

“My heart was in my mouth. Thou wert﻿—thou wert﻿—*terrible*!”

Margaret laughed.

===Chinese===
“哪有，我看没有。让我算算。我们离开贝雷米（Belrémy）是在四点半——让娜，是不是容易得很？那些傻大个英国卫兵一个也没起疑，西蒙大人连影子都不见！接着我们走到巴尔丹（Balderin），离贝雷米两里格半，到的时候是八点钟。我们在吃早饭的那家客栈耽搁了多久？”

“五分钟。”让娜叹道。

“不对，我看有半个钟头。让娜，我那副大摇大摆的架势扮得像不像？还——还亲了伺候咱们的小娘子一口！”

让娜一个哆嗦。

“我的小心脏都提到嗓子眼了。你真是——你真是——可怕极了！”

玛格丽特大笑。

===Original===
“I was wonderful. Just a pert pageboy. Well, when we left Balderin we went onward to Razincourt. And then it was a little after eleven, was it not?”

“That village! Ugh! The tavern! Oh, *mon Dieu*!”

“And the drunken peasant who would have clouted me for a saucy knave had he not stumbled over the chair.” Margaret gave a little skip, chuckling light-heartedly. “We ate dinner there, and I drank sack,” she grimaced. “Some of it I managed to spill,” she added pensively. “I think we remained there an hour. The dinner was not﻿—very nice, was it?”

Jeanne closed her eyes for a moment.

“The meat﻿ ﻿…” she moaned.

===Chinese===
“我演得妙极了。就是个目中无人的小侍童。嗯，出了巴尔丹，我们便往拉赞库尔（Razincourt）去，那时是十一点刚过，是不是？”

“那个村子！呸！那家客栈！哦，我的老天（mon Dieu）！”

“还有那个醉汉，若不是自己绊在椅子上，就要当我是没规矩的小无赖，给我一顿好打了。”玛格丽特轻巧地一蹦，咯咯直乐。“我们在那儿吃了午饭，我还喝了萨克酒，”她做了个怪相。“有几分被我设法泼掉了，”她沉吟着补充。“我想我们在那儿待了一个钟头。那顿饭可不——不怎么样，是不是？”

让娜闭了一会儿眼睛。

“那肉……”她呻吟道。

===Original===
“I know. Well, after that we did walk on and on﻿—on﻿—”

“And on and on and on.”

“Thou silly Jeannette!﻿—till we came to the brook. And there we rested a while. And now we are here, and I wonder where it may be?”

“Where what may be?”

“Here,” said the Lady Margaret, embracing the whole countryside. “I had planned to rest the night at Tourdelonne. It is a tiny village, my poor Jeanne, and mayhap we shall sleep in the stable. What is the time, I wonder? It must be after three.”

===Chinese===
“我知道。呃，打那以后我们就走啊走啊——走——”

“走啊，走啊，再走啊。”

“你这傻小让娜（Jeannette）！——一直走到那条小溪边，歇了一阵。这会儿咱们到了这儿，我就是纳闷这儿是哪儿了？”

“什么在哪儿？”

“这儿呀。”玛格丽特夫人说着，张开手臂把四野都揽了进来。“我原本打算在图尔德洛讷（Tourdelonne）过夜。那是个小得出奇的村子，我可怜的让娜，没准儿咱们得睡在马厩里。不知现在是什么时辰了？该有三点过了。”

===Original===
“Long after three. We have been walking for hours.”

“It has not seemed so to me. But if we have, then Tourdelonne must be near. And when we are there we shall have covered five leagues, Jeannette, and another day will see us at Turincel.”

Jeanne wilted.

“We﻿—we must walk tomorrow﻿—as much as today?” she asked.

“It is wiser,” nodded the Lady Margaret firmly. “Oh, Simon of Beauvallet, ye shall rue the day ye sought to quell Margaret of Belrémy!”

===Chinese===
“三点早过啦。我们都走了好几个钟头了。”

“我倒不觉得。不过要是真走了这么久，图尔德洛讷就该到了。到了那儿，咱们就走满了五里格，小让娜，再走一天就能到图兰塞尔（Turincel）了。”

让娜蔫了下去。

“我们——我们明天还得走——跟今天一样多？”她问。

“那样才稳妥。”玛格丽特夫人坚决地点头。“哦，博瓦莱的西蒙（Simon of Beauvallet），你既想压服贝雷米的玛格丽特，总有你懊悔的那一天！”

===Original===
“I do trust we shall not rue the day we sought to escape from Simon of Beauvallet,” Jeanne said peevishly. “My feet are raw and blistered.”

“Thou poor little one!” Margaret slipped an arm in hers. “Lean on me, Jeannette. I should not have brought thee. It was thoughtless and cruel! Thou hast not my strength!”

Jeanne pulled herself together.

“Nay, I am well enough, Margot. Shall﻿—shall we have to sleep﻿—in the common room, think you?”

“I do not know. Perhaps we may find two rooms. One for you, at least.”

===Chinese===
“我只求咱们逃出博瓦莱的西蒙手心这件事，别到头来也叫我们懊悔才好。”让娜没好气地说。“我的脚都磨破了，起了水泡。”

“你这可怜的小人儿！”玛格丽特伸手挽住她的胳膊。“靠着我吧，小让娜。我真不该带上你。是我欠思量，太狠心了！你没有我这把力气！”

让娜振作起来。

“不，我还撑得住，玛戈（Margot）。你说——你说咱们是不是得睡——睡在大堂里？”

“我不知道。也许能弄到两间房。至少，给你弄一间。”

===Original===
Jeanne clutched her arm.

“Margot, we must not be separated!” she implored.

Margaret drew her arched brows together.

“I must see what I can do,” she said. “Perhaps if I say that thou art sick, they would give thee a chamber, and I could steal into it when none is watching. It is too cold to sleep in the woods, alack!”

“Sleep in the woods!” Jeanne almost shrieked. “Margot!”

===Chinese===
让娜一把攥紧她的胳膊。

“玛戈，咱们千万不能分开！”她哀求道。

玛格丽特蹙起两道弯眉。

“我得见机行事。”她说。“要是我说你病了，兴许他们会给你一间屋子，我就趁没人瞧见的时候溜进去。唉，这天睡林子可太冷了！”

“睡林子里！”让娜差点尖叫起来。“玛戈！”

===Original===
“But it is impossible in this weather,” Margaret assured her. “Ah, look ahead! I see houses!”

“Hovels!”

“Tourdelonne!”

“I could welcome my Geoffrey,” sighed Jeanne. “Even though he were in a rage with me.” Her face brightened. “I wonder, is he very terrible when he is angered?” For a time she pondered this question deeply. “I think he might be,” she said at length, and nodded, pleased.

“If ever ye take him to husband he will assuredly beat thee,” Margaret remarked. “Because he is English.”

===Chinese===
“这天气自然睡不成。”玛格丽特宽慰她。“啊，往前看！我瞧见房子了！”

“破棚子！”

“图尔德洛讷！”

“这会儿能迎见我家杰弗里（Geoffrey）也好啊。”让娜叹道。“哪怕他正冲我发火。”她的脸亮了起来。“不知他发起火来凶不凶？”她把这个问题认认真真地琢磨了一阵。“我看多半凶。”她末了说，还欣然点头。

“你若哪天真嫁了他，他必定揍你。”玛格丽特评道。“谁叫他是英国人。”

===Original===
“Oh, dost thou think so?” Jeanne stepped out more briskly. “He might, of course, and yet﻿ ﻿… no, I think he is too gentle and kind. But very masterful. I wonder what he will say when he finds me gone?” She dimpled a little.

“I wonder what he will say when I bring Turincel about his ears?” Margaret said viciously.

“Ah no, Margot! You﻿—you must not! Kill﻿—Lord Simon, if you will﻿—but﻿—but not Geoffrey!”

“Never fear.” Margaret patted her shoulder. “Thou shalt have him as thy prisoner.”

“Then I shall not like it at all,” said Jeanne decidedly.

===Chinese===
“哦，你当真这么想？”让娜脚步轻快起来。“他当然可能，可是……不，我看他太温柔太心善了。就是霸道了些。不知他发现我跑了，会说什么？”她脸上绽出浅浅的酒窝。

“我倒想知道，等我领着图兰塞尔的人马杀到他头上，他会说什么？”玛格丽特恶狠狠地说。

“啊，不，玛戈！你——你不能这样！要杀——随你杀西蒙大人——可是——可是别动杰弗里！”

“别怕。”玛格丽特拍拍她的肩。“到了时候，把他活捉了给你。”

“那我可一点也不乐意。”让娜斩钉截铁地说。

===Original===
“What! Dost thou like to be his prisoner?”

“Yes, I do,” Jeanne said.

Margaret’s lip curled.

“An English tyrant’s prisoner! *Mon Dieu*, where is thy spirit?”

“It went when I fell in love with Geoffrey,” Jeanne answered. “One day thou wilt understand.”

“God grant that day be long in coming!” Margaret cried.

===Chinese===
“什么！难道你倒喜欢让他把你当俘虏？”

“对，我就是喜欢。”让娜说。

玛格丽特撇了撇嘴。

“做英国暴君的俘虏！我的天，你的胆气哪儿去了？”

“爱上杰弗里的那一天，它就跑掉了。”让娜答道。“总有一天你会懂的。”

“上帝保佑那一天晚点来吧！”玛格丽特嚷道。

===Original===
They were come now to the straggling hamlet, and they halted before the rude tavern. From within came the sound of men’s voices, laughing noisily. Jeanne shrank a little closer to the Countess.

“Margot﻿—must we﻿—!”

“We must,” Margaret said resolutely, and drew a deep breath. She knocked loudly on the door, and took a firm hold on her staff.

Presently the door was opened, and the landlord faced them, clad in a soiled leather jerkin.

“Good sir,” began Margaret, as deeply as she was able, “my sister and I journey to Joulinceaulx for the festival. Have ye room for the night?”

===Chinese===
这时她们已到那片零零落落的小村，在一家粗陋的客店门前停住。店里传出男人的嗓音，闹哄哄地笑着。让娜往伯爵夫人身边又缩了缩。

“玛戈——咱们非得——！”

“非得。”玛格丽特毅然说道，深深吸了一口气。她大声敲门，一手紧紧攥住手杖。

不多时门开了，店主人出现在她们面前，一身污渍斑斑的皮紧身衣。

“好先生，”玛格丽特把嗓音压得尽量低沉，开了口，“我和舍妹前往若兰索（Joulinceaulx）赶节庆。今晚可有空房？”

===Original===
“No,” said the landlord uncompromisingly, and would have shut the door, but that Margaret set her foot within.

“But listen, good host, my﻿—my father gave me a little money for the journey, and I can pay for our lodging.”

The landlord seemed undecided, but Jeanne, plucking up courage, clinched the matter.

“Oh, sir, do not turn us away! Indeed, I am weary, and hungry. Could ye let us sleep in the loft above the stable, perhaps?” She smiled wistfully upon him.

“Well, come within,” he grunted. “But I’ll see thy money first!” he added, turning truculently upon Margaret.

She pulled out a gold piece, and gave it him. His eyes shone greedily, and he pocketed it, beckoning them in.

===Chinese===
“没有。”店主人一口回绝，正要关门，玛格丽特却把一只脚跨进了门里。

“可是听我说，好店家，我——我爹给了我一点路费，住店的钱我们出得起。”

店主人似乎还在犹豫，让娜却鼓起勇气，一锤定了音。

“哦，先生，别赶我们走！我真的又累又饿。要不，您能让我们睡马厩上头的阁楼么？”她朝他凄然一笑。

“好吧，进来吧。”他哼了一声。“可我得先见着钱！”他又补上一句，气势汹汹地转向玛格丽特。

她摸出一枚金币递给他。他眼里闪着贪婪的光，把金币揣进兜里，招手让她们进去。

===Original===
The kitchen was very hot, and smelt of sack. Some half-a-dozen men were sprawled about a large table, upon which supper was laid. When they saw Jeanne one or two of them sat up, smirking, but for the most part they paid no heed to the newcomers.

As unobtrusively as possible the two girls slipped into their places in the table. Margaret pushed her shrinking companion on to a stool at the end of the table, and seated herself beside her, in between Jeanne and a burly fellow with a ferocious red beard. The landlord thrust two wooden platters of salt-beef before them, and some coarse bread. Hungry as she was, Jeanne’s dainty palate turned from the unappetising, ill-cooked and badly served food, but she made shift to eat, choking down her repulsion. Margaret, who was made of sterner stuff, betrayed no disgust at the rude fare, but fell to with a will. One of the men sitting opposite eyed Jeanne curiously, so that she blushed, and kept her eyes lowered.

“Yon wench picks at her food,” remarked her tormentor. “A dainty maid!”

“My sister is not strong,” Margaret said quickly. “She hath no appetite.”

“The food is good enough,” growled their host. “If it is not to thy liking﻿—”

“It is good indeed,” Margaret made haste to assure him. “Is it not, Jeanne?”

===Chinese===
厨房里热得难受，一股萨克酒的气味。五六个汉子横七竖八地围着一张大桌，桌上已摆好晚饭。瞧见让娜，有一两个坐直了身子，嬉皮笑脸，不过大多并没有理会新来的两人。

两个姑娘尽量不惹眼地在桌边落了座。玛格丽特把直往后缩的同伴推到桌梢的一张凳上，自己在她旁边坐下，一边是让娜，一边是个满脸凶相的红胡子壮汉。店主人把两木盘咸牛肉和几块粗面包往她们面前一推。让娜虽然饿了，那娇刁的胃口却实在受不了这卖相粗劣、烹调糟糕的东西，可她还是强忍着反感，勉强吃了下去。玛格丽特天生一副硬骨头，对这粗粝饭食没有半分嫌恶，痛快地大嚼起来。对面坐着的一个人好奇地打量让娜，看得她红了脸，眼帘低垂。

“那小娘们吃东西挑三拣四。”纠缠她的人开口道。“真是个娇小姐！”

“我妹妹身子弱。”玛格丽特赶忙说。“她没什么胃口。”

“这饭食够好的了。”店家低声咆哮。“你要是不对胃口——”

“好吃得很。”玛格丽特忙不迭地保证。“是不是，让娜？”

===Original===
“Yes, Léon. Very good,” Jeanne answered in a small voice.

“Perchance thy sister is used to richly cooked meats?” sneered the landlord, unconvinced that his guests were not slighting his culinary efforts.

Margaret nodded.

“My sister is serving-maid to the Lady Margaret of Belrémy,” she said daringly, and heard Jeanne gasp beside her.

There was a guffaw of laughter.

“That for a tale!” jeered the man opposite. “Thou pert youngster!”

===Chinese===
“是，莱昂（Léon）。很好吃。”让娜细声细气地答道。

“莫非你妹妹吃惯了精细烹调的肉食？”店主人冷笑道，总疑心这两位客人是在小看他的手艺。

玛格丽特点点头。

“我妹妹是贝雷米的玛格丽特夫人的贴身侍女。”她大着胆子说，听得身旁的让娜倒抽一口气。

桌边爆出一阵哄笑。

“这话谁信！”对面的汉子讥笑。“你这不知天高地厚的小子！”

===Original===
Margaret’s neighbour leaned across her to stare at Jeanne, whose hands had begun to tremble.

“Well, she is pale enough,” he rumbled. “Thy hands are too white, lass. Thou dost not labour on the fields, i’ faith.”

“She is my lady’s tiring-woman,” Margaret said.

A little stir went round the table.

“And what art thou, springald?” asked one. “Page, belike, with thy grand tunic?”

“Page indeed,” nodded Margaret. “My lady hath given us leave of absence to﻿—to journey to Joulinceaulx for the festival.”

===Chinese===
玛格丽特的邻座探过身来，直勾勾地盯着让娜，让娜的双手已开始发抖。

“唔，她脸色是够白的。”他瓮声瓮气地说。“你这双手也太白了，小娘子。说真格的，你可不像下田干活的人。”

“她是我家夫人的梳妆侍女。”玛格丽特说。

桌边掠过一阵小小的骚动。

“那你呢，小哥？”有一个问。“瞧你这身神气的紧身上衣，八成是个侍童吧？”

“正是侍童。”玛格丽特点头。“我家夫人准了我们假，去——去若兰索赶节庆。”

===Original===
“And what may be thy name?” inquired the red-bearded man.

“Léon Margrute,” Margaret answered promptly.

The landlord came to the table.

“The accursed English are in Belrémy, is it not so?” he asked.

“Ay.” Margaret’s eyes flashed.

“What does thy lady?”

===Chinese===
“那么你叫什么名字？”红胡子汉子问道。

“莱昂·马尔格吕特（Léon Margrute）。”玛格丽特应声便答。

店主人走到桌边。

“那伙天杀的英国人进了贝雷米，是不是？”他问。

“是。”玛格丽特眼里闪着光。

“你家夫人在做什么？”

===Original===
“She is prisoner.”

“Ho-ho!” The man opposite Jeanne clapped his hands to his sides. “The proud countess prisoner! Ho-ho! There’s for her and her hot blood!”

Jeanne laid an imploring hand on Margaret’s arm, for the Countess had grown suddenly stiff. She recovered herself, and forced a laugh.

“Hast seen my lady, then?” she asked.

“Once, when she rode out with her fine court. A haughty maid, indeed! Men say that she leads her men into battle. There’s a shrew!”

“She﻿—she did so once,” Margaret admitted. “And well they fought!”

===Chinese===
“她成了囚犯。”

“嗬嗬！”让娜对面的汉子拍着大腿。“那位傲气的女伯爵当了俘虏！嗬嗬！这就是她那火爆脾气的报应！”

让娜恳求地把手搭上玛格丽特的胳膊，伯爵夫人浑身已骤然僵住。她随即缓过神来，逼出一声笑。

“这么说，你见过我家夫人？”她问。

“见过一回，她带着她那排场的扈从骑马出行。好一个傲气的小娘们！男人们说，她领着手下人上阵打仗。真是个泼妇！”

“她——她确实那么干过一回，”玛格丽特承认。“而且打得漂亮极了！”

===Original===
“Riding astride her horse, clad in armour! A forward, masterless wench!”

Someone cracked a lewd joke, and Margaret’s cheeks became scarlet with fury. The red-bearded man grinned.

“See the young turkey-cock! Perchance thou dost love thy lady, Léon Margrute?”

“That do I!”

“And is she kind to thee?”

The colour died out of Margaret’s face. She laughed.

===Chinese===
“跨骑在马上，还披着铠甲！一个轻狂放肆、没人管教的野丫头！”

有人说了句下流笑话，玛格丽特气得双颊通红。红胡子汉子咧嘴直笑。

“瞧这只小雄火鸡！莫非你爱戴你家夫人，莱昂·马尔格吕特？”

“爱又怎样！”

“那她待你好么？”

玛格丽特脸上的血色褪尽了。她笑了。

===Original===
“Oh, she is sometimes kind, and sometimes cruel.”

He nodded sympathetically.

“Ay, ay! ’Tis ever thus with these noble dames. But surely thou art over-young, lad?”

Certainly she looked it in her boy’s gear, though in reality she numbered twenty-five summers.

“I﻿—oh, I am﻿—seventeen,” she stammered.

“And thy pretty sister?” asked the man before Jeanne, leaning over the table to leer into her face.

===Chinese===
“哦，她有时和善，有时狠心。”

他同情地点点头。

“是啊，是啊！这些贵妇人向来都是这样。不过小伙子，你的年纪也忒小了些吧？”

一身男孩装束的她看上去确实如此，虽然论实际年纪，她已度过了二十五个寒暑。

“我——哦，我——十七岁。”她结结巴巴地说。

“你那位俊俏的妹妹呢？”让娜对面的汉子问，隔着桌子探过身来，好色地凑近她的脸。

===Original===
Jeanne shrank back, gripping her fingers together.

“Eighteen,” Margaret answered. “Be good enough to sit back, sir. Ye discommode my sister.”

“Thou saucy knave! Is thy sister so nice then, that an honest man﻿—”

“Let be, let be!” growled Margaret’s burly neighbour. “The maid is tired.”

“Too tired to kiss?” the tormentor grinned, and lurched forward across the table.

Jeanne gave a tiny cry, but Margaret was on her feet in a trice, dagger in hand.

===Chinese===
让娜往后一缩，手指绞在一起。

“十八岁。”玛格丽特答道。“先生，劳驾靠后坐。你碍着我妹妹了。”

“你这放肆的小无赖！敢情你妹妹就金贵成这样，正经男人碰不得——”

“算了，算了！”玛格丽特身旁的壮汉低吼。“姑娘累了。”

“累得亲一口都不行？”那纠缠不休的家伙咧嘴一笑，踉跄着隔着桌子扑过来。

让娜低低惊叫一声，玛格丽特却已霍地站起，匕首在手。

===Original===
“Keep off, sirrah!” she commanded. “My dagger is sharp.”

On the instant there was an outcry, and three men scrambled up and would have come at Margaret had not the red-bearded giant interposed his huge frame.

“Tush! Sit ye down, Jacques and Louis! ’Tis but a lad. Let the girl be, Founard!”

“I would teach the pert knave to speak his elders fair,” grumbled one, but he sat down again. “Thou art too soft, Ranaud.”

Ranaud thrust Margaret into her seat.

“Put up thy dagger, foolish pup, else I will let them at thee.”

===Chinese===
“退后，小子！”她厉声喝道。“我的匕首快得很。”

刹那间一片嚷嚷，三条汉子跳起身，就要朝玛格丽特扑来，却被那红胡子巨汉横身挡住。

“嘁！坐下，雅克（Jacques）、路易（Louis）！不过是个小子。放过那姑娘，富纳尔（Founard）！”

“我得教教这不知上下的小子怎么好好说话。”一个嘟囔着，到底还是坐了回去。“你也太软了，拉诺（Ranaud）。”

拉诺把玛格丽特按回座位。

“把匕首收起来，蠢小狗，不然我就放他们过去收拾你。”

===Original===
“I’ll have no brawling here!” the landlord cried. “Out ye go, young sir, and your sister with ye! Thy pretty ways and mincing tongue!”

Ranaud brought his great fist down on the table so that the platters jumped.

“Let be, I say!” he roared. “God’s Wounds, what is this pother? If the wench is modest, why, the better for her! I’ll crack thy skull for thee, fat host!”

The landlord drew back muttering, for Ranaud was too formidable for his taste. The discontent subsided gradually, and in a little while Margaret took Jeanne’s hand and rose.

“Good sir,” she said, addressing the landlord. “Wilt show us the way to the stable-loft?”

“I have no room. Hast eaten. Go now.”

===Chinese===
“我这儿不许斗殴！”店主人嚷道。“出去吧，小少爷，带上你妹妹！瞧你那忸怩作态的做派，还有那拿腔拿调的舌头！”

拉诺的大拳砸在桌上，震得盘子直跳。

“说了，住手！”他大吼。“上帝的伤口！这算哪门子乱子？姑娘稳重，那是她的福气！你再多嘴，我就敲碎你这胖店家的脑袋！”

店主人嘟嘟囔囔退了回去，拉诺这号人物可不是他招惹得起的。不满的声浪渐渐平息，不多时，玛格丽特牵起让娜的手站起身。

“好先生，”她对店主人说。“劳驾指点我们马厩的阁楼怎么走？”

“我没房。饭吃过了。走吧。”

===Original===
“Nay, I prithee﻿—”

Up got Ranaud, his little eyes blazing fiercely.

“Have I to teach thee a lesson in manners?” he thundered, and the landlord retreated.

“I will show thee, I will show thee!” he said hastily.

“And I will come too,” said Ranaud.

Out they went into the fast-gathering gloom, the landlord hurrying nervously before them, Jeanne clinging to Margaret’s hand, and Ranaud striding along beside them, towering over all. So they came to the tumbledown stable, and with a muttered word that they would find the ladder into the barn in place, the landlord went away.

===Chinese===
“不，求您——”

拉诺霍然起身，一双小眼睛凶光毕露。

“还要我教教你规矩么？”他雷霆般地喝道，店主人连连后退。

“我带路，我带路！”他慌忙说道。

“我也一道去。”拉诺说。

一行人走进愈来愈浓的暮色里，店主人提心吊胆地在前头急走，让娜死死攥着玛格丽特的手，拉诺在她们身旁大步而行，高出一众人等。就这样来到那座摇摇欲坠的马厩前，店主人咕哝了一句进谷仓的梯子就在原处，便走开了。

===Original===
Margaret turned to the kindly giant.

“I have to thank thee, sir, for thy protection,” she began. “Indeed﻿—”

“It is naught. Up with ye into the loft, and bolt the trap, youngster. Mayhap I will accompany thee part of the way to Joulinceaulx. All ways are one to me.”

“Why, it is﻿—it is very kind,” Margaret said nervously, “but﻿—”

“It is not your goal, belike?” Ranaud asked shrewdly.

“I﻿—of course it is﻿—I mean﻿—”

===Chinese===
玛格丽特转向这位好心肠的巨人。

“先生的护佑之恩，我不能不谢。”她开口道。“真的——”

“不算什么。上阁楼去吧，小家伙，把活板门闩好。兴许我能陪你走一程去若兰索的路。天下的路在我看来都一样。”

“这——这真是太好心了。”玛格丽特忐忑地说。“可是——”

“若兰索多半不是你们要去的地方吧？”拉诺精明地问。

“我——当然是——我是说——”

===Original===
“Oh, I am not curious!” he answered. “Go thy way if ye will, but I am a masterless man, and I have taken a fancy to thee. Art over-young to go wandering over the country alone with thy sister.”

“You﻿—would come with us?” Margaret asked uncertainly.

“Ay, if ye will. There are a-many rogues about, and mayhap ye will be robbed or killed. If we join company I can guard thee from such. Ye can trust me.”

“Indeed, I think so,” Margaret said, and put out her hand. “May﻿—may we speak more of this in the morning?”

“Ay, if ye will.” Margaret’s hand was lost in a gigantic paw. “Get thee to rest now. And bolt the trap.”

“I will,” she promised. “I thank you, sir.”

===Chinese===
“哦，我并不多问！”他答道。“你们要走只管走，可我是个无主之人，一眼瞧着你便投了缘。带着妹妹独自在外乡闯荡，你的年纪还忒小了。”

“你——愿意跟我们同行？”玛格丽特拿不准地问。

“嗯，只要你们愿意。这一带歹人极多，没准儿你们会被劫，或是送命。咱们结了伴，这些祸事我都能替你们挡掉。你们信得过我。”

“确实，我信得过。”玛格丽特说着伸出手去。“明——明早咱们再细谈这事可好？”

“嗯，听你们便。”玛格丽特的手整个没进一只巨掌里。“这就歇着去吧。记得闩活板门。”

“我会的。”她许诺道。“多谢您，先生。”

===Original===
