“他们这会儿该已经过来了，不过会走我留开的那扇门，”他喘着气说。

前头传来玛格丽特的声音。

===Original===
“Stairs! Stairs!”

“Gently!” Simon hissed, and pushed by Geoffrey. “There may be men below. I go first.” Sword in hand he went down the stairs, to find a scullion staring at him open-mouthed. They had come to the kitchens.

The scullion fled for his life, down yet another passage, calling for help.

“The window!” Geoffrey gasped.

===Chinese===
“楼梯！楼梯！”

“轻些！”西蒙压着嗓子喝道，挤到杰弗里身前。“底下兴许有人。我先走。”他提着剑走下楼梯，只见一个帮厨小厮张着嘴，直勾勾地瞪着他。原来他们已闯到了厨房。

那小厮没命地逃了，顺着又一条过道奔下去，一路呼救。

“窗户！”杰弗里喘着气说。

===Original===
“Nay, the door,” Simon answered, pointing. “For your life!”

Ranaud tore it open, and out they tumbled into a narrow yard. At the end of it was a barred gate, and to this they ran.

Sounds betokening pursuit came from behind them, and it was with desperate fingers that Simon and Ranaud dragged back the bolts. The gates swung outward, and they found themselves upon greensward. To the right was Santoy, with his men. He saw them, and spurred forward, leading Simon’s horse, and shouting to his men to follow.

Simon attempted no explanation, but flung Margaret up on to his horse. She clutched at the animal’s mane, sitting astride, and gripping hard with her knees.

===Chinese===
“不，走门，”西蒙一指答道。“逃命要紧！”

拉诺把门扯开，众人跌跌撞撞冲出去，落进一个狭窄的院子。院子尽头有一扇装着铁栅的门，他们朝它直奔过去。

身后已传来追兵将至的声息，西蒙和拉诺十指发狂般拽开了门闩。栅门向外荡开，他们发觉自己已踏上草地。右边正是桑托伊（Santoy），带着他的人马。他望见了他们，立即催马向前，一手牵着西蒙的马，一面高声喝令部下跟上。

西蒙不费话解释，把玛格丽特一举托上马背。她攥住马鬃，横坐鞍上，双膝死死夹紧马腹。

===Original===
Geoffrey seized his own mount, and swung himself up, setting Jeanne on her feet before he did so.

“Hand her up!” he called, and Simon tossed her into his arms.

Ranaud clambered clumsily on to the back of one of the spare horses, grunting and cursing.

“God’s my life, I’ve never sat a horse but once before.”

Simon heaved himself into the saddle behind Margaret, his strong arms about her, lifting her across the saddlebow.

===Chinese===
杰弗里抓住自己的坐骑，先把让娜放到地上站稳，方才翻身跃上。

“把她递上来！”他喊道，西蒙便把让娜抛进他的臂弯。

拉诺手忙脚乱地爬上一匹备用的马背，哼哧咒骂个不停。

“上帝的性命，我此前统共只骑过一回马。”

西蒙奋力跨上马鞍，坐在玛格丽特身后，两条铁臂环住她，把她拢到鞍桥之前。

===Original===
“Cling tight,” he said, and smiled down at her. “To the south, and spur them on!” he commanded his men, and on the word his horse sprang forward.

It was not a moment too soon, for through the gate behind them came their pursuers, yelling in hideous discord. For a while they ran after the mounted men, but soon they realised the hopelessness of the chase, and turned back.

Simon looked over his shoulder.

===Chinese===
“抱紧了，”他说着低头朝她一笑。“往南，催马！”他号令部下，话音落处，他的坐骑已纵身向前。

这一走再迟片刻便不及了：身后的院门里追兵已然涌出，怪叫乱嚷，嘈杂得刺耳。他们徒步追了马队一阵，很快便知追之无望，掉头回去了。

西蒙回头望了一眼。

===Original===
“Gone to get horses, belike. Well, we are near the border, and a little while should see us out of this accursed land.” He looked across at Geoffrey, and laughed. “Geoffrey, this is the first time﻿—and the last, please God﻿—that I have turned my back on the enemy.”

“And the first time that thou hast lost thy head,” Geoffrey retorted. “I was so taken aback﻿—after thy warning to me, too, that I should keep a cool brain! God’s my life, what will King Henry say?”

“He will say good riddance to a foul knave. Bear to the right, Santoy.”

Raoul’s palace stood but a league from the border, and soon they had crossed it, riding in close formation. Not until they were half a league into the neighbouring domain did Simon give the order to draw rein. Then they halted, while Simon slammed his sword home into the scabbard, and unstrapped his great green cloak from the saddle. This he threw over his shoulders, clasping it at the neck, and drew the heavy folds round him so that they covered the Lady Margaret, shielding her both from the cold wind and from curious eyes. He shifted her a little, so that she lay cradled in his left arm, held in an unyielding grip. Her late labours, the terror she had passed through, and the hardships she had endured during these last five days all told on her. While danger threatened and she had to take command of her emprise she bore up, shaking off fatigue, but now that Simon had come and swept all before him, the need for strength and watchfulness was gone. She lay limp in his arms, half-conscious, knowing herself safe at last. Too tired to realise﻿—or, if she did realise, to care﻿—that Simon was her hated foe, she nestled close against his hard armour, clutching his cloak with a little sigh of relief. Simon looked down at her, and saw that her eyes were shut. And something else he saw, which made the fierce light come into his eyes again. A red patch showed on the sleeve of her tunic. He turned his head, addressing Geoffrey, who was busy wrapping his Jeanne in a cloak.

===Chinese===
“多半是牵马去了。也罢，咱们已近边境，再有小半会儿就该出这块可诅咒的地界了。”他望向杰弗里，笑出声来。“杰弗里，这是我有生以来头一回——求上帝保佑，也是末一回——背朝敌人而走。”

“也是你头一回昏了头脑，”杰弗里回敬道。“我真是惊得呆了——你还告诫过我，要我保持头脑冷静呢！上帝的性命，亨利国王（King Henry）会怎么说？”

“他会说，少了个无耻恶棍，正好清净。往右走，桑托伊。”

拉乌尔的宫邸距边境只有一里格，不久他们便驰过边界，一路结阵而行。直到深入邻境半里格，西蒙才下令收缰。众人停了下来，西蒙把长剑啪地插回鞘中，又从鞍上解下那件巨大的绿氅。他把绿氅往肩上一披，在颈前扣好，将厚重的褶层拢向身周，盖住玛格丽特夫人，既为她遮挡寒风，也遮住旁人好奇的目光。他把她的身子挪了挪，让她稳稳卧进自己左臂的弯里，箍得纹丝不动。方才的劳碌、经历的惊怖，加上这五天来所受的颠连困顿，此刻一齐压上了她。危险当头、须由她亲自统领这番壮举之时，她强自撑持，把疲乏抖落；可如今西蒙既到，所向披靡，再要气力与警醒便无用了。她软软卧在他臂弯里，半昏半醒，只知道自己终于平安了。她累得顾不上意识到——即便意识到，也顾不上在意——西蒙正是她切齿的仇敌，只顾紧紧偎着他坚硬的铠甲，攥着他的斗篷，轻轻舒出一口宽慰之气。西蒙低头看她，见她双目已阖。他还看见了别的一样东西，那东西使他眼中重新燃起凶光。她短衣的袖子上洇着一块红斑。他扭头招呼正忙着拿斗篷裹让娜的杰弗里。

===Original===
“Geoffrey, she is wounded. I want linen.”

Jeanne started.

“Wounded? Margot? Oh, sir, is﻿—is it deep?”

“Nay, I think not. Give me thy kerchief.”

===Chinese===
“杰弗里，她受伤了。给我布。”

让娜吓了一跳。

“受伤？玛戈（Margot）？哦，先生，伤——伤得深么？”

“不深，我想。把你的围巾给我。”

===Original===
Jeanne tore it away from her neck, handing it to him, and for a while Simon bent over his charge, slitting the sleeve of Margaret’s tunic with his dagger. The wound was above the elbow, and slight, but Margaret gave a little cry when Simon started to bind it tightly round. He paid no heed, but tied the bandage, and drew his cloak round her once more, so that she was entirely hidden.

“Art ready, Geoffrey?”

Geoffrey was kissing Jeanne at the moment, but he nodded, and they trotted forward briskly. He drew away from Simon, and looked down into the big eyes that surveyed him.

===Chinese===
让娜把围巾从颈间扯下递给他。西蒙俯身看护他的伤者，用匕首裁开玛格丽特短衣的袖管。伤口在肘弯上方，伤势轻微，可当西蒙动手用布紧紧缠绕时，玛格丽特仍轻轻叫出了声。他毫不理会，系紧布条，又把斗篷往她身上拢好，把她整个掩藏起来。

“都妥了么，杰弗里？”

杰弗里此刻正吻着让娜，但他点了点头，一行人便轻快地小跑向前。他离了西蒙，低头望进那双正打量自己的大眼睛。

===Original===
“Art﻿—art thou﻿—angered with me, Geoffrey?” Jeanne asked him.

“No,” he said simply. “I could not be.”

The eyes grew rounder.

“I﻿—I thought thou wouldst be furious,” Jeanne said, just a little disappointed.

He shook his head.

===Chinese===
“你——你是不是——生我的气了，杰弗里？”让娜问他。

“没有，”他老实答道。“我气不起来。”

那双眼睛睁得更圆了。

“我——我还当你准要大发雷霆呢，”让娜说，语气里透着些许失望。

他摇了摇头。

===Original===
“Nay, but I will take good care ye play me not such a trick again, sweetheart.”

This was better. Jeanne sighed.

“But how wilt thou prevent me?” she asked.

“I will wed thee,” Geoffrey said. “Then shalt thou see that I am a stern husband.”

===Chinese===
“不会，可我会好好看住你，心肝儿，不许再跟我玩这等把戏。”

这样说话才像话。让娜舒了口气。

“可你拿什么拦我呢？”她问。

“我会娶你，”杰弗里说。“到那时你就明白我是个严苛的丈夫了。”

===Original===
Jeanne’s spirits were reviving fast. She dimpled.

“Thou wilt bear me, then, to the altar by force, sir.”

“If need be,” Geoffrey replied.

“Would﻿—would you really?” she asked in keenest admiration.

“I would.”

===Chinese===
让娜的兴致回得飞快。她绽出一对酒靥。

“这么说，你会硬把我架上圣坛了，先生。”

“若有必要，”杰弗里答道。

“你——你当真会？”她问，语调里透着十二分的钦佩。

“当真。”

===Original===
“Then I shall hate thee,” Jeanne said severely.

He laughed.

“And make thy life a misery with my shrewish ways.”

“Thou wilt be punished, then,” Geoffrey said.

===Chinese===
“那我可要恨死你了，”让娜板着脸说。

他大笑起来。

“还要拿我这份泼辣劲儿，把你的日子搅得苦不堪言。”

“那你就得受罚，”杰弗里说。

===Original===
“How?”

He kissed her.

“Thus.”

“It is very grievous,” she said. “I do not think I could bear it.”

“Then it is thy life which will be a misery,” Geoffrey told her.

===Chinese===
“怎么罚？”

他吻了她。

“就这么罚。”

“这刑罚好重，”她说。“我怕是受不了的。”

“那不得安宁的，可就是你这辈子了，”杰弗里告诉她。

===Original===
“In truth ye would make me your chattel,” she sighed. “It is very sad and ungallant. But English, no doubt! A barbarous race.”

“I will show thee how the English make love, sweet.”

“Oh, I can guess, sir. With a club. As Beauvallet will woo my mistress.”

“Beauvallet? Woo the Lady Margaret?” Geoffrey said incredulously. “Thy wits are wandering, Jeanne.”

“It is you that are just a great stupid man,” she replied scornfully. “I have seen it coming this many a day.”

===Chinese===
“真个的，你竟要拿我当你的私产，”她叹道。“真可悲，真不解风情。不过，英格兰人嘛，自然如此！一个野蛮的种族。”

“我要让你见识见识英格兰人怎样谈情说爱，甜心儿。”

“哦，我猜得到，先生。抡着大棒。就像博瓦莱（Beauvallet）要向我家夫人求婚那样。”

“博瓦莱？向玛格丽特夫人求婚？”杰弗里难以置信地说。“让娜，你的神志糊涂了。”

“你才是个十足的大蠢货，”她轻蔑地答道。“这事我眼看它要来，已不知多少时日了。”

===Original===
“But Simon doth not﻿—”

“If Simon loves not my lady, why did he slay Raoul?”

“I do not know. I﻿—”

“That is very true,” Jeanne said firmly, and closed her eyes.

===Chinese===
“可西蒙并不——”

“西蒙若不是爱我夫人，他为何要杀拉乌尔？”

“这我不知道。我——”

“这话千真万确，”让娜斩钉截铁地说，便合上了眼睛。

===Original===
They rode on in silence then, but at noon they halted at a tavern. Both ladies were asleep, so their bearers carried them into the parlour. They did not wake until dinner was served, and even then Margaret was too worn-out to eat. She drank a little wine, but relapsed almost at once into heavy slumber.

An hour later they set out again, and rode steadily onward, not drawing rein again until dusk, when the gates of Belrémy loomed large ahead. They went through, and along the street to the castle. Jeanne woke then and stretched herself.

“Where are we?” she asked drowsily.

Geoffrey dismounted, holding her against his shoulder.

“Home, dear heart. See!”

===Chinese===
之后他们便默默赶路，直到正午才在一家客店歇脚。两位女眷兀自睡着，抱她们的人便把她们径直抱进客堂。正餐端上来时两人才醒，即便这时，玛格丽特也已累得吃不下去。她呷了几口酒，随即又沉沉睡去。

一个钟头后他们重新上路，一路平稳疾驰，直到黄昏方才再度收缰，这时贝雷米（Belrémy）的城门已巍然高耸在望。他们穿门而过，沿街直抵城堡。让娜这时醒了，伸了个懒腰。

“咱们到哪儿了？”她睡意惺忪地问。

杰弗里翻身下马，把她揽在肩头。

“到家了，小心肝儿。瞧！”

===Original===
“Ah, how good!” she exclaimed. “Set me down, Geoffrey. I will not be carried.”

He put her on her feet, turning to Simon and holding out his arms.

“Let me take her, lad.”

“Nay.” Simon’s arm tightened about Margaret’s sleeping form. He dismounted carefully, and strode into the castle.

There were several people in the hall. Alan, the Chevalier, and a big man who sat back in the shadow. Hélène too was there, and she ran forward.

===Chinese===
“啊，多好呀！”她欢呼。“放我下来，杰弗里。我不要人抱着。”

他让她双脚落地，转身向西蒙伸出双臂。

“把她交给我吧，小子。”

“不必。”西蒙搂着玛格丽特睡影的手臂收得更紧。他小心翼翼地翻身下马，大步走进城堡。

大厅里有好几个人。艾伦（Alan）、弗勒里瓦尔骑士（the Chevalier），还有一个深坐在阴影里的高大汉子。埃莱娜（Hélène）也在，她抢步迎了上来。

===Original===
“Thou hast my lady?” she cried, and would have drawn back the folds of Simon’s cloak.

He warded her off.

“Ay.”

Alan hurried forward.

“Already! Both, lad? Ah, Geoffrey!”

===Chinese===
“夫人救出来了？”她喊道，伸手便要去掀西蒙斗篷的褶层。

他把她挡开了。

“嗯。”

艾伦急忙上前。

“这么快！两个都救回来了，老弟？啊，杰弗里！”

===Original===
The Chevalier minced forward.

“Milor’, set my cousin down. It is not fitting that you should carry her thus. Her ladies will attend to her.”

“Out of my way,” Simon said curtly, and brushed past him to the stairs.

Margaret woke, pushing aside the cloak, and looking about her. She was flushed from sleep, and drowsy still.

“Home! Hélène!” She glanced up into Simon’s rugged face, and her eyelids fluttered.

===Chinese===
弗勒里瓦尔骑士碎步趋前。

“英国老爷，把我表姐放下吧。您这般抱着她，于礼不合。她的女官们自会照料她。”

“让路，”西蒙冷冷地说，擦身而过，径直往楼梯走去。

玛格丽特醒了，拨开斗篷，四下张望。她睡得两颊绯红，犹带倦意。

“到家了！埃莱娜！”她抬眼望进西蒙粗犷的面孔，眼睫颤了颤。

===Original===
“If you please﻿—I will walk,” she said.

“I will carry thee to thy rooms,” Simon answered. “Lie still, madame.”

She remembered her boy’s clothing, and obeyed. Simon swung quickly up the stairs, Jeanne and Hélène at his heels.

A bevy of ladies swarmed about him, but he pushed by to the Countess’s chamber, laying her on the bed.

“Get her to bed,” he commanded. “One of you fetch the surgeon for her wound.” In his turn he was swept aside. The Lady Margaret’s ladies gathered about her, exclaiming and fondling. Simon went out, back to the great hall.

===Chinese===
“劳驾——我自己走，”她说。

“我要抱你回房去，”西蒙答道。“躺着别动，夫人。”

她想起自己一身男童装束，便依了他。西蒙疾步抢上楼梯，让娜与埃莱娜紧跟在他身后。

一大群女官蜂拥围住他，他推开众人，一直走进伯爵夫人的寝房，把她放到床上。

“扶她上床，”他吩咐道。“你们之中哪一个，去请外科医士来治她的伤。”这一回却轮到他被人推到了一旁。玛格丽特夫人的女官们围拢在她身边，惊呼着爱抚不迭。西蒙走了出去，回到大厅。

===Original===
A bluff voice smote his ears.

“Now by the Rood, is that my Simon? God’s Body, what doth he with a maid in his arms? Ha, Simon, thou rogue! Come hither!” Fulk limped forward, hands outstretched.

“My lord!” Simon strode to meet him, and gripped his hands. “My dear lord!”

Fulk embraced him.

===Chinese===
一个粗豪的嗓音直撞进他的耳朵。

“哦，凭圣架（the Rood）起誓，那不是我的西蒙吗？上帝圣体，他怀里抱着个姑娘作甚？哈，西蒙，你这无赖！过来！”富尔克（Fulk）一瘸一拐地迎上前来，双手大张。

“大人！”西蒙大步迎上去，紧握住他的双手。“我亲爱的大人！”

富尔克拥抱了他。

===Original===
“My Simon﻿—my lion-cub! I could not stay away. Fiend seize thee, thou hast grown again, or else I had forgotten thy great height. What a-God’s Name do ye in all this golden armour? Thou popinjay! My lad, my lad, kneel not to me!” For Simon had dropped on his knee. Fulk pulled him up. “Give me thy hand again! I have heard of thy prowess, lion-cub. And thou wert once my pert squire! Glory, glory, I never thought I should live to be proud of thee!” He held Simon at arm’s length, gazing at him. “Ay, ay, the same beetle-brows, and the same cold eyes. Turn to the light, silly boy! Now, by my troth, I do see a difference!”

Alan came to Simon’s side.

“Wert thou surprised? My lord did come yesterday, straight from the King.”

“I thought I dreamed,” Simon said. “How came ye to these shores, my lord?”

===Chinese===
“我的西蒙——我的小狮子！我实在待不住了。恶鬼抓了你去，你这是又长个儿了，还是我把你这一身高个头给忘了？看在上帝份上，你穿这一身金甲是作什么？花孔雀！我的孩子，我的孩子，别向我下跪！”原来西蒙已经单膝跪倒。富尔克把他拽了起来。“再把手给我！你的威名我听说了，小狮子。你从前还是我手下那个神气活现的小扈从呢！荣耀啊，荣耀，我从没想到活着还能亲见为你骄傲的这一天！”他把手臂伸直，捧着西蒙，凝神打量。“嗯，嗯，还是那两道浓眉，还是那双冷眼睛。往亮处转一转，傻孩子！嗯，千真万确，我真瞧出了不一样！”

艾伦走到西蒙身边。

“你吃了一惊吧？大人昨天就到了，径直打国王那儿来。”

“我还当自己在做梦呢，”西蒙说。“大人是怎么到这处地方来的？”

===Original===
“Faith, in a boat, lad. There was I at home, fretting for news of ye both, which came not, and could bear it no longer. Since my lady died and my daughters are both wed, I must e’en be near one or other of you. So off went I to London to my cousin Granmere. Then went I to the King, and he sent me here. And since yesterday I have heard of naught save thy prowess, and how thou didst capture this place. Simon, Simon, it was well done! Would that I had been with thee, lad, but I am old and this accursed gout﻿—well, well! What hath come to mine Alan? He left Montlice a silly boy, sighing and singing for his ladyloves, and here I find him a man at last, which I never thought to see him. Hast made a soldier of him, lad?”

Simon led him to a chair.

“Nay. King Hal calls him his poet, but he can lead an attack better than Geoffrey here, if he has a mind to it.”

Fulk turned to look at Malvallet, who stood apart, watching them. Up he struggled once more and stumped forward.

===Chinese===
“说真格的，坐船来的，小子。我在家里为你们两个的消息发愁，偏偏音信不至，实在熬不下去了。自我夫人过世、两个女儿又都出阁，我就非得挨着你们二人中的一个住不可。于是我便动身去了伦敦，投我表亲格雷曼（Granmere）。随后我又去见国王，是他打发我来的此地。打昨天起，我耳朵里听来听去全是你的威名，还有你怎样夺下这座城。西蒙，西蒙，干得漂亮！但愿我当时与你同在，小子，可我老了，又有这该诅咒的痛风——唉，罢了，罢了！我的艾伦这是怎么了？他离开蒙利斯（Montlice）时还是个傻小子，为一个个心上人长吁短叹、唱歌寄情，这回我却见他到底成了个男子汉，这我原不敢指望。是你把他调教成了军人吗，小子？”

西蒙扶他在一张椅子上坐下。

“不是我。哈尔王（King Hal）唤他做自己的诗人，可只要他起了意，他带队冲锋比这儿的杰弗里还强。”

富尔克转身去看马尔瓦莱（Malvallet），他正独自立在一边望着他们。老人又挣扎着起身，跺着脚走了过去。

===Original===
“Needs must I take thy hand, Sir Geoffrey,” he mumbled. “If thou wilt have it so. This is wartime, and there is no room for enmity between us two.”

Geoffrey bent the knee gracefully.

“I am only too well pleased to have it so, my lord,” he said. “For Simon, Alan and I are one.”

So they clasped hands, and Fulk sat down again with all three about him. The Chevalier had minced away some time ago, and Santoy had taken the wounded and very much shaken Ranaud to find a surgeon, so that they were alone. Fulk blew out his cheeks, looking proudly from Alan to Simon, and smiled a little at the glory of Simon’s armour.

===Chinese===
“我非得握一握你的手不可，杰弗里爵士，”他喃喃道。“只要你肯。眼下是战时，你我两家容不下仇怨。”

杰弗里优雅地屈下一膝。

“能这样，我高兴还来不及，大人，”他说。“为了西蒙和艾伦，我与你是同心的一体。”

于是两人执手相握，富尔克重新坐下，三人围在他身边。弗勒里瓦尔骑士早些时已碎步离去，桑托伊则带走了负伤而又惊魂未定的拉诺去寻外科医士，厅中便只剩了他们。富尔克鼓起腮帮，目光自豪地从艾伦移到西蒙，望着西蒙那一身光华夺目的铠甲，微微而笑。

===Original===
“Well, I had heard of thy gilded armour, lad, but never till now have I seen thee in it. Thou coxcomb! And tell me, lion-cub, who was the lady whom ye bore in your arms?”

Simon rose, and glanced from one to the other of them. For a moment he was silent, and then the glimmering of a smile came into his green-blue eyes.

“That, my lord, is the lady whom I will one day take to wife,” he said deliberately.

===Chinese===
“喂，你这鎏金铠甲我早有耳闻，小子，可直到今日才头一回见你穿上它。你这纨绔子！告诉我，小狮子，方才你抱在怀里的那位夫人是谁？”

西蒙站起身，目光在两人脸上逐一掠过。他静默了片刻，随即一缕微光般的笑意浮上他那双蓝绿的眼睛。

“大人，那位夫人，便是有朝一日我要娶作妻子的，”他一字一顿地说。

## XIV / 第十四章


===Original===
How He Received the Lady Margaret’s Submission

My Lord of Montlice hobbled out on to the terrace. It was the day after Simon’s return, and he was still in a state of incoherent surprise over the amazing announcement that Simon had made the night before. At the time, he had stared open-mouthed, and before he had in the smallest degree recovered from the first shock of surprise, Simon had gone. All the evening he had been busy, so there had been no more private conversation. This morning he was closeted with his secretary, so Fulk wandered out in search of his son, whom he found on the terrace, fitting a new string to his harp. Alan smiled when he saw his father, and his smile was very sweet, as always.

Fulk lowered himself on to the chair that Alan vacated.

“Fiend seize my foot!” he growled, and glared at it.

Alan sat down on the parapet, a gay figure against the dull stone. His father grunted.

“Still harping, silly lad?”

===Chinese===
他如何赢得玛格丽特夫人的降服

蒙利斯（Montlice）老爷一瘸一拐地踱上露台。这是西蒙（Simon）回来的第二天，头天晚上西蒙那句令人咋舌的宣告，仍叫他惊愕得语无伦次。当时他张口结舌地愣在当场，还没从最初一惊中缓过半分，西蒙已经走开了。整个晚上西蒙都忙得脱不开身，父子俩便没能再作私谈。这天早上西蒙又同秘书闭门议事，富尔克（Fulk）便踱出门去寻儿子，在露台上找到了他——他正往竖琴上装一根新弦。艾伦（Alan）一见父亲便笑了，那笑容一如既往地甜美。

富尔克在艾伦腾出的椅子上坐下。

“恶鬼捉了这只脚！”他咆哮着，狠狠瞪它一眼。

艾伦在矮护墙上坐了下来，一身明快的颜色，衬着黯淡的石头。他父亲哼了一声。

“还在弹琴哪，傻小子？”

===Original===
“Still,” Alan answered.

“Hast naught better to do?”

“Simon would tell thee ay. But Simon hath no ear for music.”

“He tells me that thou art Master of his Horse.”

Alan laughed.

“It is true, alack.”

===Chinese===
“还在弹，”艾伦答道。

“就没有旁的事好做？”

“西蒙准会说你没有说错。不过西蒙没有听音乐的耳朵。”

“他告诉我，你如今做他的骁骑长了。”

艾伦笑了。

“是真的，唉。”

===Original===
“He saith that thou art a good master, but I doubt he seeks to flatter thee to me.”

“I think he doth,” Alan said, and smiled again.

“Alan!” Fulk drove his stick on to the ground. “What meant the lad last night?”

Alan glanced up through his lashes.

“Last night?”

Fulk roared at him.

===Chinese===
“他说你是个好骁骑长，只怕他是当着我的面奉承你。”

“我看正是，”艾伦说着，又笑了。

“艾伦！”富尔克把手杖往地上一顿。“那小子昨晚那话，是什么意思？”

艾伦抬起眼睛，从睫毛底下朝上瞟了一眼。

“昨晚？”

富尔克冲他咆哮。

===Original===
“Thou foolish boy! When he said that he would take the Lady Margaret to wife!”

“Well, sir﻿—” Alan twanged his harp meditatively﻿—“He is Simon of Beauvallet, so I suppose he did mean﻿—just that.”

“But thou didst tell me that the Lady Margaret hated him, and sought to slay him!” exploded Fulk.

“Ay, my lord.”

“Then what maggot hath Simon in his silly head?”

Alan drew his hand across the strings so that they sang softly under his fingers.

===Chinese===
“你这傻孩子！就是他说要娶玛格丽特夫人（the Lady Margaret）为妻那句！”

“这个嘛，父亲——”艾伦若有所思地拨响琴弦——“他是博瓦莱（Beauvallet）的西蒙，所以我想，他的意思——就是这个意思。”

“可你不是对我说过，玛格丽特夫人恨他入骨，还要取他性命！”富尔克炸了。

“是啊，父亲大人。”

“那西蒙那颗傻脑袋里，钻的是什么怪念头？”

艾伦的手在弦上一拂，琴弦便在指下轻轻吟唱起来。

===Original===
“The maggot of love, my father.”

“Love for a froward woman? Much have I heard of the lady on the way hither, and it seems to me that she is a bold, spiteful hussy.”

“Bold, sir, and tigerish, but so is Simon. No milk-and-water maid could touch his heart. He must take a fitting mate unto himself. The Countess is such an one, and not soft speech will move her, but rugged strength, and maybe rough usage.”

Fulk stared at him.

“Art very wise. I would fain meet this lady.”

“Oh, sir, she will send thee from her side with a flea in thine ear.”

===Chinese===
“是爱情的怪念头，父亲。”

“爱上一个悖逆任性的女人？来这一路上，这位夫人的事我听得多了，照我听来，她是个胆大妄为、心肠歹毒的泼妇。”

“胆大，父亲，而且凶悍如虎，可西蒙也正是这路人。柔弱似水的姑娘打动不了他的心，他得给自己觅一个相当的配偶。伯爵夫人正是这样的人：能叫她动心的不是软语温存，而是粗粝的膂力，说不定还得添上几分粗暴。”

富尔克瞪着他。

“你见识不小。我很想见见这位夫人。”

“哦，父亲，她会把你抢白一顿，从她身边撵走的。”

===Original===
“Oho!” said Fulk. “I am an old man.” He stroked his grey hair ruefully.

Alan touched his hand affectionately.

“Yet the old man did come to France and is none the worse for his tedious journey,” he said.

Fulk puffed, pleased at the compliment.

“Oh, there is life in the lion yet!” he nodded. “Who comes?”

Alan sprang up, for Jeanne was limping towards them.

===Chinese===
“哦嗬！”富尔克说，“我可是个老人啦。”他懊丧地捋着花白的头发。

艾伦亲热地抚了抚他的手。

“可这位老人到底还是到了法兰西，一路劳顿，也不曾亏了分毫。”他说。

富尔克哼哼直乐，受用这份恭维。

“哦，这头狮子还有的是活气呢！”他点点头。“谁来了？”

艾伦一跃而起，原来是让娜（Jeanne）一瘸一拐地朝他们走来。

===Original===
“It is Mademoiselle Jeanne, who is soon to be Geoffrey’s lady,” he said, and kissed Jeanne’s hand. “Mademoiselle, ye see here my father, Lord Fulk of Montlice.”

Jeanne curtseyed in response to Fulk’s bow, and went to sit beside him on the bench.

“Is it the gout?” Fulk asked, interested, pointing to her feet.

Jeanne dimpled charmingly.

“Nay, milor’, it is﻿—oh, it is blisters!”

“Ay, ay! Art a brave lass, I do hear.”

===Chinese===
“是让娜小姐，眼看就要做杰弗里（Geoffrey）夫人的人。”他说着，吻了吻让娜的手。“小姐，这位是我父亲，蒙利斯的富尔克老爷。”

让娜屈膝还了富尔克一礼，挨着他在长凳上坐下。

“是痛风么？”富尔克来了兴致，指着她的脚问。

让娜迷人地绽出两个笑靥。

“不是的，英国老爷，这是——哦，是脚上磨的水泡！”

“哦，哦！听说你是个有胆量的姑娘。”

===Original===
“Not I, sir. ’Tis Margot who is brave.”

“Mademoiselle,” Alan interrupted, “what chanced in Raoul’s palace? Simon says naught, and I have had no word with Geoffrey. Is it true that Simon slew Raoul?”

Jeanne closed her eyes.

“It was terrible,” she said. “Raoul﻿—Raoul had Margot in his arms. He﻿—he kissed her, and she fought him. Then﻿—then, when I thought all was lost, there came the clank of armour, and Lord Simon stood in the doorway with Geoffrey beside him. Oh, sir, I thought mine eyes deceived me! So great they looked, the one all black and grey, and the other gold and green! Raoul pushed my lady away, but he was too late.” Jeanne threw out her hand dramatically. “I saw my Lord of Beauvallet grow stiff all at once, and there came a light into his eyes such as I have never seen before. He smiled, and indeed, indeed, that smile drove terror into my heart. Just one moment he stood there, while I wondered what he would be at. And then he seemed to leap forward! In a second he was by us, and had seized up Raoul in his arms. He bent him over his knee, backwards, until methought Raoul’s spine would snap. And he said”﻿—Jeanne tried to imitate Simon’s snarl﻿—“ ‘*Die*, thou dog!’ Then he stabbed suddenly, and the blood spurted up! It was horrible, horrible! After that it is all﻿—a mist. They fought, all of them, even Margot, but they could not hope to conquer, so we fled through a door behind us, and ran, and ran, and ran! And at last we found a stairway, which led out of the castle. Raoul’s men were hard on our heels, but we ran across a courtyard, and Ranaud wrenched the gate open. Then found we the horses, and fled for our lives.”

===Chinese===
“我可谈不上，先生。有胆量的是玛戈（Margot）。”

“小姐，”艾伦插言道，“拉乌尔（Raoul）的宫邸里到底出了什么事？西蒙什么也不肯说，我又没顾上同杰弗里说上话。西蒙杀了拉乌尔，是真的么？”

让娜阖上了眼睛。

“那真可怕，”她说。“拉乌尔——拉乌尔把玛戈搂在怀里。他——他吻她，她拼死挣扎。后来——后来，就在我以为全完了的时候，传来铠甲的铿锵之声，西蒙大人立在门口，杰弗里就在他身旁。哦，先生，我还当自己的眼睛骗了我！他们显得那么高大，一个通身黑灰，一个金绿交映！拉乌尔把我家夫人推开，可是已经迟了。”让娜猛地一挥手，煞有介事。“我看见博瓦莱大人浑身骤然一僵，他眼里亮起一道光，那样的光我从来不曾见过。他笑了——说真的，说真的，那一笑叫我心里直冒寒气。他在那里立了不过一霎，我还纳闷他要干什么。跟着，他像是猛扑出去！一眨眼他已到了我们跟前，一把将拉乌尔整个擒在臂中。他把拉乌尔反扳过膝头，扳得我只道拉乌尔的脊梁骨就要断了。他喝道”——让娜竭力学着西蒙的低吼——“‘*去死*吧，你这条狗！’跟着猛然一剑刺下，血花迸溅！那太可怕了，太可怕了！再往后就全是一片——一片迷雾。他们厮打起来，人人都在打，连玛戈也动了手，可断没有取胜之望，于是我们从身后的一扇门里逃出去，跑啊，跑啊，跑啊！最后寻到一道楼梯，直通城堡之外。拉乌尔的人紧紧追在脚后，我们跑过一座庭院，拉诺（Ranaud）一把拽开大门。随后寻着了马，就没命地逃出来了。”

===Original===
“Simon ran way?” Fulk asked incredulously.

“What else could he do? I think﻿—he lost his head. He meant not to kill Raoul, but when he saw my lady in his arms, he forgot caution, and only thought of vengeance.”

“That is not like Simon!” Fulk said.

“It is like the new Simon,” Alan answered.

===Chinese===
“西蒙逃跑了？”富尔克难以置信地问。

“不逃又能怎样？我想——他一时失了方寸。他本没打算杀拉乌尔，可一看见我家夫人在那人怀抱里，他便把谨慎忘了个干净，心里只剩下报仇二字。”

“这可不像西蒙！”富尔克说。

“这正像如今这个新的西蒙，”艾伦答道。

---

===Original===
To Simon came a French page, bowing low.

“Milor’, I bear a message from madame.”

“What is it?”

“Madame requests milor’ to visit her. She hath that which she would say to milor’.”

Simon rose.

“Lead me to madame.”

===Chinese===
一个法兰西侍童（page）深深躬着身，来到西蒙跟前。

“英国老爷，夫人有话命小人捎给您。”

“什么事？”

“夫人请英国老爷过去一叙。夫人有话要对英国老爷说。”

西蒙起身。

“领我到夫人那儿去。”

===Original===
The page conducted him to Margaret’s rooms, and announced him.

The Countess was alone, standing by the window. She was clad in a long red robe, and she wore a horned headdress upon her head. She came forward a few steps, to meet Simon, and he saw that her hands were tightly clenched.

“Well, madame?”

Margaret moistened her lips. She began to speak jerkily, her eyes dark and troubled.

“Milor’, there is much I must say to you. Ye have﻿—placed me in your﻿—debt.” Her eyelids dropped a little, and the proud lips quivered.

Simon said nothing, watching her.

===Chinese===
侍童把他领到玛格丽特的居所，进去通传。

伯爵夫人独自一人，立在窗前。她穿一袭曳地的红袍，头上戴着一顶角式头饰。她迎着西蒙上前几步，他看见她的双手紧紧攥着。

“怎么，夫人？”

玛格丽特舔了舔嘴唇，一开口便断断续续，两眼乌沉沉的，尽是纷乱。

“英国老爷，我有许多话非对您说不可。您——叫我欠下了——您的恩义。”她的眼帘微微垂落，高傲的嘴唇颤了一颤。

西蒙一言不发，只是望着她。

===Original===
“I have first﻿—to thank you﻿—for﻿—what you did﻿—yesterday.” The words stuck in her throat a little, but she went on bravely. “Had ye not come﻿—to my rescue﻿—I had been﻿—what I will not think﻿—today.” Her eyes searched his face, but it was impassive. Simon’s arms were folded across his great chest, and he stood very still before her. Again she moistened her lips. “Margaret of Belrémy﻿—leaves not﻿—her debts﻿—unpaid. Had I not﻿—fallen into Raoul’s clutches﻿—I would have﻿—brought﻿—an army to Belrémy﻿—to fight you. But﻿—I failed, and﻿—you﻿—rescued me. I﻿—I desire now﻿—to wipe away﻿—the debt I owe you. So﻿—so﻿—I will﻿—make my submission to you.” Her voice had sunk, but it vibrated with her pride.

“I want more than that.”

She started, clasping her hands nervously together.

“You﻿—you seek﻿—my life?” she asked, and squared her shoulders.

===Chinese===
“我首先——要谢谢您——为了——您昨天——的所作所为。”这话在喉头卡了一卡，她还是勇敢地说了下去。“若不是您——赶来相救——我今天早已落得——我不忍去想的——下场。”她的目光在他脸上搜寻，那张脸却纹丝不动。西蒙双臂环抱在宽阔的胸前，一动不动地立在她面前。她又舔了舔嘴唇。“贝雷米（Belrémy）的玛格丽特——欠下的债——从没有——不还的。倘若我没有——落进拉乌尔的魔掌——我本会——带一支大军到贝雷米城下——同你一决胜负。可是——我败了，而您——救了我。我——如今要——清偿我欠您的债。所以——所以——我愿——向您降服。”她的声音低了下去，字字却迸着她的傲气。

“我要的还不止这个。”

她一惊，双手慌乱地绞在一起。

“你——你要——我的性命？”她问着，把双肩一挺。

===Original===
Simon came heavily up to her, and took her wrists in his hold.

“Thy life, ay. All of thee.” Suddenly he bent forward, and kissed her, full on her red lips.

She sprang away, trembling and shaken, pressing her hands to her hot cheeks.

“You﻿—oh, you insult me! I have not deserved﻿—that! My God, I had﻿—I had come to think you﻿—a man of honour!”

“I insult thee not,” Simon said calmly. “I want thy hand in marriage.”

She stared at him, hardly comprehending. Then she recoiled eyes aflame.

===Chinese===
西蒙沉沉地走到她跟前，一把攥住她的双腕。

“你的性命，不错。还有你整个人。”他骤然俯下身去，正正地吻上她殷红的唇。

她一跃退开，浑身战栗，六神无主，两手死死捂着滚烫的双颊。

“你——哦，你这是侮辱我！我不该受——这个！天老爷，我竟——我竟把你当作——一个讲究荣誉的人！”

“我没有侮辱你，”西蒙平静地说。“我要你嫁给我。”

她瞪着他，半天没有明白过来。随即她倒退一步，两眼冒火。

===Original===
“You﻿—you﻿—For what do ye take me? Think ye I would wed﻿—an English boor?” She spat the words at him, and her bosom heaved.

“I think that thou wilt wed me, madame. What I want, I take.”

“Ye take not me! Mordieu, are ye mad? Wed me? I﻿—I am Margaret of Belrémy!”

“Thou art my prisoner.”

“No longer!” She stepped quickly up to him, her silken skirts brushing the ground. “I have made my submission!”

He looked down at her for a moment, in silence; then he drew a folded parchment from his belt, and spread it upon the table.

===Chinese===
“你——你——你把我当什么人了？你以为我肯嫁——一个英国莽夫？”她把这些字一个一个朝他啐过去，胸口剧烈起伏。

“我认为你会嫁我，夫人。我要什么，便取什么。”

“你取不走我！圣母在上，你疯了不成？娶我？我——我是贝雷米的玛格丽特！”

“你是我的俘虏。”

“再不是了！”她疾步抢到他面前，丝裙拂过地面。“我已经递了降服！”

他默然俯视她片刻；随后从腰带里抽出一卷折起的羊皮文书，在桌上摊开。

===Original===
“It awaits thy signature, madame. Thy submission to my master.”

Slowly she approached the table, and read the formal words. A little shiver ran through her, and she bit her lip. She sat down, and picked up her quill. For a long time she sat very still, but presently she dipped the quill in the ink, and quickly signed her name. She would have risen then, but Simon’s hand was on her shoulder.

“There lies thy submission to the King my master,” he said and she saw that his eyes gleamed. “But thy submission to me must come soon. Thy life is mine by right of conquest, and well dost thou know it. Willingly shalt thou come to me, and willingly give thy heart. For I will have all or nothing.”

“Nothing, then!” she said hoarsely.

He smiled, and picked up the parchment.

“ ‘I have not, but still I hold,’ ” he said, and laughed, swung round on his heel, and went out.

===Chinese===
“只欠你的签押了，夫人。你向我主上的降书。”

她慢慢走到桌前，读那几行程式的词句。一阵轻微的战栗掠过全身，她咬住了嘴唇。她坐下来，拿起羽笔。许久许久，她一动不动地坐着，终于还是把羽笔探进墨水瓶，飞快签下了名字。她正待起身，西蒙的手已按在她肩头。

“你向我主上国王的降书，就在这儿了。”他说。她看见他两眼闪闪发光。“可你向我本人降服，也为期不远了。凭征服之权，你的性命已归了我，这你心里有数。你会心甘情愿地到我身边来，心甘情愿把心交给我。因为我非全得，即全舍。”

“那就一样都不要！”她哑声说。

他微微一笑，拿起羊皮文书。

“‘我一无所有，却依然持守。’”他说罢纵声大笑，脚跟一旋，扬长而去。

===Original===
Margaret stumbled up, trying to control the wild leaping of her pulses. To her came Jeanne, and cast her a shrewd glance.

“Jeanne!” Margaret cried. “*He* has been here! He﻿—he kissed me. Oh, how I hate him!” Raging, she paced the floor, lashing herself to a fury.

“I have heard that hate is akin to love,” Jeanne remarked placidly.

“Love! I love that﻿—that﻿—” she choked for words. “He thinks to wed me! He! Ah, how I hate him!”

“Thou didst not hate him when he killed Raoul,” Jeanne said.

Margaret paused, staring at her, wild-eyed.

===Chinese===
玛格丽特踉跄起身，竭力按捺狂奔乱跳的脉搏。让娜走了过来，锐利地瞥她一眼。

“让娜！”玛格丽特喊道。“*他*方才来过！他——他吻了我。哦，我多恨他！”她怒不可遏，在屋里踱来踱去，把自己越激越愤。

“听人说，恨与爱原是近亲，”让娜淡淡地说。

“爱！我爱那个——那个——”她气得找不出话来。“他妄想娶我！他！啊，我多恨他！”

“他杀拉乌尔的时候，你并没有恨他，”让娜说。

玛格丽特顿住脚，眼睛瞪得老大，直勾勾望着她。

===Original===
“Did I not? Did I not? Oh, what ails me, Jeanne?” She sank down upon the floor beside her lady, sobbing.

“Pride dies hard,” Jeanne said softly. “Thou art torn between love and hate.”

“No, no! It is all hate, all hate!”

“Then why dost thou weep?”

“I﻿—I do not know﻿—I am distraught. It was his kiss, burning me! Shaming me! Ah, let me go!” She sprang up and away, rushing from the room straight into the arms of her cousin.

===Chinese===
“我没有么？我没有么？哦，我这是怎么了，让娜？”让娜挨着夫人在地板上坐下，低声啜泣。

“骄傲这东西，不到咽气不肯断，”让娜柔声说。“你在爱与恨之间，两头受着撕扯。”

“不，不！全是恨，全是恨！”

“那你为什么哭？”

“我——我不知道——我六神无主。是他的那个吻，烧着我！羞辱我！啊，放开我！”她一跃而起，夺门而出，一头撞进她表弟的怀里。

===Original===
“Victor! You? What﻿—do ye here?”

He twirled his scented kerchief, eyes running swiftly over her.

“I came to wait upon thee, sweet Margot, but yon yellow-haired Saxon was before me. Thou art strangely disordered, cousin.” He bent forward, scrutinising her. “Now what hath he done, I wonder?”

“Out, out of my way!” she cried, and swept past, down the corridor.

The Chevalier entered her room. Jeanne looked coldly at him, but he smiled.

“So the English oaf kissed my cousin?” he said gently, and showed his teeth a moment.

===Chinese===
“维克托（Victor）！是你？你——怎么会在这儿？”

他捻着熏了香的手帕，目光在她身上飞快地溜了一遭。

“我是来伺候你的，亲爱的玛戈，无奈那位黄头发的撒克逊人抢在了我的前头。表姐，你神色好生纷乱。”他俯下身来，细细端详她。“不知他对你做了些什么？”

“闪开，别拦我的路！”她喝道，拂袖而过，径往走廊那头去了。

骑士走进她的房间。让娜冷冷地望着他，他却面带微笑。

“原来那英国蠢货吻了我表姐？”他柔声说着，龇了龇牙。

===Original===
“Ye would appear to be in his confidence,” Jeanne snapped.

He paid no heed.

“And she is all distraught. What does that betoken?﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
“看来你倒是他的心腹呢，”让娜抢白道。

他毫不理会。

“而她又是这般心慌意乱。这说明了什么？……”

## XV / 第十五章

===Original===
How He Came Upon the Lady Margaret in the Gallery

===Chinese===
他如何在回廊撞见玛格丽特夫人


===Original===
On a voyage of exploration through the castle, Fulk came to a wide gallery where the musicians were wont to play. Coming towards him, away from her rooms, was the Lady Margaret, tall and stately as ever in a cloth of gold, with her long hair braided, and a gold band about her forehead from which glowed a single sapphire stone. She paused when she saw Fulk, and looked him over, for he was a stranger to her.

Fulk looked back at her squarely, leaning on his stout ash-plant. The Lady Margaret would have passed on, chin lifted, but he blocked her passage.

“Know ye the way back to the hall, madame?” he asked, in very fair French. “I have lost my path.”

===Chinese===
富尔克（Fulk）在城堡里信步探幽，走到一条宽阔的回廊上——乐师们素来在此奏乐。迎面而来、正离了她房间的，是玛格丽特夫人（the Lady Margaret），身量高挑、仪态雍容一如往昔，一身金线织锦，长发编作辫子，额间束一条金带，正中一颗蓝宝石熠熠生辉。她一见富尔克便驻了步，把他上下打量一番，因为她并不认得这位生客。

富尔克拄着粗实的白蜡木手杖，坦坦荡荡地回望她。玛格丽特夫人本要扬起下巴径直走过去，却被他拦住了去路。

“夫人可知道回大厅的路？”他开口问道，法语说得相当漂亮。“我迷了路了。”


===Original===
“The stairs are yonder, sir,” she said, pointing.

Fulk sighed, and thought that he would be very cunning.

“Stairs, stairs, stairs! If there were chairs I should like it better. I have had the gout this many a day, lady, and it plagues me sorely.”

The Countess hesitated, but Fulk’s white hairs made her courteous.

===Chinese===
“楼梯在那边，先生。”她抬手一指。

富尔克叹了口气，暗自盘算要来一手妙的。

“楼梯，楼梯，楼梯！要是换成椅子，我倒更乐意。夫人，我这痛风害了不是一天两天了，痛得我好苦。”

伯爵夫人迟疑了一下，可富尔克满头白发叫她不好失礼。


===Original===
“There are chairs behind you, sir,” she said.

“Why then, madame, if you will be seated, so will I,” he answered.

“I thank you, no.” On swept my lady, but was arrested by Fulk’s roar. He could never be patient for long.

“Come back, come back! God’s Body, have I not been lonely enough? Come hither, whoever ye may be, and bear an old man company.”

The Lady Margaret spoke coldly.

===Chinese===
“椅子就在您背后，先生。”她说。

“那么好，夫人，您若肯坐，我也坐。”他答道。

“多谢，不必了。”夫人款款前行，却被富尔克一声吼喝钉在原地。他的耐心从来长不了。

“回来，回来！上帝的圣体，我孤零零的还不够么？管你是谁，过来陪陪老人家。”

玛格丽特夫人冷冷地开口。


===Original===
“I am the Countess of Belrémy,” she said, and her tone should have crushed him.

“What care I for that?” he demanded. “If you wish to sing titles, I am the Earl of Montlice. Now sit ye down, a-God’s sake!”

Margaret was somewhat taken aback.

“I﻿—I do not know the Earl of Montlice, sir.”

“That do ye. Sit thee down, I say!”

===Chinese===
“我是贝雷米女伯爵（Countess of Belrémy）。”她说，这口吻本该把他压下去。

“那与我何干？”他喝道。“夫人要比头衔，我是蒙利斯（Montlice）伯爵。行了，看上帝份上，坐下吧！”

玛格丽特不由一怔。

“我——我不认识什么蒙利斯伯爵，先生。”

“你认识的。坐下，我说了，坐下！”


===Original===
Margaret was inclined to be haughty, but when Fulk stamped his foot and swore at the pain, she laughed, and came to him, sitting down.

“I do not know why ye should desire to keep me with you,” she said frankly. “I have no love for Englishmen.”

Fulk lowered himself beside her.

“Now what hath been done to thee by an Englishman?” quoth he.

She flushed.

===Chinese===
玛格丽特原想端起架子，可富尔克一跺脚、疼得咒骂出声，她忍不住笑了，走到他跟前坐下。

“我不明白您为何定要留我在此。”她坦率地说。“我对英国人可没什么好感。”

富尔克在她身边缓缓落座。

“那英国人待你怎么了？”他问道。

她涨红了脸。


===Original===
“Ye call it nothing that my land hath been ta’en by an Englishman?” she cried.

“Fortune o’ war,” he grunted. “Thou hadst a worthy foe.”

“Sir?”

“Why I do hear that my boy Simon and thou do tilt at one another. Now Simon is a man, God wot!”

“Indeed, sir?”

===Chinese===
“我的国土叫一个英国人夺了去，这在你眼里竟算不得什么？”她嚷道。

“兵戈常事罢了。”他哼了一声。“你的对头也不算辱没你。”

“先生？”

“咳，我听说我家小子西蒙（Simon）正同你针锋相对呢。西蒙可是条汉子，天知道！”

“是么，先生？”


===Original===
“Did ye doubt it?” Fulk slewed round to face her. “A plague be on the lad, what hath he done? He was ever a pert, headstrong child, but I never heard that he did more harm to a maid than turn his back on her.”

“Oh, he is very chivalrous!” she sneered. “See this scar on my breast! That did he with his sword!”

Fulk looked at it.

“Did he so? Wherefore should he do that?”

“Because I would not yield thy son to him, nor my castle!”

===Chinese===
“你还有疑？”富尔克扭转身子正对着她。“这遭瘟的小子，他干了什么？他打小就是个没大没小、天不怕地不怕的孩子，可我从没听说他伤过哪位姑娘——顶多是对人家掉头不顾罢了。”

“哦，他可真骑士风度！”她讥诮道。“瞧瞧我胸口这道疤！就是他用剑留下的！”

富尔克瞧了瞧那道疤。

“他竟下这样的手？所为何来？”

“因为我既不肯把你的儿子交给他，也不肯交出我的城堡！”


===Original===
“Ah, well!” Fulk puffed out his cheeks. “Alan is dear to him. As for thy castle﻿—what he had sworn to take he would take, willy-nilly. It is his way. Lord, Lord, I should know, for had I not to bear with him four long years? The lad was my squire, lady.”

“Thy squire?” She was surprised, in spite of herself. “How could he be that?”

“Why, look ye, he was Malvallet’s bastard, and Malvallet was my foe. When Simon’s mother died he came to me, and bearded me in my lair.” He chuckled. “I was a fierce fellow in those days, lady, but Holy Virgin, he was as fierce! A square-set whelp, some fourteen years old, and forced himself into my service. A pretty time I had with him, madame, and an obstinate, impudent cub he was. Many’s the beating I’ve given him, but do ye think he cared? Not he! He’d e’en go his own road, say or do what I would. Cold as a stone, as strong as I was myself. Up he grew, like a young tree. The shoulders of him! He hath a blow which would fell an ox, lady, and the coolest brain ever I knew. Now hark while I tell thee how he came by his land.” Fulk settled himself more comfortably, and proceeded to recount the exploits of his beloved lion-cub. Margaret listened, eyes downcast, but once she raised them, and they were sparkling with sympathy for one of Simon’s deeds. But at the end of the recital the colour died out of her cheeks, and she remembered that Simon was her enemy.

“Ye would seem to have a fondness for this Simon, milor’.”

“Needs must I,” Fulk grunted. “I do indeed love the boy. He cares for me a little, but he hath never asked a favour of me, and never will. What he wants, he will win himself. Never was there a prouder, more cocksure lad!”

===Chinese===
“唉，是这样！”富尔克鼓起腮帮。“艾伦（Alan）在他心里金贵着呢。至于你的城堡——他起过誓要拿的东西，就一定会拿到手，谁也拦不住。他就是这个脾性。老天，老天，我最有数了，漫漫整整四年，我不都这么受过来的么？夫人，那小子做过我的扈从。”

“你的扈从？”她不由自主地吃了一惊。“他怎么会做了你的扈从？”

“咳，你听我说，他是马尔瓦莱（Malvallet）的私生子，而马尔瓦莱正是我的对头。西蒙的娘一死，他便来寻我，直闯进我的老巢，当面捋我的胡须。”他呵呵一笑。“夫人，我那些年是个狠角色，可是圣母在上，这小子比我还狠！一个方肩正背的小兽崽子，年方十四，硬是钻进我家门下效力。我同他过的那段日子可真够‘美妙’的，夫人，又犟又没规矩的一个野崽子。我揍过他不知多少顿，可你以为他在乎么？他才不在乎！任我说什么做什么，他照旧走他自己的路。冷得像块石头，壮得跟我自己不相上下。他一天天长起来，就像一棵小树。瞧那副肩膀！夫人，他一拳能撂倒一头牛，头脑却是我平生所见最冷静的。现在且听我讲讲，他那份家业是怎么得来的。”富尔克把身子安顿得更舒坦些，便讲起他心爱的小狮子的一桩桩功绩来。玛格丽特垂着眼帘听着，只有一回抬起眼来，眸子里为西蒙的某桩事迹闪着同情的亮光。可待到讲完，她颊上的血色也褪尽了，她又记起，西蒙是她的敌人。

“您对这位西蒙似乎颇有偏爱，英国老爷。”

“由不得我不偏爱。”富尔克哼道。“这小子我是真心疼。他也还有几分惦记我，可他从没求过我一件什么事，往后也不会。他要什么，自己去挣。天底下再没有比他更倨傲、更自信满满的小子了！”


===Original===
“It is praiseworthy, perhaps,” Margaret said slowly, “but he cannot﻿—always﻿—win.”

Fulk’s eyes twinkled.

“So, so! And who shall teach him that, lady?”

She looked at him, and he saw her lips tight-shut.

“Aha! So ye think to bring Simon to heel, madame? I wonder if you will do it?”

===Chinese===
“这也许值得称道，”玛格丽特缓缓地说，“可他不可能——永远——得胜。”

富尔克眼中闪出狡黠的光。

“哦嗬！那夫人打算叫谁来教他这一课呢？”

她盯着他，他看见她双唇抿得死紧。

“啊哈！原来夫人想叫西蒙俯首帖耳？我倒想瞧瞧您能不能成？”


===Original===
“I desire only that he shall leave my land, never to return.”

“Well, he is like to,” Fulk announced. “He goes soon to join the King.”

“I am glad,” said the Lady Margaret primly. “I hope it will be very, very soon!”

“Here’s a heat!” Fulk remarked. “Why dost thou hate him so?”

“I have told thee. Once I sought to kill him﻿—” she spoke through clenched teeth﻿—“and could not! Could not, though he would have let me! I was a coward, and now I do owe my life to him.”

===Chinese===
“我只要他离开我的土地，永不回来。”

“唔，他多半快了。”富尔克宣布。“他不久就要去追随国王了。”

“我很高兴，”玛格丽特夫人一本正经地说。“我但愿那会非常、非常快！”

“好大的火气！”富尔克叹道。“你怎么这样恨他？”

“我对你说过了。有一回我想杀他——”她从咬紧的齿缝里迸出这几个字，“却没能下手！没能下手，尽管他本会容我动手！我是个懦夫，如今我还欠他一条命。”


===Original===
“And didst fight at his side, if Malvallet and thy lady speak sooth. That was not done of hate, madame.”

“I fought because﻿—because I had to escape. Not to save him!”

Fulk grunted.

“And even now, had I the means to hand, I would slay him gladly! Ay, gladly!”

“Brave words,” Fulk said. “Simon is not one to be worsted by a maid. What good would his death bring you? King Henry would fall upon thy land.”

===Chinese===
“你还曾在他身边并肩作战呢——要是马尔瓦莱和夫人您说的是真话。那可不是恨做得出的事，夫人。”

“我那样做，是因为——因为我必须逃出去。不是为了救他！”

富尔克哼了一声。

“便是此刻，只要家伙就在手边，我也会痛痛快快地杀了他！是啊，痛痛快快！”

“好豪壮的话。”富尔克说。“西蒙可不是会被个女子难倒的人。他死了于你何益？亨利国王（King Henry）会挥师直扑你的领地。”


===Original===
“I held out ’gainst Umfraville!”

“Ay, but the English are in now,” Fulk said.

A soft yet heavy tread sounded. Along the gallery came Simon, and at sight of him the Lady Margaret rose, yet was too proud to seek refuge in flight.

Simon halted before her, looking gravely into her eyes. But all at once a smile came to disperse the gravity, and it was so unlike the smile she had seen on his lips before, that almost it drew from her an answering gleam. There was no grimness in it, but a species of amused understanding.

===Chinese===
“乌姆弗拉维尔（Umfraville）我都顶住了！”

“是啊，可英国人如今已经进来了。”富尔克说。

一阵轻缓却沉重的脚步声响起来。西蒙顺着回廊走来，玛格丽特夫人一见他便站起身，却又骄矜得不肯逃开回避。

西蒙在她面前站定，神色郑重地凝视她的双眼。可转瞬之间，一抹笑容散开了那份郑重；那笑与她从前所见他唇边挂着的全然不同，竟几乎引得她眼中也泛出一丝回应的光。那笑里没有半分冷厉，倒是一种含着趣味的了然。


===Original===
“So my lord hath found thee?” he said. “I dare swear he hath told thee that I was once the bane of his life.”

“My lord is generous in his praise of you,” she answered stiffly.

Simon glanced at Fulk with uplifted brows.

“Never said I one word of praise!” Fulk roared. “I praise thee? God’s Body, I am not yet in my dotage! Praise﻿—thou pert boy, what ails thee? My lady knows now thy stubborn temper. Praise, forsooth!”

Simon laughed.

===Chinese===
“原来我的老爷找到您了？”他说。“我敢打赌，他准告诉过您，我曾是搅得他一生不宁的祸根。”

“老爷对您的夸奖可真慷慨。”她生硬地答道。

西蒙挑起眉毛，瞥了富尔克一眼。

“我一个字的夸奖也没说过！”富尔克吼道。“我夸你？上帝的圣体，我还没老糊涂呢！夸奖——你这没大没小的臭小子，中了什么邪？夫人如今可领教了你那副倔脾气。还夸奖呢，亏你说得出口！”

西蒙笑了。


===Original===
“Wert ever chary of praise to my face, sir,” he said mildly.

“And behind thy back!” Fulk averred. “A more worthless, blundering, silly-pated, obstinate lad never I saw! A pity is it that none ever thought to knock a little sense into thee.”

“Nay, my lord, one did try, but it seemed he failed, although he had me in my youth to mould.”

“A graceless, impudent coxcomb thou wert!”

“Indeed, I think I was so indeed,” Simon reflected. “A sore trial to thee, sir.”

===Chinese===
“您当着我的面，向来吝于夸奖，先生。”他温和地说。

“背后也没少夸！”富尔克一口咬定。“再没有比你更不成器、更毛手毛脚、更浑头浑脑、更顽固不化的小子了！可惜当年竟没有人想着敲你脑袋，灌进点道理。”

“有倒是有过一位，老爷，只是看来他失败了，尽管年少的我尽在他手里揉捏。”

“你那时是个放肆无礼、目中无人的纨绔子！”

“说真的，我想我确实就是。”西蒙沉吟道。“真让您受尽了熬煎，先生。”


===Original===
“Thou art well enough,” Fulk grunted. “Ye need not seek to cozen me.”

“Why, sir, I do know it to be useless,” Simon said.

Margaret glanced from one to the other. This new Simon was a stranger to her. The Simon she knew was a stern lord with little humour but great strength, not a smiling man who meekly listened to abuse of himself. She drew her skirts about her, preparing to depart, but Fulk struggled up, laying a hand on her shoulder.

“Now here is a right noble lady,” he informed Simon bluffly. “Shouldst take a lesson from her, lad.”

Simon’s eyes were upon her face, and Margaret felt the colour rise to her cheeks.

===Chinese===
“你也还算过得去。”富尔克哼了一声。“不必想法子哄我。”

“唉，先生，我知道哄您没用。”西蒙说。

玛格丽特的目光在两人之间转来转去。这个新的西蒙，于她是个陌生人。她认识的西蒙是个铁面老爷，没什么幽默，却有千斤膂力，不是一个笑呵呵、乖乖听着人数落自己的男人。她拢起裙裾预备离去，富尔克却挣扎着起身，一只手搭上她的肩头。

“瞧，这才是位真正高贵的夫人，”他直统统地对西蒙说。“小子，你该向她学学。”

西蒙的眼睛正落在她脸上，玛格丽特只觉得血往双颊涌。


===Original===
“It boots not to sing my praise to Lord Simon of Beauvallet, sir,” she said icily.

“Nay.” It was Simon who answered. “I need no telling.”

“Hadst best have a care to thyself,” Fulk warned him jovially. “My lady will be satisfied with naught save thy life.”

Margaret’s cheeks were flaming now. She bit her lip, glaring at the well-meaning but tactless Fulk.

“My life is hers,” Simon said quietly.

===Chinese===
“先生，对博瓦莱的西蒙大人夸我，是不管用的。”她说得冷若冰霜。

“不。”这回答话的是西蒙。“不必人告诉我。”

“你最好当心你自己的性命，”富尔克乐呵呵地告诫他。“夫人若不取了你的命，是决不肯罢休的。”

玛格丽特的双颊此刻烧成一片。她咬住嘴唇，狠狠瞪着一番好意却全无分寸的富尔克。

“我的命是她的。”西蒙平静地说。


===Original===
“I should have said thy death,” Fulk chuckled.

Simon drew his dagger from its sheath and presented the hilt to Margaret.

“That also.”

Margaret drew away from under Fulk’s hand.

“The jest is no doubt amusing, sir. I will leave you to enjoy it.”

===Chinese===
“我该说的是要你的死才对。”富尔克咯咯直乐。

西蒙从鞘中拔出短匕，把柄递到玛格丽特面前。

“这个也一样。”

玛格丽特从富尔克的手掌底下抽身退开。

“这玩笑想必有趣得很，先生。恕我告退，留二位细品。”


===Original===
Fulk conceived that this curious pair of lovers should now be left alone, so he stumped off towards the stairs, shaking his head over the incomprehensible ways of the younger generation.

Simon stood before Margaret, barring her passage. He was in a genial mood this morning, and strange forces were at work within him.

“Be pleased to let me pass,” Margaret said imperiously.

He shook his head.

“In a little while, Margot.”

===Chinese===
富尔克心下暗忖，这对古怪的情人此刻该留给他们独处了，便一步一顿地朝楼梯挪去，一路摇头，感叹年轻一辈的行事实在叫人捉摸不透。

西蒙站在玛格丽特面前，拦住她的去路。这天上午他心绪正好，体内有一股陌生的力量在涌动。

“请让我过去。”玛格丽特颐指气使地说。

他摇了摇头。

“再等一小会儿，玛戈（Margot）。”


===Original===
“My name, sir?” Her eyes flamed.

“Thy name.” He turned the naked dagger in his hand, looking down at it. “It was no jest, madame. If thou wouldst strike, strike now.”

“Thou hast tied my hands,” she answered bitterly. “I am not sunk so low. Thou hast told me that my life is thine by right of conquest. That is not so, but thou didst rescue me, in my dire peril, for which I must needs be grateful.”

“I want not thy gratitude. That debt is paid, and the past is dead. If thou dost indeed hate me﻿—”

“Ah, can you doubt that?” she cried.

===Chinese===
“我的名字，先生？”她双眼冒火。

“你的名字。”他在手中转着那柄出鞘的短匕，垂目望着它。“方才并非玩笑，夫人。您若要刺，现在就刺。”

“你已经捆住了我的双手。”她苦涩地答道。“我还没沦落到那一步。你说过，凭征服者的权利，我的命归你所有。并不是这样，可你确曾在千钧一发之际救了我，为此我不能不心怀感激。”

“我不要你的感激。那笔债已经还清，往事已经死了。你若当真恨我——”

“啊，这您也疑心么？”她喊道。


===Original===
He smiled a little.

“Thou hast assured me of thy hatred many a time, and of thine undying lust for vengeance. And yet.﻿ ﻿… Thou didst lie in mine arms once, content to be there, and it was not hate that prompted thee to feel thyself safe, and to sleep with thy head on my breast.”

“You taunt me with that? I was weary, and beside myself with fear and﻿—and everything!”

“Nay, I do not taunt thee. The memory of that ride is precious to me.”

She was silent.

===Chinese===
他微微一笑。

“你无数次向我保证你恨我，保证你怀着不死不休的复仇渴望。可是。……你曾有一次躺在我怀里，安然自适；叫你觉得自己安全、叫你枕着我的胸膛睡去的，绝不是恨。”

“您拿这个奚落我？我那时筋疲力尽，被恐惧和——和种种一切逼得神志迷乱！”

“不，我不是奚落你。那一程同骑的记忆，于我弥足珍贵。”

她不言语了。


===Original===
“Methinks,” Simon went on, “I never knew thee until I saw thee clad in thy boy’s clothes, fighting at my side.”

She flushed.

“Not for nothing am I the Amazon,” she said through her shut teeth.

“The Amazon? Nay, thou didst seem just a helpless child, grown suddenly small in thine unaccustomed raiment. It was that, I think, that awoke some devil within me, and made me slay Raoul.”

She laughed harshly.

===Chinese===
“依我看，”西蒙续道，“直到看见你一身男装、在我身侧并肩而战，我才真正认识了你。”

她脸上飞红。

“我这亚马逊悍女（Amazon）的名号，不是白得的。”她从咬紧的齿缝里迸出这句话。

“亚马逊悍女？不，你那时看上去只是个无助的孩子，穿着一身不惯常的衣裳，忽然显得那么娇小。我想，正是那副模样唤醒了我心里的魔障，叫我杀了拉乌尔（Raoul）。”

她笑得刺耳。


===Original===
“I thank you, milor’! So it was with a child that thou didst﻿—didst﻿—fall in love﻿—if love this be!”

“It must be love, Margot, but I know little of such matters. I only know that I want thee, and must have thee.”

“Then know also, sir, that I will none of thy wooing! Now let me pass!”

He stood aside at once, and she almost ran down the gallery to her rooms, meeting Alan on the way, and brushing past him without a glance in his direction. Alan strolled up to Simon, half-smiling.

“One pair of lovers left I in the hall, and here I stumble upon yet another. And I﻿—I the only real lover amongst you﻿—am maid-less. It is a sad world.”

===Chinese===
“多谢您了，英国老爷！原来您爱上的是一个孩子——是一个孩子——如果这也算爱的话！”

“那必定是爱，玛戈，只是这类事我所知甚少。我只知道我要你，而且非你不可。”

“那么也请你知道，先生，你的求爱我一概不领！现在让我过去！”

他立刻闪身让路，她几乎是奔着穿过回廊回房去了，半路迎头遇上艾伦，也头也不偏地从他身旁擦过。艾伦踱到西蒙跟前，嘴角半挂着笑。

“我在大厅里留下一对情人，转眼在这里又撞见一对。而我呢——我可是你们当中唯一真正在恋爱的人——身边反倒没有姑娘。这世道真叫人伤心。”


===Original===
“Alan,” Simon said abruptly. “Tell me of love. What is it?”

“I can tell thee naught that thou dost not know already. Long, long ago I did say that the day would come when some maid should wake thy cold heart. Behold, it is here at last, and thou dost ask me to tell thee of love!”

“It is love, then, that stirs my blood? But﻿—but﻿—but﻿—”

Alan laughed softly.

“It comes to all men at least once, and to some many times. To thee it came slowly, but to some it comes as a sudden shock.”

===Chinese===
“艾伦，”西蒙没头没脑地说。“给我讲讲爱情。它究竟是什么？”

“你早已知道的我再讲不出新意。很久很久以前我就说过，总有一天，会有哪个姑娘唤醒你这颗冷心。瞧，这一天终究到了，你倒要我给你讲爱情！”

“那么，搅得我热血翻腾的就是爱情？可是——可是——可是——”

艾伦低低地笑了。

“它至少会降临到每个男人身上一次，有的人要遭逢许多回。它到你这儿来得慢，可落在旁人身上，就像骤然一记震击。”


===Original===
Simon pondered gravely, and in a few moments Alan spoke again.

“I came in search of thee. I want to warn thee.”

Instantly Simon was on the alert, and the softness went out of his face.

“Well?”

“I mislike the looks of yon Frenchman, the Chevalier. Of late there hath come a new gleam into his eyes, and I think he seeks to do thee harm.”

===Chinese===
西蒙一本正经地沉吟起来，片刻之后，艾伦又开了口。

“我是来找你的。我有话要提醒你。”

西蒙霎时警觉，脸上的柔和一扫而空。

“讲。”

“我不喜欢那个法国人的模样，就是弗勒里瓦尔骑士（the Chevalier）。近来他眼里多了一种新的光，我想他存心要害你。”


===Original===
“That little popinjay!”

“But he was first in the field,” Alan said quietly.

“What mean ye?”

“Why, that he also loves the Lady Margaret, although she slights him.”

“He﻿—loves﻿—!” Simon’s hand clenched. “If I find﻿—”

===Chinese===
“那个小花孔雀！”

“可他先占了阵位。”艾伦不动声色地说。

“您这是什么意思？”

“咳，意思是他也在爱着玛格丽特夫人，虽然夫人不把他放在眼里。”

“他——爱——！”西蒙的手攥紧了。“若叫我发觉——”


===Original===
“Nay, listen, thou jealous lover! As I came hither I chanced on him, descending the stairway. Methinks he doth play the spy, and if it seems to him that thou art like to win the Countess, he will dispose of thee as best he can.”

Simon shrugged.

“What can he do! He made his submission long since.”

“And ye would trust to his honour, Simon?”

“I have as yet no reason﻿—since his submission﻿—for doubting it.”

===Chinese===
“别急，听着，你这醋海翻波的情人！我过来时，恰巧撞见他正下楼梯。依我看他是在刺探虚实；一旦叫他看出伯爵夫人多半要被你赢得，他就会千方百计地除掉你。”

西蒙耸了耸肩。

“他能做什么！他早就归降了。”

“你打算信他的名誉，西蒙？”

“自他归降以来，我还没有理由怀疑它。”


===Original===
“Save his shifty eyes, and spying ways. I would like to see him safe under lock and key, lad.”

“I cannot do that,” Simon answered shortly. “Do ye think I fear him?”

“Not I, but the soft-spoken are the most dangerous of all foes. Look well to thyself, Simon.”

“Ye think he will slay me?”

“Nay, I think he will try to,” Alan riposted. “Or mayhap he will hire some rogue to do it for him, and thus in a little salve his conscience.”

===Chinese===
“除了他那双滴溜溜的眼睛和刺探的行止。我倒巴不得把他锁起来关个牢靠，小子。”

“我不能那么办。”西蒙没好气地答。“你以为我怕他？”

“我不信你会怕，可话说得绵软的敌人，往往顶危险。你千万提防着点，西蒙。”

“你觉得他会来杀我？”

“不，我想他会试着来。”艾伦回敬道。“说不定他会雇个无赖代他下手，好叫自己的良心略微好过些。”


===Original===
Simon smiled.

“I doubt that same rogue will find his task hard indeed,” he remarked. “I have ears that hear that which makes no sound, and eyes that see in the dark.”

“Still, be more watchful than ever,” Alan warned him. “When do ye go to Bayeux?”

“Next week. I leave Geoffrey here, with thy father. Huntingdon must go with me.”

“And I.”

===Chinese===
西蒙微微一笑。

“只怕那位无赖会发现他的差事着实难办。”他说。“我这双耳朵，听得见无声之声；这双眼睛，看得见暗中之物。”

“话虽如此，还得比以往更加提防。”艾伦告诫他。“你几时动身去巴约（Bayeux）？”

“下礼拜。我把杰弗里（Geoffrey）留在这里，陪着你父亲。亨廷登（Huntingdon）必须同我走。”

“还有我。”


===Original===
“And thou.”

“When wilt thou return?”

“I know not.” Simon sighed faintly. “The message that thy father brought told me that the King had need of me. He waits but to see Gloucester triumph, and Domfront fall to Warwick. Then he will march on Rouen.”

“Whom will he leave to govern this land?”

“The Lady Margaret hath submitted. She will rule here.”

===Chinese===
“还有你。”

“你几时回来？”

“说不好。”西蒙轻轻叹了口气。“你父亲捎来的口信说，国王用得着我了。他如今只在等格洛斯特（Gloucester）奏捷，等多姆弗龙（Domfront）落入沃里克（Warwick）之手。随后他便要挥师直取鲁昂（Rouen）。”

“他会留谁来治理这片土地？”

“玛格丽特夫人已经归降。这里将由她主政。”


===Original===
“Some overlord he will appoint.”

“Perhaps Salisbury. Who knows?”

“Who indeed?” said Alan softly.

===Chinese===
“他还会指派一位宗主大人。”

“没准是索尔兹伯里（Salisbury）。谁知道呢？”

“是啊，谁知道呢？”艾伦轻声说。

## XVI / 第十六章

===Original===
How He Walked Alone in the Garden

Barehead he walked slowly through the garden that surrounded the castle, and the pale sunlight played about his fair hair, while the wind stirred it gently and blew it across his face. His sword hung at his side, but his hands were clasped listlessly behind him, and he bent his head, deep in thought.

It was four days since his talk with Margaret in the gallery and nothing further had passed between them since then. In two more days he would be gone from Belrémy, and for the first time in his life he was worried.

===Chinese===
他如何在园中独行

他光着头，缓缓穿过环绕城堡的花园，淡白的日光在他金发上跳跃嬉戏，微风轻轻撩动发丝，把它吹拂到他的脸上。佩剑悬在他腰际，双手却无精打采地拢在身后，他垂着头，陷入沉思。

自画廊里与玛格丽特（Margaret）那番谈话，已经过去四天，打那以后，两人之间再无只字片语。再过两天，他就要离开贝雷米（Belrémy）了；有生以来，他头一回烦忧起来。

===Original===
He paced slowly to and fro across the lawn before the castle, seemingly lost in his thoughts, frowning slightly. From an arbour close by the Lady Margaret watched him, hidden from his sight by the bushes through which she peeped. She had escaped from her ladies and come here to be alone, why, she knew not. Ever since the day when Simon had rescued her from Raoul she had been racked and torn by conflicting emotions. Not one of them could she recognise, but she knew that a strange misery had her in its hold, that would not let her rest, causing her sleepless nights and storm-tossed thoughts. She was hungry for an unknown something, and at times she would bite hard on her lip to hold back the rush of angry, heartsick tears that sprang to her eyes. She was restless, too, and short with her ladies. Not even Jeanne would she have near her for long, but fled away by herself as now, fighting what she half-guessed to be a yearning for her mate. Try as she might she could not forget the feel of his arms about her on the ride from Raoul’s land, or the touch of his lips on hers. Again and again she tried to lash her anger to fresh energy, remembering all Simon’s iniquities, dwelling on them fiercely, pressing the scar on her bosom with nervous, trembling hands.

For a long time she sat motionless in the arbour, heedless of the cold, watching Simon’s ceaseless, measured pacing with eyes that burned dark and troubled. Presently she saw him wheel to the left, and in a moment he had passed from her sight, through a gap in the yew hedge. Some of the rigidity left her then, and she fell to plucking at her gown, twisting the silk between her fingers, her mouth all awry with some inward pain. But in a little while she covered her face with her hands, and so remained for a long time, silent.

She did not know why she suddenly looked up, every nerve strained to attention. No one was in sight, but from somewhere near at hand had come the sound of brushing against leaves. It was a tiny sound; a bird might have caused it, or some small animal, and yet she leaned forward, peering through the bushes with eyes that were narrowed and keen. Again came the sound, and she rose, noiseless, her skirts gathered up in one tense hand.

===Chinese===
他在城堡前的草坪上缓缓踱来踱去，仿佛全然沉在思绪里，眉头微微蹙起。近旁一座凉亭里，玛格丽特夫人正望着他；她藏身灌木之后，从枝叶间偷觑，他浑然不觉。她从女官们身边脱身出来，独自到这里图个清静，为什么，连她自己也不知道。自打西蒙（Simon）把她从拉乌尔（Raoul）手中救出的那一天起，她就被种种交缠的情绪煎熬撕扯。哪一种她都认不出来，只知道一股陌生的愁苦攫住了她，不放她安宁，搅得她夜夜无眠、心绪如惊涛骇浪。她饥渴般渴望着某种不知名的东西，有时只得狠狠咬住嘴唇，才压得住涌上眼来的那股又恼又伤心的泪水。她还坐立不安，对女官们也没了好声气。连让娜（Jeanne）也不许在身边久待，总是像眼下这样独自躲开，同那隐约猜到的、对一个伴侣的渴念苦苦相搏。任凭她怎么用力，也忘不掉从拉乌尔领地策马而归的路上，他双臂环着她的感觉，忘不掉他嘴唇贴上她嘴唇的触感。她一遍又一遍竭力把怒火重新烧旺，翻检西蒙的桩桩恶行，狠狠地念念不忘，又神经质地用颤抖的手指按住胸口那道伤疤。

许久，她一动不动地坐在凉亭里，不觉着冷，只用那双灼灼的、幽黑而烦乱的眼睛，望着西蒙那不知疲倦、有板有眼的踱步。不多时，只见他转身向左，穿过紫杉树篱的一处缺口，转眼便从她视线里消失了。她周身那股僵直顿时松了几分，伸手扯弄起裙裾来，指间绞着丝绸，嘴角因某种内里的痛楚扭得歪歪的。可没过多久，她又用双手掩住了脸，就这样久久待着，默然无声。

她也不知道为什么，忽然抬起头来，浑身神经都绷得紧紧的。四下里不见人影，可是近旁什么地方，传来一阵擦着树叶的窸窣声。声音极细，兴许是只鸟儿，或是哪只小兽弄出来的，她却俯身向前，眯起眼睛，目光锐利地透过灌木细察。那声响又起，她悄没声儿地站起身，一只绷紧的手把裙裾攥在掌中。

===Original===
To the left was the castle, to the right the hedge that bordered the bowling-green. Straight ahead, at the far end of the lawn was the gap through which Simon had gone. It led along an alley between high hedges, to her own garden, the pleasaunce, away from the castle. On the other side of the hedge that ran parallel to the castle were fields, leading down to the moat. It was towards this hedge that she looked, and presently, where the leaves were sparse, saw a shadow, moving stealthily beside it, on the other side. It was but a fleeting glimpse that she had, but her nerves sprang to a conclusion. Quickly she pushed through the tangled bush, and stepped on to the green. One moment she stood there, staring intently to the right, and again heard the faint rustle. The leaves seemed to quiver in one spot, and grew still again.

Every pulse was throbbing in her body, but she forced herself to walk calmly forward, outwardly careless and aimless. The blood sang in her ears, for she knew that there was one, perhaps more, behind the hedge, watching her intently. On she went, heart beating loud and unevenly, but walking slowly, looking about her. It seemed to her that the bowling-green had become of a sudden a vast desert which she could never span, but at last she came to the end, pretended to hesitate a moment, and then went through the gap. The path twisted almost at once, and so soon as she had rounded the bend, she caught up her skirts and ran as if for dear life along the tortuous alley.

===Chinese===
左边是城堡，右边是紧邻滚木球场的树篱。正前方，草坪尽头，就是西蒙穿过去的那个缺口。缺口里一条夹道在高高的树篱之间蜿蜒，通向她自己的花园——那座游憩园（pleasaunce），远离城堡。与城堡平行的那道树篱的另一侧是田野，一路下延到护城河边。她望着的正是这道树篱，不多时，便在枝叶稀疏处瞥见一条影子，在篱那一面悄悄移动。但只这一瞥，她的神经已霍然得出结论。她飞快拨开纠缠的灌木丛，踏上球场草地，在原地站定一瞬，凝神望向右边，又听见一声轻微的窸窣。有一处枝叶似乎颤了一颤，随即重新归于静止。

她周身的血脉突突直跳，却强迫自己安安静静向前走，外表一派漫不经心、全无目的的模样。耳中血流轰鸣，她知道树篱后面藏着一个人，兴许还不止一个，正目不转睛地盯着她。她只管往前走，心口擂得又响又乱，脚步却放得缓慢，且走且四下张望。在她感觉里，那座滚木球场蓦地变成了一片渺无际涯的荒漠，任她怎么也走不到头；好容易挨到尽头，她装模作样迟疑了一下，这才走进缺口。小径几乎立刻拐了个弯，她刚一绕过弯角，便一把兜起裙裾，没命地沿着曲折的夹道飞奔而去。

===Original===
Simon was in the pleasaunce gazing abstractedly down upon the sundial that stood in the centre. All about him were little walks and flowerbeds, with snowdrops growing in them. The sound of light footsteps speeding towards him made him look up quickly, a hand to his sword-hilt. Into the pleasaunce came the Lady Margaret, panting for breath and running like one possessed. He started forward, brow lowering.

“What is it? Who hath dared﻿—”

She almost fell into his arms, outstretched to receive her, clutching at his long tunic with desperate fingers.

===Chinese===
西蒙正在游憩园里，恍恍惚惚地俯望着园心那座日晷。他四下里尽是小小的步道和花坛，坛中雪滴花正在吐蕊。一串轻捷的脚步声飞快地朝他而来，他霍然抬头，一手按上剑柄。玛格丽特夫人奔进园来，气喘吁吁，跑得像是着了魔。他抢步迎上前去，眉头拧紧。

“出了什么事？谁人竟敢——”

她几乎是跌进他张臂相迎的怀抱里，十指死命攥住他的长罩袍。

===Original===
“Come, come away﻿—I implore thee! Give﻿—give me﻿—thine arm! Thy﻿—thy﻿—sword? Ah! Quickly, quickly! Away﻿—from this﻿—spot!”

Simon’s hands were on her shoulders, his voice rang harshly in her ears.

“Who hath dared to molest thee? Answer!”

===Chinese===
“走，快走——我求求你！把——把手臂给我——扶着我！你的——你的——剑呢？啊！快，快！离开——离开这——地方！”

西蒙双手按在她的肩上，声音厉声震响在她耳际。

“谁人竟敢冒犯于你？说！”

===Original===
“None﻿—none!” She tugged wildly at his tunic. “It﻿—it is not that! Oh, come, come! Every moment you stay﻿—may mean﻿—death!”

He stared at her in surprise, then cast a quick look around.

“Death? What mean ye, child?”

===Chinese===
“没有——没有人！”她发疯似的扯着他的罩袍。“不是——不是那么回事！哦，走，快走！你多耽一刻——便可能——没命！”

他愕然瞪着她，随即飞快环顾四周。

“没命？你说什么，孩子？”

===Original===
“Oh, tarry not!” she implored. “I﻿—take me to the castle﻿—I beg of you! I﻿—oh, come, milor’! Come! Come! There is someone﻿—lurking﻿—behind the bushes! He﻿—I saw him yonder, creeping in thy wake! Even now﻿—he may be﻿—upon us! For God’s sake come away!”

But Simon had his arms about her and his voice was strangely moved.

“And thou didst come to warn me, Margaret?”

===Chinese===
“哦，切莫耽搁！”她哀告道。“我——带我回城堡去——我求求你！我——哦，走吧，英国老爷（milor’）！走吧！走吧！有人——埋伏——在树丛后面！他——我方才看见他在那边，鬼鬼祟祟跟在你身后！这会儿——他没准——已经扑到咱们跟前了！看在上帝分上，快走吧！”

可西蒙已经张开双臂把她搂住，声音里透着一种异样的感动。

“你是特意来给我报信的，玛格丽特？”

===Original===
In her frenzy she scarcely noticed his embrace, but beat her clenched fists against his breast.

“Oh, will you not come? Will you not come?”

“I fear no assassin that ever drew breath, child,” he said gently, “but I will come if you will it so.”

===Chinese===
她神智狂乱，几乎没觉察到他的拥抱，只顾把攥紧的双拳擂在他胸口。

“哦，你到底走不走？你到底走不走？”

“但凡喘气的刺客，没有一个能叫我害怕，孩子，”他温声说道，“可你既然要，我便走。”

===Original===
She drew a sobbing breath of relief, falling back.

“Then﻿—then﻿—walk on my right, milor’, and﻿—and﻿—walk swiftly!”

Even as she spoke he turned sharply round on his heel, staring into the bushes. Slowly he drew his sword, and went forward, panther-like. Margaret stayed by the sundial, trembling, but fearful of uttering a single cry lest it warn the prey he stalked of danger. She saw Simon leap forward, as if some spring within him had been loosed, and thrust with his sword through the hedge. A muffled shriek came, a scuffle and the sound of thudding footsteps, retreating in haste. Simon turned, wiped his sword upon the grass, and sheathed it. Then he came back to Margaret and stood before her.

===Chinese===
她如释重负，抽噎着深深吸了口气，向后退开。

“那你——那你——走在我右手边，英国老爷，还有——还有——要走快些！”

话音未落，他已霍地旋过脚跟，直瞪瞪盯进树丛。他缓缓拔出剑来，如黑豹般潜行向前。玛格丽特守在日晷旁，浑身簌簌发抖，却连一声也不敢喊出，生怕惊动他悄悄扑猎的猎物。她看见西蒙猛地向前一纵，仿佛周身哪根弹簧骤然松开，一剑透过树篱直刺过去。一声闷哑的尖叫，一阵扭打，随即是沉重的脚步声，仓皇遁走。西蒙转过身来，在草上拭净剑锋，还剑入鞘，然后回到玛格丽特面前站定。

===Original===
“Was it only out of gratitude that thou didst come to warn me?” he asked.

She started. Gripping the edge of the sundial, and gazing up at him with wild, hungry eyes.

“Thine is a strange hatred,” Simon went on, and held out his hands. “Is it hate indeed?”

===Chinese===
“你来给我报信，当真只是出于感激？”他问道。

她猛地一怔，双手死死扣住日晷边沿，仰头望着他，一双眼睛又狂野又饥渴。

“你这份恨法好生古怪，”西蒙接着说道，向她伸出双手。“当真是恨么？”

===Original===
Her knees shook under her; her breath came fast and uneven.

“Ay﻿—hate﻿—hate﻿—hate! Ah, what ails me? What have I done? What﻿—what﻿—I am mad! I﻿—it was gratitude! I﻿—I have not changed! I yield me﻿—never!” She shrank away, warding him off. “Touch me not! I could not let him slay thee﻿—thus! I﻿—I could not, but﻿—I﻿—I think﻿—I am﻿—like to﻿—swoon!”

He caught her even as she swayed, sweeping her off her feet. One instant she struggled, crying out, and then crumpled up in his arms, her head falling back lifeless.

===Chinese===
她双膝颤个不住，呼吸急促而紊乱。

“是——恨——恨——恨！啊，我这是怎么了？我做了什么？什么——什么——我疯了！我——那是感激！我——我没有变！要我屈服——永不！”她缩身后退，抬手挡他。“不许碰我！我不能叫他杀了你——这样杀了你！我——我不能，可是——我——我看——我怕是——就要——晕过去了！”

她身子一晃，他已一把将她揽住，打横抱了起来。一刹那间她还挣扎喊叫，随即整个人在他臂弯里瘫软下去，脑袋向后一仰，没了生气。

===Original===
Swiftly Simon bore her to the castle, brushing past staring lackeys, and striding to the stairs. He came to the Countess’s rooms, and there found Jeanne with Hélène.

“She hath swooned only,” he said in answer to the startled outcry. “It was her wound, belike.”

“Lay her down, lay her down!” Jeanne commanded, and spread cushions on the wooden settle.

Gently Simon laid his burden on them.

===Chinese===
西蒙抱着她飞快回到城堡，从一个个瞪目结舌的仆役身旁擦身而过，大步登上楼梯，来到伯爵夫人的居处，见让娜正同埃莱娜（Hélène）在一起。

“她不过是晕了过去，”他冲着那一片惊呼答道，“多半是旧伤的缘故。”

“放下，快放下！”让娜发话下令，一面在木长椅上铺开坐垫。

西蒙轻轻把怀中人放到垫上。

===Original===
“She ran to warn me of danger from an assassin’s knife,” he said curtly. “She hath ta’en no hurt, nor I. Look to her, mademoiselle, and have a care.”

Jeanne smiled a little at that.

“Yes, milor’,” she said demurely, and with twinkling eyes watched him go out.

===Chinese===
“她是奔来给我报信，说有刺客持刀行凶，”他简短地说。“她没受着伤，我也无恙。照看好她，小姐，千万小心。”

让娜闻言微微一笑。

“是，英国老爷，”她一本正经地应道，眼睛里闪着狡黠的光，目送他走出去。

===Original===
Sighing the Lady Margaret came to her senses.

“Jeanne? Methought﻿—ah, he is safe?” She struggled up, staring about her.

Jeanne pressed her back on to the cushions.

“Yes, *chérie*, quite safe. He brought thee here. *Mignonne, mignonne*, I would not lie to thee!”

===Chinese===
玛格丽特夫人长叹一声，悠悠醒转。

“让娜？我还只道——啊，他平安么？”她挣扎着起身，四下里张望。

让娜把她按回垫子上。

“平安呢，*亲爱的*（chérie），千真万确。是他把你抱回来的。*小宝贝，小宝贝*（mignonne），我不会骗你的！”

===Original===
The strained muscles relaxed. Margaret lay still, eyes closed. Presently she opened them, and looked wistfully up at her lady.

“I﻿—I am mad, Jeanne,” she said, and her lips quivered. “I﻿—do not﻿—really﻿—care﻿—whether he﻿—is alive﻿—or dead! I﻿—my head﻿—is reeling! Jeanne! I﻿—I am﻿—weeping! What﻿—comes to me?”

“Love, *chérie*,” Jeanne whispered, and kissed her softly.

===Chinese===
紧绷的筋肉松弛了下来。玛格丽特静静躺着，双目紧闭。过了一会儿，她睁开眼睛，怅然仰望她的女官。

“我——我疯了，让娜，”她说，嘴唇簌簌直颤。“他——是死——是活——我——当真——满不在乎！我——我的头——晕得厉害！让娜！我——我——在哭！我这是——怎么了？”

“是爱，*亲爱的*，”让娜低低说道，温柔地吻了吻她。

## XVII / 第十七章

===Original===

How He Left Belrémy, and How the Lady Margaret Dealt with Her Cousin

===Chinese===

他如何离开贝雷米（Belrémy），玛格丽特夫人（the Lady Margaret）如何处置她的表弟

===Original===

Geoffrey burst in upon him, Alan following languidly at his heels.
“Simon, what is this I hear?” Geoffrey demanded. “Is it true that one sought to slay thee?”
“Ay.” Simon smiled a little. “A creature in the Chevalier’s pay.” He nodded to Alan. “Thou wert right, O sage!”
“Of course I was right,” Alan said placidly. “What wilt thou do now?”
“I go to Bayeux.”

===Chinese===

杰弗里（Geoffrey）一头闯了进来，艾伦（Alan）有气无力地跟在他脚后。
“西蒙（Simon），我听来的这是什么话？”杰弗里喝问。“有人要害你性命，可是真的？”
“是真的。”西蒙微微一笑。“一个受弗勒里瓦尔骑士（the Chevalier）豢养的亡命徒。”他朝艾伦点了点头。“你说对了，贤哲！”
“我自然对，”艾伦心平气和地说。“如今你打算怎么办？”
“我去巴约（Bayeux）。”

===Original===

“Ay, but what of thy would-be assassin?” Geoffrey cried.
“Naught. I know not who he was, and I have no proof. Once I am gone the Chevalier will be happy enough.”
Geoffrey was dissatisfied.
“I would clap him up!”
“I have not the power. He would deny the charge, and the Lady Margaret rules here now.”

===Chinese===

“是，可那个想行刺的家伙呢？”杰弗里嚷道。
“不与他计较。我不知道他是谁，手里也没有凭据。只等我一走，弗勒里瓦尔骑士便自会快活起来。”
杰弗里大不以为然。
“换了我，就锁拿他下狱！”
“我没有这个权柄。他自会抵赖，何况如今这里是玛格丽特夫人当家。”

===Original===

“Simon, it is not like thee to be magnanimous!” Geoffrey exclaimed. “What ails thee?”
“God knows. The Chevalier is too little for my vengeance, I think. I can punish him best by ignoring him. But when I am gone, do thou have a care, Geoffrey.”
“I mislike the task of ruling this land,” Geoffrey grumbled. “Leave Huntingdon, and take me with thee.”
“He is too young. And thou wilt be content enough with thy Jeanne. She would never forgive me an I wrested thee from her now.”

===Chinese===

“西蒙，宽宏大量可不像你的为人！”杰弗里喊道。“你这是怎么了？”
“天晓得。我想，弗勒里瓦尔骑士这号人物，还不配我的报复。不理他，就是对他最重的惩罚。只是我走之后，你可得当心，杰弗里。”
“治理这地方这差事我可不喜欢，”杰弗里咕哝道。“把亨廷登（Huntingdon）留下顶班，带我一块儿走吧。”
“他年纪太轻。再说，有你的让娜（Jeanne）在，你也尽会心满意足。这当口我要是把你从她身边夺走，她决计不会饶我。”

===Original===

“What is that to thee?” Geoffrey stated. “Thou art changed indeed, Simon!”
Simon shrugged.
“Maybe,” he said, and then was silent for a long time.

===Chinese===

“那与你何干？”杰弗里不客气地说。“你当真是变了，西蒙！”
西蒙耸了耸肩。
“也许吧，”他说，随后久久没有作声。

===Original===

Later, Ranaud came to him, recovered now, and in high spirits. Simon received him unemotionally, but Ranaud tried to kiss his hand.
“Ah, lord, it was well done! I would it had been my hand that had slain the toad. By the Virgin, your grip was of iron on his fat neck!”
Simon smiled a little.
“Ye did well, Ranaud, though I slew Raoul. Art a brave man, methinks. What want ye of me?”

===Chinese===

后来，拉诺（Ranaud）来见他，伤已痊愈，兴致极高。西蒙不动声色地接待他，拉诺却想要亲吻他的手。
“啊，大人，干得漂亮！但愿掐死那癞蛤蟆的是我的手！凭圣母起誓，您钳住他那肥脖子的一握，真是铁打的！”
西蒙微微一笑。
“你干得不错，拉诺，虽说拉乌尔（Raoul）是我杀的。我看你是条好汉。你想求我什么？”

===Original===

Ranaud smote his thigh.
“Thought I to myself, by God, this is a fit master for me! So please you, sir, I’ll join your guards, or your archers. I have some training with a crossbow.”
Simon looked him over for a moment, and then nodded abruptly.
“If I am the master for thee, thou art the man for me, yet the Lady Countess doth command your loyalty.”
“I am Ranaud, and I serve whom I please,” the giant answered. “But it seems to me that the day is not far distant when I shall call ye both master, and own not two neither.”
“That is as maybe,” Simon said coldly, and drew pen and parchment forward to enrol Ranaud.

===Chinese===

拉诺一拍大腿。
“我心里就琢磨：凭上帝说，这才配做我的主上！您若肯收纳，老爷，我就投您的卫队，或是您的弓箭手队。弩弓我练过几手。”
西蒙把他上下打量了片刻，随即干脆地点了点头。
“我若是配做你的主上，你也正是配我用的人；只是你的忠心，如今先得听伯爵夫人（the Lady Countess）号令。”
“我是拉诺，我高兴伺候谁就伺候谁，”巨人答道。“不过依我看，那样的日子不远了：我既要管你们二位都唤作主上——而这主上却又并不作两个数。”
“那也难说。”西蒙冷冷说道，随即取过笔和羊皮纸，把拉诺登入了名册。

===Original===

On the day of his departure he went to the Lady Margaret’s bower where she reclined on a couch, pale and listless. He was clad in his armour, and at the sight of it her lips quivered.
“I come to bid thee farewell, Margot,” he said quietly.
She rose, gazing at him.

===Chinese===

他动身那日，到玛格丽特夫人的闺房去；夫人正倚在软榻上，面色苍白，恹恹没有生气。他一身铠甲在身，她一见那副甲胄，嘴唇便颤了起来。
“我是来向你辞行的，玛戈（Margot），”他轻声说。
她站起身，凝望着他。

===Original===

“You﻿—you are going﻿—to Bayeux?”
“Ay. Thou art rid of me at last.”
She winced at that, and her eyes filled with tears.
“You﻿—return﻿—not?”

===Chinese===

“你——你——要走了——去巴约？”
“是。你总算摆脱我了。”
这话刺得她一颤，眼里顿时蓄满了泪。
“你——不——回来了？”

===Original===

“If God wills, I shall return. But if so be I fall in battle, think this of me, Margot, that if ever I harmed thee, or hurt thee, at least it was not of mine own desire. And remember also that I did love thee very dearly.” He went down on his knee, most unexpectedly, and kissed her cold hand. “Plague not Malvallet,” he said humorously. “He is no match for thy fierceness.”
She smiled wanly.
“I have submitted.”
“Ay.” He rose and looked at her for a moment. “Farewell, Margot.”

===Chinese===

“若上帝愿意，我会回来。可倘若我阵亡了，玛戈，请这样想我：我纵然曾伤害你、苦待你，至少那从来不是我的本心。还要记得，我确曾深深地爱过你。”说着他出人意料地单膝跪下，吻了吻她冰凉的手。“别去搅扰马尔瓦莱（Malvallet），”他打趣道，“他可不是你凶悍脾气的对手。”
她凄然一笑。
“我已降了。”
“是。”他站起身，朝她凝视了片刻。“别了，玛戈。”

===Original===

“Fare﻿—well﻿—” The whisper just reached him. He turned and went to the door.
The weights that held Margaret to the floor seemed to fall away. She stumbled forward, hands outstretched.
“Ah, thou wilt come back? Thou wilt!”

===Chinese===

“别——了——”那声低语轻得刚够他听见。他转身向门口走去。
仿佛压着玛格丽特把她钉在原地的千钧重负骤然卸落。她踉跄着向前扑去，双手前伸。
“啊，你会回来的？你会的！”

===Original===

He caught her in his arms.
“I will come back. But when I come it will be to lead thee to the altar, if I am alive still.” He bent his head and kissed her long and passionately, and although she did not return his kiss, she was passive under it. The next moment he had released her, and was gone through the door, away.
The Lady Margaret fell on her knees beside the table, clinging to it, while hard, dry sobs shook her. How long she remained there she did not know, but presently came the noise of horses’ hoofs without, and the sound of voices. She pulled herself up, and dragged her feet to the window, kneeling on the high bench below it. Dry-eyed, she watched Simon clasp Geoffrey’s hand in farewell, and kneel to receive Fulk’s blessing. Then she saw him mount his horse, and ride towards the drawbridge, in the midst of his men. Once he looked up at her window and seeing her there, raised his mailed hand in salute. Then he was gone, and the clattering of the horses’ hoofs died away in the distance.

===Chinese===

他把她揽进怀里。
“我会回来。只是我回来之时，若还活在世上，便是要领你走向圣坛的。”他低下头，长长久久、热热烈烈地吻她；她虽没有回吻，却静静地由他吻着。转瞬之间他已松开手，出门去了，走远了。
玛格丽特夫人扑跪在桌旁，双手死死攀着桌沿，干涩的硬咽一阵阵摇撼着她。她在那里跪了多久，自己也不知道；不多时，外面传来马蹄杂沓之声，还有人语。她挣扎着撑起身子，拖着两脚挪到窗前，跪在窗下高高的长凳上。她眼眶干干的，望着西蒙与杰弗里执手道别，又跪下去领受富尔克（Fulk）的祝福。随后她看他翻身上马，在部众簇拥之下朝吊桥驰去。他抬头朝她的窗望了一望，见她在那里，便举起戴甲的手扬了扬，算是致意。接着他去了，得得的马蹄声在远处渐渐消散。

===Original===

Jeanne entered softly, and came to her mistress, passing an arm about her waist. So they stood for a time, silent, until Margaret disengaged herself. Her voice was calm, now, and cold.
“Jeanne, bid them fetch my cousin and his father.”
“Yes, *chérie*. What will you do?”
“Fetch them, Jeanne,” Margaret repeated gently.

===Chinese===

让娜悄没声儿地进来，走到女主人身旁，伸臂环住她的腰。两人这样静静站了一会儿，直到玛格丽特轻轻挣开。她的声音此刻已经平静，而且冷冷的。
“让娜，叫人去请我表弟和他父亲来。”
“是，*亲爱的*（chérie）。您打算怎么办？”
“请他们来，让娜，”玛格丽特柔声又说了一遍。

===Original===

When the Chevalier and her uncle came in they found Margaret seated in a high-backed chair by the table, her hands folded in her lap, her stately head held high.
The Sire de Galledemaine bowed to her.
“You desired our presence, madame?”
“Yes, my uncle. I desire that ye shall hear what I have to say to your son.”

===Chinese===

弗勒里瓦尔骑士和她的叔父进来时，只见玛格丽特端坐在桌旁一把高背椅上，双手交叠在膝头，尊贵的头颅高高扬起。
加勒德曼老爷（the Sire de Galledemaine）向她躬身。
“夫人召见，有何吩咐？”
“是的，叔父。我要对你儿子说的话，要请你亲耳听着。”

===Original===

The Sire looked surprised, glanced inquiringly at the Chevalier.
“Is it possible, madame, that Victor has annoyed thee?”
Her lips curled.
“Annoyed is a small word, sir. The Chevalier will understand, when I say that I think his own lands stand in need of him.”

===Chinese===

老爷面露讶色，探询地瞟了骑士一眼。
“夫人，莫非是维克托（Victor）开罪了您？”
她的嘴唇撇了撇。
“‘开罪’二字，说得太轻了，老爷。我说，依我看，他自己的领地正用得着他——弗勒里瓦尔骑士自会懂得这话的意思。”

===Original===

The Chevalier started, dropping the flower he held.
“Margot!”
“My name is not for such as you to use, sir!” she said haughtily. “Ye do know why I will no longer harbour you.”
“Fair cousin, you are distraught,” the Chevalier said silkily. “I know naught.”

===Chinese===

骑士浑身一震，手里的花掉了下来。
“玛戈！”
“我的名字，轮不到你这等人来叫，先生！”她傲然道。“我为何再不肯收留你，你是知道的。”
“美丽的表姐，您这是昏了神智，”骑士油腔滑调地说。“我什么都不知情。”

===Original===

Her look was full of scorn.
“Ye desire that I should be more explicit?”
“Most certainly, madame, for I am in the dark.”
“Then know, sir, that I was in the garden when you did send your bravo to slay my Lord of Beauvallet.”

===Chinese===

她满眼轻蔑。
“你要我说得更明白些？”
“自然，夫人，我实在蒙在鼓里。”
“那么听好，先生：你遣刺客去谋害博瓦莱大人（Beauvallet）的时候，我正在园子里。”

===Original===

Her uncle gasped, falling away from his son.
“Victor! Madame, it cannot be true! My son﻿—”
“Look at his face,” she said disdainfully. “Is it not proof enough?”
The Chevalier was gnawing at his lip, livid, but he contrived to smile.

===Chinese===

她叔父倒抽一口冷气，身子从儿子身边退开。
“维克托！夫人，这决不会是真的！我的儿子——”
“看看他的脸色，”她鄙夷地说，“这还不够作证么？”
骑士咬着嘴唇，脸色铁青，却还强挤出笑容。

===Original===

“You rave, cousin. I know naught of this affair.”
“But ye will return to your own lands, sir, nevertheless.”
The Sire came towards her, and his eyes were haggard all at once.
“Madame, it cannot be true! It were dishonour! I implore thee listen to Victor’s defence!”

===Chinese===

“表姐，您说胡话了。此事我一概不知。”
“可是先生，您还是得回您自己的封地去。”
加勒德曼老爷朝她走近前来，眼神霎时间枯槁了。
“夫人，这决不会是真的！那是奇耻大辱！我求您听一听维克托的分辩！”

===Original===

“Can he deny it?” she sneered.
The Sire turned to his son.
“Victor! My God, Victor, thou didst not do this thing?”
“Nay,” the Chevalier muttered, but could not look at him.

===Chinese===

“他抵赖得了么？”她冷笑道。
加勒德曼老爷转向儿子。
“维克托！我的上帝，维克托，这事不是你干的吧？”
“不是，”骑士含混地说，却不敢抬眼看他。

===Original===

His father started forward, seizing him by the shoulder.
“Look at me! Is it true?”
The Chevalier shot one glance at Margaret’s rigid countenance, and laughed.
“You are over squeamish, *mon père*,” he said carelessly.

===Chinese===

他父亲抢上前去，一把抓住他的肩头。
“看着我！是不是真的？”
骑士朝玛格丽特铁板的面孔飞快瞟了一眼，忽然笑了。
“你也太讲究这些虚体面了，*父亲大人*（mon père），”他满不在乎地说。

===Original===

The Sire’s hand fell away from him as from a thing unclean.
“Thou craven cur!” he whispered, and turned again to the Countess. “Madame, I can say naught, save that this deed is as foul to me as it is to you.”
She bent her head.
“That I know, sir. I do trust that thou wilt continue here, for I do value thy friendship. But thy son goes within forty-eight hours, or I will formally banish him from my domain. That is my last word.”

===Chinese===

老爷的手从他肩上滑落，仿佛碰着了什么腌臜东西。
“你这怯懦的狗东西！”他低声骂道，随即又转向伯爵夫人。“夫人，我无话可说，只有一句：这桩行径，我同您一样深以为耻。”
她垂下头来。
“这个我知道，老爷。我诚愿您仍旧留居此地，您的情谊我是十分珍重的。但令郎须在四十八个钟头之内离去，否则我将正式把他逐出我的领地。这是我最后的话。”

===Original===

“You are generous, madame,” her uncle said, very low.
“For thy sake, my uncle,” she answered, and stretched out her hand to him.
The Chevalier bowed.
“Then I take my leave of thee, fair cousin.” He sneered at her. “When thou art miserable in yon Saxon’s arms, think of me!”

===Chinese===

“您宽宏大量，夫人，”她叔父低低地说。
“那是看在您的情分上，叔父，”她答道，向他伸出手去。
骑士躬了躬身。
“那么，美丽的表姐，我告辞了。”他朝她冷笑。“待你在那撒克逊人怀里受苦的时候，想想我吧！”

===Original===

“Go!” his father thundered. “Must you add to your vileness? Go!”
The Chevalier bowed again, ironically, and went out. His father picked up the flower he had dropped, and threw it into the fire.
“Madame, ye will excuse me. I am﻿—not myself. This hath been a bitter blow. I would fain retire.”

===Chinese===

“滚！”他父亲厉声如雷。“你还要往你的丑行上再添一笔么？滚！”
骑士又讥诮地一躬身，出去了。他父亲拾起他掉落的那朵花，掷进了火里。
“夫人，请恕我失礼。我——有些神志恍惚。这一下打击太痛切了。容我告退。”

===Original===

“Indeed, I am very sorry,” Margaret said, her hand on his arm. “I could not do otherwise.”
“Ye were too generous,” he said shakily, and kissed her hand.
As soon as he was gone, Margaret turned to Jeanne, who all the time had stood silent behind her chair.

===Chinese===

“我心里实在难过，”玛格丽特说，一只手搭在他的臂上。“可我别无他法。”
“您太宽大了，”他声音发颤地说，吻了吻她的手。
他前脚才出门，玛格丽特便转身面向让娜——让娜自始至终静静立在她椅后。

===Original===

“*Chérie*, wilt thou request thy Geoffrey to wait on me here?”
Jeanne threw her arms about Margaret.
“Oh, Margot, Margot, it was well done. I will fetch Geoffrey at once!” She ran out, flushed and excited.

===Chinese===

“*亲爱的*，劳你请你的杰弗里到这里来见我，可好？”
让娜张开双臂搂住玛格丽特。
“哦，玛戈，玛戈，这事办得漂亮！我立刻去叫杰弗里来！”她涨红着脸，欢天喜地地跑了出去。

===Original===

She found Geoffrey in the great hall, disconsolate at his friends’ departure. When he saw her his brow cleared, and he held out his arms.
“Nay, I am come on an errand,” she said demurely, and curtseyed. “The Lady Countess doth request your presence in her chamber, milor’.”
Geoffrey came to her, sweeping her off the ground in his embrace.

===Chinese===

她在大厅里找到杰弗里，他正为友人们的离去怏怏不乐。一见是她，他眉头舒展，张开了双臂。
“别，我是奉命传话来的，”她一本正经地说，屈膝一福。“伯爵夫人请您到她房中去相见，英国老爷（milor’）。”
杰弗里向她走来，一把将她整个抱离了地面。

===Original===

“What care I for the Lady Countess? Kiss me, thou rogue!”
Jeanne obeyed meekly, and was set down.
“This is very wrong,” she reproved him. “It is no way to treat a herald. Follow me now, Geoffrey, at once!”

===Chinese===

“伯爵夫人与我何干？亲亲我，你这促狭鬼！”
让娜乖乖从命，随后被放了下来。
“这样很不好，”她数落他道。“哪有这样对待传令使的！现在就跟我走，杰弗里，立刻！”

===Original===

“What wants thy mistress?” he asked.
Jeanne led him up the stairs.
“No doubt she will tell thee,” she said. “Geoffrey, it is not at all seemly to put thine arm about a herald’s waist.”
“Nay, but it is very seemly to put mine arm about my betrothed’s waist,” he retorted, and drew her protesting onward. Outside Margaret’s door he paused. “Kiss me, or I will no further,” he threatened.
“Thou art a sore trial,” Jeanne sighed, and raised her bewitching little face. “No, that is enough, Geoffrey! What if someone was looking?” She opened the door. “Sir Geoffrey, madame!”

===Chinese===

“你家夫人有何吩咐？”他问。
让娜领着他上楼去。
“不用说，她自会告诉你，”她说。“杰弗里，把胳膊搂着传令使的腰，可实在不像话。”
“可不是，搂着未婚妻的腰，就再得体不过了，”他回嘴，不顾她的抗议拖着往前走。到了玛格丽特房门外，他停住脚。“亲亲我，不然我可不走了，”他吓唬道。
“你可真是个磨人的冤家，”让娜叹了口气，仰起那张娇俏迷人的小脸。“好了，尽够了，杰弗里！要是叫人瞧见了呢？”她推开门。“杰弗里爵士到，夫人！”

===Original===

“Enter, enter!” Margaret said, and came forward to meet them. “Methinks thou wert gone a long time on thine errand, *chérie*?” Her eyes sparkled a little.
“That was not my fault, madame,” Jeanne said. “Indeed, Sir Geoffrey is very﻿—very﻿—obstinate in the matter of﻿—coming quickly.”
“I doubt it not,” Margaret smiled, and looked at Malvallet. “Sir, I did request your presence to tell you that I have banished my cousin from this land for﻿—for setting his men to slay Lord Simon. I﻿—I will have no such﻿—dishonour on my head﻿—so﻿—so will ye please to﻿—see to it that he is gone within﻿—forty-eight hours? I﻿—I do not desire that﻿—anyone should be told﻿—why he goes.”
Geoffrey recovered from his amazement with difficulty.

===Chinese===

“进来，进来！”玛格丽特说着，迎上前来。“我看你这趟差事去了好半晌呢，*亲爱的*？”她眼里闪着一点笑意。
“那可不怪我，夫人，”让娜说。“实在是杰弗里爵士在——在——‘快些过来’这件事上——固执得很。”
“这我毫不怀疑，”玛格丽特微笑着，望向马尔瓦莱。“先生，我请你前来，是要告诉你：我已将我的表弟逐出此地，为的是——是他派人去杀西蒙大人。我——我不能容这等——耻辱落在我的名头——所以——所以请你费心——让他四十八个钟头之内——务必离去，可好？我——我不愿——叫任何人知道——他离去的缘故。”
杰弗里好不容易才从惊愕中缓过神来。

===Original===

“Madame! Ay, I will see to it. Let me say, madame, that I honour you﻿—greatly.”
She smiled rather sadly.
“It was﻿—the least I could do,” she said. “I﻿—Sir Geoffrey, you and I﻿—you and I﻿—have fought in the past﻿—and I have given ye﻿—no cause to love me. But﻿—but I am wiser now﻿—a little﻿—and I would wish to﻿—live at peace with you.”
Geoffrey knelt at once, kissing her hand.

===Chinese===

“夫人！是，这事我一定督办。容我说一句，夫人：我敬重您——敬重得很。”
她笑了笑，颇为凄然。
“这是——我至少该做的，”她说。“我——杰弗里爵士，你和我——你和我——从前兵戎相见——我也从没——给过你什么喜欢我的缘由。可是——可是如今我明白些了——明白了一点——我愿意——与你和平相处。”
杰弗里当即跪下，吻她的手。

===Original===

“Madame, I thank you. Be assured that I will do all in my power to aid and uphold you in this land.”
She pressed his fingers slightly.
“Thank you,” she said. “When do ye steal my Jeanne from me?”

===Chinese===

“夫人，多谢您。请放心，我必竭尽全力，在这片土地上扶助您、维护您。”
她轻轻握了握他的手指。
“多谢，”她说。“你几时来把我的让娜偷走呢？”

===Original===

He rose.
“Why never, madame. I only seek to wed her. And that right soon.”
“I﻿—I wish you﻿—happiness,” she said unsteadily, and tried to smile.

===Chinese===

他站起身。
“怎么会偷呢，夫人。我只是要娶她为妻。而且就要快快成礼。”
“我——祝你们——幸福，”她说得声音不稳，努力想笑。

===Original===

Jeanne caught her hands.
“Ah, *chérie*, you too shall have happiness!”
Margaret’s head was bowed.
“Maybe. I﻿—I think I will be﻿—alone for a while.”
