They left her then, her head held bravely and her lips smiling.

===Chinese===

让娜握住她的双手。
“啊，*亲爱的*，您也一定会得到幸福的！”
玛格丽特垂着头。
“也许吧。我——我想我要——独自待上一会儿。”
于是两人离开了她；她把头昂得高高的，依然勇敢，唇边含着笑。

## XVIII / 第十八章

===Original===

How He Came to Bayeux, to the King

He rode slowly into Bayeux, at the head of his men, Alan at his side, and Huntingdon some way behind, leading the rearguard. He had ridden north and west, past Falaise and Caen, for Belrémy was situated between Argentan and Falaise, commanding the centre of Lower Normandy some twenty-five leagues from Bayeux. On his arrival he went straight to where the King dwelt, taking Alan with him. Henry received him at once, and dispatched a page to conduct him to his room. He was there with his brother, the Duke of Clarence, who commanded one of the three divisions of the army, and whose task it was to prepare for the advance on Rouen.

When Simon entered Henry came quickly forward.

“Ah, my Soldier!” He would not permit Simon to kneel, but embraced him, and also Alan. “And my Poet! Where is my Knight?”

===Chinese===

他如何来到巴约，觐见国王

他率着部众，缓缓策马进了巴约（Bayeux）城；艾伦（Alan）在他身侧，亨廷登（Huntingdon）领着殿后部队，缀在稍后之处。他们一路先北行、再西折，取道法莱斯（Falaise）与卡昂（Caen）；盖因贝雷米（Belrémy）正坐落于阿让唐（Argentan）与法莱斯之间，扼守下诺曼底（Lower Normandy）的中央腹地，距巴约二十五里格上下。一到城中，他便带着艾伦径直奔赴国王驻跸之处。亨利（Henry）即刻召见，并遣了一名侍童（page）引他入内室。国王正在室中，与他的弟弟克拉伦斯公爵托马斯（Duke of Clarence）同在；公爵统带全军三路中的一路，职责是筹备进军鲁昂（Rouen）。

西蒙（Simon）进屋，亨利快步迎上前来。

“啊，我的武人！”他不许西蒙下跪，拥抱了他，也拥抱了艾伦。“还有我的诗人！我的骑士呢？”

===Original===

“At Belrémy, sire,” Simon answered. “Huntingdon came with me.”

Henry was disappointed.

“I would you had brought Malvallet in his stead, Simon, but ye know best.”

“Why, sir,” Alan said, “Simon left Geoffrey in a lady’s arms. He is shortly to be wed.”

“What?” Henry turned in astonishment. “Is it indeed so? No﻿—not the Amazon?”

“No!” It was Simon who answered, quickly. “One of her ladies!”

===Chinese===

“在贝雷米，陛下，”西蒙答道。“亨廷登随我一道来了。”

亨利面露失望。

“西蒙，我倒宁愿你换马尔瓦莱（Malvallet）家那位来，不过你自有道理。”

“嗳，先生，”艾伦说，“西蒙把杰弗里（Geoffrey）留在了一位夫人的怀抱里。他不日便要成婚了。”

“什么？”亨利愕然转过身来。“当真有这等事？不——总不会是那位亚马逊悍女（Amazon）吧？”

“不是！”抢着回答的是西蒙。“是她手下的一位女官！”

===Original===

“Is it so? I had suspected Alan of it, but not Malvallet. Sit down, Simon, and tell me all.” He touched a pile of parchment sheets on the table. “Thy dispatches are very curt.” He smiled, and picked one up. “Listen, Alan! ‘My very dread and sovereign lord the King’﻿—so we start, and all is well. But wait!﻿—‘I have the honour to inform your Majesty that the town of Belrémy did yesterday morning make submission after an attack from my forces. I have also the honour to inform your Majesty that the castle has ceded, save for the Lady Countess, who holds out against your Majesty. I am your Majesty’s faithful servant, Simon of Beauvallet.’ Well! My Majesty’s faithful servant just whets mine appetite for news, and no more. Here again﻿—‘I am constrained to tell your Majesty that on Tuesday last the Lady Countess did escape from Belrémy, accompanied only by her lady, Mademoiselle Jeanne. I did set forth in pursuit, and finding madame in the hands of your Majesty’s ally, Raoul, called the Terrible, did slay him for the treatment he did mete out to the Lady Countess. And so have rid Normandy of a very foul rogue.’ I thank thee, Simon.” Henry’s eyes twinkled. “As you say in this lengthy dispatch, Raoul was mine ally. And a pretty time I have had, seeing his people who flocked here demanding thy head.”

“What said your Majesty?”

“Why, I did say that I desired no further dealings with Raoul’s men, but I would know why my allies are thus summarily slain, without trial or delay.”

===Chinese===

“是么？我疑心的是艾伦，倒没疑到马尔瓦莱家的头上。坐下，西蒙，从头细细讲来。”他伸手触了触桌上那叠羊皮纸。“你的军报写得未免太简了。”他含笑拈起一份。“听着，艾伦！‘我最敬畏尊崇的至尊君王陛下’——起头这样，礼数周全。且慢！——‘臣有幸奏禀陛下：贝雷米城经臣部进击，已于昨日清晨献城归顺。臣还有幸奏禀陛下：城堡亦已献降，唯女伯爵夫人（the Lady Countess）仍拒守抗命，不从陛下。臣乃陛下忠顺之仆，博瓦莱（Beauvallet）的西蒙。’好啊！我这位‘忠顺之仆’光吊我的胃口，便再无下文。这里又有一份——‘臣不得不奏禀陛下：女伯爵夫人已于上周二仅携女官让娜（Jeanne）小姐一人，自贝雷米出逃。臣随即发兵追赶，探得夫人已落入陛下盟友「可怖」拉乌尔（Raoul the Terrible）之手，因其凌虐女伯爵夫人，遂将他诛杀。如此已为诺曼底除去一个卑劣透顶的无赖。’多谢你了，西蒙。”亨利眼里闪着狡黠的笑意。“正如你这份长篇大论的军报所写，拉乌尔原是我的盟友。我这段日子可真够热闹——他手下那班人成群结队拥到这里，定要讨你的脑袋。”

“那陛下是怎么答复的？”

“嗳，我当时说的是：我无意再与拉乌尔的党羽往来，可我要弄个明白，我的盟友为何竟被这般不问情由地杀却，不经审判，也不容分说。”

===Original===

Simon stood up.

“Ay, sir. I do owe you an apology, but in my place ye would have done the same.”

“I doubt it not,” Henry said. “But it was unlike thee to kill him without trial, vile though we guessed him to be.”

“I did it in sudden, overwhelming anger, sir.” Curtly Simon told him all that happened in Raoul’s palace.

Henry smote his hands together.

===Chinese===

西蒙站起身来。

“正是，先生。我确当向您赔罪，可换了您处在我的位置，也会一般行事。”

“这我毫不怀疑，”亨利说。“可不经审判便杀他，实在不似你的作为，尽管我们都猜定他是个恶棍。”

“我是骤然间怒火压顶，先生。”西蒙简短地把拉乌尔宫邸里发生的一切讲给他听。

亨利双掌一击。

===Original===

“By my troth, I would I had been there! The knave! I am well rid of him, indeed. But it has caused a deal of pother, Simon.”

“The blame is mine alone, sir.”

“I’d not lay it on thy shoulders, my Simon. I must uphold my generals.”

“Sire, if it please you, punish me for the deed, so the French shall not call you assassin.”

“It is over now,” Henry answered. “Clarence dealt with them.”

===Chinese===

“凭真心起誓，我恨不得当时在场！这恶棍！除了他这祸害，实在痛快。可这事也惹出了好大一场风波，西蒙。”

“罪责全在我一人，先生。”

“我并不会把罪名扣到你头上，我的西蒙。我必须撑持我的将帅。”

“陛下，若是陛下允准，便请为此事降罚于我，免得法国人骂您是刺客。”

“如今已经了结，”亨利答道。“克拉伦斯料理过他们了。”

===Original===

Simon smiled across at the Duke, of whom he was very fond.

“Then I thank your Grace.”

“I’d uphold thee through fire and water!” Clarence said. “But I was all amazed when I heard how Simon the Just had fallen.”

Alan upraised his dreamy voice.

“Nay, it was justice. Quick justice.”

===Chinese===

西蒙含笑望向公爵，对这位殿下他素来极是喜爱。

“那么，多谢殿下了。”

“纵是赴汤蹈火，我也撑你到底！”克拉伦斯说。“只是当初听说‘公正’西蒙（Simon the Just）竟也栽了跟头，我着实吃惊不小。”

艾伦用他那梦幻般的声嗓开了口。

“不，那正是公正。迅捷的公正。”

===Original===

“The Poet hath spoken,” Henry laughed. “Now, Simon, tell me from beginning to end, how you took Belrémy.”

“By siege first, and then by storm, sir.”

Henry clicked his tongue impatiently.

“And now I know,” he remarked. “Alan, tell me!”

Alan crossed his legs.

===Chinese===

“诗人发话了，”亨利朗声笑道。“好了，西蒙，从头至尾讲给我听，你们是怎样拿下贝雷米的。”

“先围困，后强攻，先生。”

亨利不耐烦地咂了咂舌。

“这我算知道了，”他说。“艾伦，你来讲！”

艾伦跷起了腿。

===Original===

“Certainly, sire. We sat down before Belrémy until Christmastide, and lived in blissful peace. I composed an ode and Geoffrey kicked his heels. Simon likes not bliss, sir, nor peace. He must always be at work. So he dug a mine into the town, under the southern ramparts, which seemed made of granite. Very pleased was he with the mine, sire, and he set his brain to work out a plan. Huntingdon had sat down before the western ramparts, which were unstable and ready for assault. Simon bade him, at a certain hour of a certain day, train his cannon upon it for a while, and at a given signal, storm the walls, thus attracting the garrison. Geoffrey and I were in readiness with the rest of our army, for it was Simon’s plan to go with eleven other men along his mine, to dig themselves out within the town, and, so as Geoffrey had given the signal for assault, and the town was in a turmoil, to speed to the southern gates and open them, letting down the drawbridge. The which he did, sire, by some miracle, and in we rode, Geoffrey first, to hem Huntingdon’s attackers in from the rear. I came second to lead my men into the town. With Simon in his gilded armour at our head, we swept all before us to the marketplace, and there had a fierce battle. I was captured, sire, and borne to the castle, whither fled most of the garrison. The town was Simon’s then, but the Lady Margaret sent to tell him that if he withdrew not his men I should hang from the battlements.” Alan paused, smiling.

“Go on!” Henry commanded. “What did Simon then?”

“He entered the castle, sir, alone, as a herald. From all I can hear he did draw upon the Lady Margaret, who would have had him slain, and held his sword-point against her breast, so that not one of her people dared move hand or foot, lest he should press home. She is a brave lady, sir, and she would not have let Simon have his way, but that he threatened to sack the town and slay the children. Then, perforce, she yielded, and led him to me. Simon conceived that it would be well for him to enter the castle in force, so he left me where I was (I was wounded and could not rise) and took the Lady Margaret back to his quarters as hostage. And after that it was simple.”

===Chinese===

“遵命，陛下。我们屯兵贝雷米城下，一直屯到圣诞节期，日子过得太平赛神仙。我作了一首颂歌，杰弗里闲得直磨靴跟。西蒙既不爱享福，陛下，也不爱太平，他总得找事做。于是他掘了一条地道通进城里，就在南城墙底下——那段城墙瞧着简直像花岗岩铸的。他对这条地道得意极了，陛下，便绞起脑汁定出一条计策来。亨廷登早已屯兵西城墙之下，那一段墙本就不牢，正堪扑攻。西蒙吩咐他，约定某日某时，先把炮口对准西墙轰上一阵，再依约发的信号扑城，好借此把守军都吸引过去。杰弗里和我就带着其余人马整装以待；因为按西蒙的计较，要由他亲率另外十一个人循地道而进，在城内掘土而出；待杰弗里发出进攻信号、满城乱作一团，便飞奔赶往南门，开了城门，放下吊桥。这一桩，陛下，他竟凭着几分奇迹办成了；我们便纵马入城，杰弗里一马当先，从背后兜住围攻亨廷登的守军。我居其次，随即领我的部卒杀进城去。西蒙一身鎏金铠甲当先开路，所过之处一概荡平，直扫到集市广场，在那里恶战了一场。陛下，我做了俘虏，被押进城堡，守军大半也逃到了那里。城池这时已归西蒙所有，可玛格丽特夫人（Lady Margaret）传话给他：若不把人马撤走，便把我吊上城垛。”艾伦说到这里，含笑一顿。

“讲下去！”亨利喝令。“西蒙随后怎样了？”

“陛下，他孤身一人，以传令使的身份进了城堡。据我所闻，他当真拔剑指向玛格丽特夫人——夫人原是要取他性命的——剑尖抵住她的胸口，她手下没有一人敢动弹手脚，生怕他把剑直送进去。陛下，她是位刚烈的夫人；若非他扬言要洗劫全城、连孩童也不留，她是断不肯让西蒙遂愿的。迫于无奈，她屈服了，把他领到我跟前。西蒙思量，还是以重兵进堡为好，便把我留在原处（我负了伤，起不得身），把玛格丽特夫人带回他的住处充作人质。这之后，便再无难事。”

===Original===

Henry drew a deep breath.

“By God, but thou art a Man!” he cried, and looked at Simon in admiration. “And the Amazon? Tell me of her!”

It was Alan who answered.

“She is the loveliest woman ever I saw, sir, and the bravest. A tigress.”

“But she submitted?”

“Ay,” Simon said. “Because I did save her life. Give me leave, sir. I would come out of this armour.”

===Chinese===

亨利深深吸了一口气。

“上帝啊，你这才真算得上一个男子汉！”他嚷道，钦佩地望着西蒙。“那位亚马逊悍女呢？讲讲她！”

答话的是艾伦。

“陛下，她是我平生所见最美丽、也最勇敢的女子。一只母虎。”

“可她竟降服了？”

“是啊，”西蒙说。“因为我救了她的性命。容我告退片刻，先生。我想把这身甲卸了。”

===Original===

Henry nodded.

“Ay, go. And Alan too. I like not Alan in armour. Did my Lord of Montlice find thee? I sent him.”

“He did arrive, roaring,” Alan smiled. “We left him with Geoffrey.”

“He is a man after mine own heart,” Henry said, and dismissed them.

===Chinese===

亨利点点头。

“好，去吧。艾伦也去。我不爱看艾伦披甲的样子。我那蒙利斯（Montlice）老爷可找着你了？是我打发他去的。”

“他倒是到了，一路吼着来的，”艾伦笑道。“我们把他留给了杰弗里。”

“他是个深得我心的人，”亨利说着，打发了他们出去。

===Original===

The very next day he called for Simon and was closeted with him for a long hour. When Simon emerged at last Alan was waiting for him, and took him apart.

“Well?”

A sigh escaped this new Simon.

===Chinese===

就在第二天，他便召西蒙过去，同他密谈了足足一个钟头。西蒙终于出来时，艾伦正候在外面，把他拉到一旁。

“怎么样？”

一声叹息，从这位换了个人的西蒙口中溜了出来。

===Original===

“I am to go into the Côtentin. To join Gloucester. Huntingdon goes to Coutances. Thou art to remain here.”

“For how long art thou to be away?”

Simon shrugged.

“Till the Côtentin is subdued. Gloucester plans to lay siege to Cherbourg as early in April as may be. Cherbourg will not fall easily.”

“I see,” Alan said, and said no more.

===Chinese===

“我要进军科唐坦（Côtentin），去与格洛斯特（Gloucester）会合。亨廷登去库唐斯（Coutances）。你留驻此地。”

“要去多久？”

西蒙耸耸肩。

“到科唐坦平定为止。格洛斯特打算四月里一到便围攻瑟堡（Cherbourg），愈早愈好。瑟堡可不是轻易攻得下来的。”

“我明白了，”艾伦说，再没有多言。

===Original===

Simon left Bayeux the following week, but it was not until some ten days had passed that Henry, much occupied with the affairs of his conquered land, had time for private speech with Alan. Then, one day, when he was listening to Alan’s harping, he roused himself, and spoke.

“Alan, what ails our Simon?”

Alan drew a last, sobbing wail from his strings, and laid the harp aside.

“Ah!”

===Chinese===

西蒙于次周离开巴约；可一直过了十来天，亨利才忙罢新征服之地的诸般政务，得空与艾伦私下叙谈。一天，他正听艾伦抚弄竖琴，忽而回过神来，开口说道。

“艾伦，咱们的西蒙这是怎么了？”

艾伦从琴弦上最后拉出一声如泣如诉的哀音，把竖琴搁到一旁。

“啊！”

===Original===

“Dour he was always, but never did his mind wander as now it doth! Half the time he dreams, and once I heard him sigh. Simon! Then there is new light in his eyes, and methinks he is more gentle than of yore. What hath come to him? Is he sick?”

“Some call it sickness, sire.”

Henry turned sharply round in his chair to gaze at Alan.

“God’s my life, he is not﻿—he cannot be﻿—in love?”

“Why, sir, have you not always said, with me, that love would one day come to him?”

===Chinese===

“他向来阴沉，可神思从没像如今这般恍惚过！一半时辰他都在出神做梦，我还听见他叹过一回气——西蒙啊！况且他眼里有了一种新的光彩，我看他也比从前温和了。他这是怎么了？莫不是病了？”

“有人管这个叫病，陛下。”

亨利在椅中霍地转过身来，直盯盯望着艾伦。

“上帝的性命，他莫不是——绝不可能——恋爱了？”

“嗳，先生，您不是一向与我一同说，爱情总有一天会临到他头上么？”

===Original===

“Ay, but﻿—Alan, I never suspected. Who is it?”

“It is the Lady Margaret of Belrémy, sire.”

The King’s jaw dropped. In blank astonishment he stared at Alan.

“The Amazon? The tigress? Alan, you jest!”

“No, sir. True it is. I saw it coming slowly, but Simon knew not his own heart till he saw my lady in Raoul’s arms.”

===Chinese===

“说过是说过，可是——艾伦，我万万没疑到这上头。是谁？”

“是贝雷米的玛格丽特夫人，陛下。”

国王的下巴险些掉落下来。他惊愕得呆了，直直瞪着艾伦。

“那个亚马逊悍女？那只母虎？艾伦，你打趣！”

“不是，先生。千真万确。这情愫徐徐滋长，我全看在眼里；可西蒙直到亲眼看见我的夫人在拉乌尔的臂弯里，才认出自己的心。”

===Original===

“Then that was why he killed him!” Henry started up. “It was jealousy!”

“It was the lion in him, sir, roused to awful rage.”

Henry sank back.

“ ’Fore God, I am glad I was not Raoul! And she? Doth she love him? Is there love in her?”

“Love there is, sir, but also pride. She loves him, but she will not admit it, even to herself. They woo with daggers, Simon and his lady.”

===Chinese===

“怪不得他要杀他！”亨利霍地站起身来。“原是妒火中烧！”

“先生，那是他体内的雄狮，被激起了骇人的怒火。”

亨利颓然靠回椅背。

“上帝在上，幸亏我不是拉乌尔！那她呢？她爱他么？她心里可有爱？”

“爱是有的，先生，可傲气也是有的。她爱他，却连对自己都不肯承认。他们这两人求爱，动的是匕首——西蒙同他那位夫人。”

===Original===

Henry smiled.

“I would give much to see it. She hates him, then?”

“So she says, sir, but it is a strange hatred. She loves him, and when he returns to her, she will wed him.”

“Return?” Henry frowned. “I had planned to have him at my side when I march on Rouen.”

Alan said nothing.

===Chinese===

亨利莞尔。

“便是舍个大价钱，我也想亲眼瞧瞧这一幕。这么说，她是恨他的？”

“她嘴上是这么说的，先生，可这恨法好生古怪。她是爱他的；等他回到她身边，她会嫁给他的。”

“回到她身边？”亨利皱起眉头。“我本打算进军鲁昂时，把他带在我身边。”

艾伦默然不语。

===Original===

“Speak, Alan!”

“If ye take Simon to Rouen, sire, it is death to his happiness. That campaign may last a year.”

Henry leaned his chin in his hand, thinking.

“What would ye have me do? If Simon loves indeed he must have his way. Geoffrey, too, I suppose. Yet I can ill spare them.”

“He will follow your Majesty unquestionably, sir. It is not for me to advise you.”

===Chinese===

“讲呀，艾伦！”

“陛下，您若带西蒙去鲁昂，便是置他的幸福于死地。那场战事说不定要拖上一年。”

亨利手托下巴，沉吟起来。

“那你要我怎样？西蒙若当真爱了，便该让他遂愿。我猜杰弗里也是。可我实在少不得他们。”

“先生，他必会不置一词，追随陛下。此事轮不到我来进言。”

===Original===

“He would sacrifice his love for his duty?”

“He is Simon of Beauvallet,” Alan said quietly.

===Chinese===

“他肯为职责牺牲自己的爱情？”

“他是博瓦莱的西蒙，”艾伦平静地说。

## XIX / 第十九章

===Original===
How They Fared at Belrémy During His Absence

The Lady Margaret walked upon the terrace of the castle alone. It was mid-March, and Simon had been absent for three long weeks. She had had news of him through Geoffrey, and knew that he was fighting in the Côtentin, away to the west, with King Henry’s brother, the Duke of Gloucester. He did not write to Margaret, and he sent no messages. The letters that came from him came rarely, and were bald and unsatisfying.

The Lady Margaret glanced across the gardens wistfully. In the pleasaunce Geoffrey sat with his bride, she knew. She craved companionship, but she would not intrude into these two lovers’ idyll. Her ladies watched her, and she had sent them from her, to pace slowly up and down the terrace, her thoughts far away, and her black eyes sad and longing.

A rumble sounded behind her; Fulk was stumping after her, his little eyes twinkling good-humouredly.

“Hey, hey! Not so fast, lass!” he roared. “Come thou here, I say!”

===Chinese===
他不在的日子里，贝雷米诸人如何度日

玛格丽特夫人（Lady Margaret）独自一人在城堡的露台上踱步。时值三月中旬，西蒙（Simon）已经离去整整三个漫长的星期。她从杰弗里（Geoffrey）那里得到他的消息，知道他正同国王亨利（Henry）的弟弟格洛斯特公爵（Duke of Gloucester）一起，在西面远处的科唐坦（Côtentin）作战。他不给玛格丽特写信，也不捎只言片语。他的信来得稀少，写得干巴巴的，读来难以解慰。

玛格丽特夫人怅然朝花园那头望去。她知道，游憩园（pleasaunce）里，杰弗里正与他的新婚妻子同坐。她渴望有人作伴，却不愿搅扰这对爱侣的旖旎时光。女官们都关切地望着她，她却早打发她们去了，独自在露台上缓缓踱来踱去，思绪飘向远方，一双黑眼睛盛满哀伤与渴念。

身后响起一阵沉重的脚步声；富尔克（Fulk）正迈着僵腿一路追来，一双小眼睛闪着和善的笑意。

“嘿，嘿！别走那么快，丫头！”他吼道。“过来这儿，我说！”

===Original===
He and she were close friends by now, and the haughty Lady Margaret came meekly to his side, to sit down on the stone seat. Fulk sank heavily beside her, puffing and blowing.

“What dost thou here, silly maid?” he demanded.

“I am not a silly maid,” she answered mildly. “I am a woman-grown. So be not so rude, milor’.”

“Ho﻿—ho! And how old art thou? No more than twenty-eight, I’ll swear.”

“Twenty-eight?” Margaret sat up indignantly. “I am not yet twenty-six!”

Fulk laughed.

===Chinese===
如今两人已成了亲密的朋友，高傲的玛格丽特夫人乖乖地走到他身旁，在石凳上坐下。富尔克沉沉地挨着她坐下，呼哧呼哧直喘气。

“你在这儿做什么，傻丫头？”他问。

“我才不是傻丫头，”她温婉地答。“我是长大成人的妇人了。所以您别这么粗鲁呀，英国老爷（milor’）。”

“嗬——嗬！那你有多大年纪？顶多二十八，我敢起誓。”

“二十八？”玛格丽特愤然坐直了身子。“我还不到二十六呢！”

富尔克哈哈大笑。

===Original===
“A maid still! Now whence this fiery blush?”

“Do﻿—do I look twenty-eight?” Margaret demanded.

“Nay, nay, twenty-one rather. What dost thou here, alone?”

“I was﻿—taking the air.”

“I’ll warrant ye were sighing and pining for that lad of mine.”

“Alan?” said the Lady Margaret coolly. “Nay, why should I?”

===Chinese===
“还是个姑娘嘛！那这阵火辣辣的红晕又从何而来？”

“我——我看着像二十八么？”玛格丽特追问。

“不，不，倒更像二十一。你怎么一个人待在这儿？”

“我——在——透透空气。”

“我敢担保，你是在为我那孩子长吁短叹、害着相思呢。”

“艾伦？”玛格丽特夫人冷冷地说。“不，我干吗要呢？”

===Original===
“Alan! Hark to the child! Simon, thou dull girl!”

“I﻿—do not think of him at all! And﻿—and I will not have ye﻿—call me names!”

“Here’s a heat! Art a pert, saucy lass, I say.”

“Well, sir, and what else?”

“A wilful, headstrong baggage!” Fulk roared.

Margaret covered her ears with her hands.

===Chinese===
“艾伦！听听这孩子的话！是西蒙呀，你这迟钝的姑娘！”

“我——压根儿没有想他！还有——我不许你——这样骂我！”

“好大的火气！我说，你就是个嘴尖舌利的泼辣丫头。”

“好啊，老爷，还有呢？”

“一个任性刁蛮的小贱货！”富尔克吼道。

玛格丽特双手捂住了耳朵。

===Original===
“Do not shout at me!” she said. “I wonder you care to sit with a﻿—a baggage!”

“So do I,” Fulk grunted. “A fitting pair will ye make, you and Simon. Belike ye will scratch his eyes out before he hath time to school ye. Maids were more gentle when I was a lad.”

“Milor’ Fulk, I do not know why ye should couple my name with that of Lord﻿—”

“There’s enough, there’s enough! Think ye I am come to listen to thy foolish chatter against Simon? Bah! Bah, I say!”

“I heard you,” said the Lady Margaret.

“Thou and thy hate! Talk for babes! Empty lies!”

===Chinese===
“不许冲我嚷嚷！”她说。“真奇怪，您还乐意陪一个——一个贱货同坐！”

“我也奇怪呢，”富尔克咕哝道。“你和西蒙凑成一对，倒真是再般配不过。只怕还没等他腾出工夫调教你，你先就把他的眼珠子挠出来了。我年轻那会儿，姑娘们可比这温柔多了。”

“富尔克英国老爷，我不明白您为何要把我的名字同那位大人的名字扯在一块——”

“够了，够了！你当我是来听你编排西蒙的那些傻话的？呸！我说，呸！”

“我听见了，”玛格丽特夫人说。

“还有你的什么恨！全是哄娃娃的话！空洞的谎言！”

===Original===
“Sir﻿—”

“Now, will ye have done, Margot? Body o’ me, do ye think to fool a man of my years? Thou froward maid!”

The Lady Margaret abandoned the struggle.

“Indeed, I have never been so set at naught and﻿—and bullied in my life!”

“Better for thee if thou hadst,” growled Fulk. “Thou dost need a master.”

The Lady Margaret tilted her chin.

===Chinese===
“老爷——”

“行了，行了，玛戈（Margot），还不打住？凭我这副身子起誓，你当骗得过我这把年纪的人么？你这悖逆的丫头！”

玛格丽特夫人只得放弃了这场争辩。

“说真的，我这辈子从没受过这样的轻慢，还——还有这样的欺压！”

“若当真如此，倒于你更好，”富尔克低吼。“你就缺一个管你的人。”

玛格丽特夫人扬起了下巴。

===Original===
“And will have one. In Simon!” Fulk went on, louder. “Shake not thy head, I say!”

“He﻿—Simon﻿—will not return. Thou﻿—thou must look for my master﻿—elsewhere,” she said, a tiny catch in her voice.

Fulk put his great arm about her waist.

“Said I not thou wert a silly lass? Did he tell thee that he would come back? Answer me, Margot!”

“I have forgotten.”

“That for a tale! He said he would return, and he breaks not his word.”

===Chinese===
“而且准会有一个。就是西蒙！”富尔克的声音愈发响了。“不许摇头，我说！”

“他——西蒙——不会回来了。你——你得到别处——去寻我的主人，”她说，嗓音里有一丝细微的哽咽。

富尔克伸出粗壮的胳膊搂住她的腰。

“我不是说过你是个傻丫头么？他可曾告诉过你他会回来？回答我，玛戈！”

“我忘了。”

“这话鬼才信！他说过要回来，而且从不食言。”

===Original===
“I﻿—I do not﻿—care!”

“Ho﻿—ho!” Fulk pinched her cheeks. “Canst look me in the face and say that, child!”

Margaret was silent, eyes downcast.

“Now here come a pretty pair,” Fulk remarked, and looking up Margaret saw Geoffrey and Jeanne wending their way across the garden. Geoffrey’s arm was about Jeanne’s waist, and his black head was bent over her brown one.

Margaret looked away, chin set firmly.

“Never fret!” Fulk said. “Simon will come. Hey, there!”

===Chinese===
“我——我不——在乎！”

“嗬——嗬！”富尔克拧了拧她的面颊。“你可敢看着我的脸说这话，孩子！”

玛格丽特默然无语，垂下了眼睛。

“瞧，来了一对可人儿，”富尔克说。玛格丽特抬头望去，只见杰弗里和让娜（Jeanne）正悠悠穿过花园走来。杰弗里一条手臂环着让娜的腰，一头黑发低垂在她棕发的头上。

玛格丽特别过了脸，下巴绷得紧紧的。

“别发愁！”富尔克说。“西蒙会来的。喂，那边的！”

===Original===
The absorbed couple below started, and looked up.

“Is this the way thou dost mind thine affairs?” Fulk bellowed jovially.

“Ay!” Geoffrey answered. “So please you, sir, this is mine affair.”

“I am not at all,” Jeanne said with dignity. “I shall warn all maids ’gainst marriage. Husbands are very ungallant persons.” She looked up at Fulk. “Once I did think Geoffrey courtly and kind,” she said plaintively.

“And thou thinkest it no longer?” Margaret asked, smiling.

Jeanne shook her head mournfully.

===Chinese===
下边那对出神的恋人吓了一跳，抬起头来。

“你的正经事就是这么办的？”富尔克乐呵呵地吼道。

“正是！”杰弗里答道。“如您乐意这么说，老爷，这正是我的正经事。”

“我才不是呢，”让娜端着架子说。“我要告诫天下的姑娘千万别嫁人。做丈夫的个个都不解风情。”她抬头望着富尔克。“从前我还当杰弗里彬彬有礼、心地又好呢，”她哀声怨道。

“如今不这么想了？”玛格丽特含笑问。

让娜凄然摇了摇头。

===Original===
“He is a tyrant, madame. My life is misery.”

“What hath Geoffrey to say?” Margaret inquired.

He laughed up at her.

“Why, madame, that maids are sweet, but wives are shrews.”

“Oh!” Jeanne turned to pummel him.

Fulk’s great laugh rang out.

===Chinese===
“他是个暴君，夫人。我的日子苦不堪言。”

“杰弗里又有何话说？”玛格丽特问道。

他仰头朝她大笑。

“呃，夫人，我是说姑娘们甜美可爱，做了妻子可就成了泼妇。”

“噢！”让娜转身就捶他。

富尔克洪亮的笑声轰然响起。

===Original===
“There’s for you, Jeanne! God’s Body, kissing again? Margot, let us hence! My stomach turns at all this billing and cooing. Give me thine arm, child.” So they went away together.

“He﻿—he﻿—called me﻿—the Amazon,” Margaret said, as they crossed the hall.

“Simon? A murrain on him for a scurvy knave!”

She smiled faintly.

“And yet you love him.”

“I? What ails the girl? I love that roystering, obstinate young hothead? Now, by the troth﻿—”

===Chinese===
“打得好，让娜！上帝圣体，又亲起嘴来了？玛戈，咱们走吧！瞧着这番卿卿我我，我的胃里直泛酸。扶着我的胳膊，孩子。”于是两人一道去了。

“他——他——管我叫——亚马逊悍女（Amazon），”两人穿过大厅时，玛格丽特说。

“西蒙？那个卑鄙无赖，愿他遭瘟！”

她浅浅一笑。

“可您还是爱他的。”

“我？这丫头中了什么邪？我爱那个酗酒闹事、顽固不化的冒失小子？哼，凭真心起誓——”

===Original===
“Who is lying now?” Margaret said softly.

Fulk squeezed her arm.

“Thou hast me there. He is a good lad, when all is said and done. I do wish to see him happy, Margot.”

“Oh?”

“Ay. And think not that a pert, wilful lass who doth not know her own heart shall gainsay my lion-cub! Think it not, Margot!”

“I﻿—I﻿—am not that﻿—that wilful lass,” she said, very low.

===Chinese===
“这会儿撒谎的又是谁呢？”玛格丽特轻声说。

富尔克捏了捏她的胳膊。

“这一下你可拿住我了。说到底，他是个好小子。我是真心想看到他幸福，玛戈。”

“哦？”

“正是。所以别以为一个嘴尖任性、连自己心思都摸不清的丫头，就能违逆我家小狮子（lion-cub）！想也别想，玛戈！”

“我——我——可不是那个——那个任性的丫头，”她低低地说。

===Original===
“Are ye not? Who﻿—”

“For﻿—for﻿—I do know mine own heart well.”

“Then what is it?”

“Ah, I﻿—I shall not tell thee that.”

“So long as ye do tell it to Simon, I care not,” Fulk said gruffly.

===Chinese===
“不是么？那谁——”

“因——因为——我顶清楚自己的心思。”

“那是什么呢？”

“啊，我——我才不告诉你呢。”

“只要你肯讲给西蒙听，我才不在乎呢，”富尔克粗声粗气地说。

## XX / 第二十章



===Original===

How He Was Sent for by the King

===Chinese===

他如何奉国王之召

===Original===

Early in April the King spoke again to Alan of Simon. He called him up to his closet one evening, and smiled upon him, holding up a bulky packet of parchment sheets.

“Come hither, my Poet. These came today from my brother of Gloucester. Simon is alive and well.”

“God be praised!” Alan said devoutly. “What says his Grace, sire?”

“He says much,” Henry answered. “On the first day of the month he came to Cherbourg, and sat down before it. Listen! ‘But so well fortified and provisioned is the town that assault were folly. It but remains for me to lay siege to it, with your lordship’s gracious leave, that in time I may starve it into submission. As I judge this task will prove long and arduous, I think not to enter Cherbourg until the summer, if I do enter it then. Your Majesty’s well-beloved, Lord Simon of Beauvallet, whom I did send to aid Sir John Robsart in the taking of Carentan and St. Sauveur-le-Vicomte, did join me three days since with the news that the aforesaid towns have yielded to your Majesty. Beauvallet doth render good account of himself, and out of his whole force hath lost but seven men, three having died of sickness. I do beseech you, my dread Sovereign lord and brother, if you have need of Beauvallet, to send for him, for I have ample force, Huntingdon having come also to join me, from Coutances, which town did surrender to your puissant Majesty the Sixteenth day of March.’ ” Henry laid the parchment down. “This is good news, Alan.”

===Chinese===

四月初，国王又对艾伦（Alan）说起西蒙（Simon）。一天晚上，他召艾伦上自己的私室来，面带笑容，手里举着厚厚一沓羊皮纸文书。

“过来，我的诗人。这些是今日我王弟格洛斯特（Gloucester）送来的。西蒙活着，安然无恙。”

“赞美上帝！”艾伦虔诚地说。“公爵大人怎么说，陛下？”

“他说了许多，”亨利（Henry）答道。“本月一日他兵抵瑟堡（Cherbourg），已在城下安营围困。你听！‘然此城设防既固、粮秣亦足，强攻实为愚行。臣所余之事，惟待陛下钧旨恩准，将城合围，假以时日，迫其饥困请降。以臣度之，此役必旷日持久、艰苦异常；纵使夏日果能得手，臣亦不敢指望入夏之前便踏进瑟堡。陛下之爱臣、博瓦莱（Beauvallet）的西蒙大人，前奉我命往助约翰·罗布萨特爵士（Sir John Robsart）攻取卡朗唐（Carentan）与圣索沃尔-勒维孔特（St. Sauveur-le-Vicomte），已于三日前来会合，并报称上述诸城均已向陛下献降。博瓦莱甚能报效王事，所部全军折损不过七人，其中三人病殁。臣谨恳奏我威严的君上兼兄长：陛下若需博瓦莱听用，即请传召前来，盖我兵力充裕；亨廷登（Huntingdon）亦已前来会合——他自库唐斯（Coutances）来，该城已于三月十六日向威赫的陛下献城。’”亨利放下那页羊皮纸。“这是好消息，艾伦。”

===Original===

“Very good, sir, save that Cherbourg is so strong.”

“Gloucester will reduce it. Mine answer to his dispatch is here.” He touched a parchment-sheet. “I have sent to command Simon to join me, with his own men.”

Alan bowed.

“What hath your lordship for him then, sir?”

===Chinese===

“再好不过了，陛下，只可惜瑟堡守备太坚。”

“格洛斯特自会把它攻下来。给他的复书在此。”他碰了碰其中一页文书。“我已传令西蒙率本部人马来我这里会合。”

艾伦躬身一礼。

“那么您为他预备了什么职位呢，陛下？”

===Original===

Henry seated himself at the table.

“I have thought deeply on it, my Poet, and at last I have seen how I may serve both mine own ends and his. I will make Simon warden of this land.”

Alan’s eyes widened.

“Sire!”

===Chinese===

亨利在桌前坐下。

“此事我已思虑良深，我的诗人，如今终于看出如何既遂我自己的心愿，又成全于他。我要任西蒙做这片国土的守将（warden）。”

艾伦双目睁圆了。

“陛下！”

===Original===

“Thou dost know that I have a Chancery in the making, Alan. Morgan is to hold the seal of the Duchy, Luttrell is to be Seneschal. But at the head of the military government I will have Simon, for he is all a soldier, and his grip on all matters military is of iron. Thus shall he remain in Normandy. Art thou satisfied?”

Alan knelt gracefully, and kissed the King’s hand.

“Your Majesty is the kindest man alive,” he said. “It is no wonder that your very name is beloved.”

Henry pulled him up.

===Chinese===

“你是知道的，艾伦，我的文秘署（Chancery）正在草创。摩根（Morgan）将执掌公国玺印，勒特雷尔（Luttrell）将出任总管。而军政府之首，我属意西蒙——他是个彻头彻尾的军人，抓军务恰似铁腕。如此，他便留驻诺曼底（Normandy）。你可满意？”

艾伦优雅地跪下，吻了吻国王的手。

“陛下是当世最仁厚的君主，”他说。“难怪单是您的名讳便为人爱戴。”

亨利把他拽了起来。

===Original===

“Have done!” he said. “Malvallet and you shall be under Simon. Thus ye shall not be separated, and thus shall I know that my warden hath under him two men who will serve him faithfully, obeying his least command. I may march then upon Rouen with a quiet mind. Thank me not. If it please you, it doth also please me, save that I must lose my three Graces for a while.”

“I cannot thank you, sir,” Alan said fervently. “No words of mine could express what I do feel.”

Henry laughed.

“I am glad of it,” he said, and waved him away.

===Chinese===

“快别这样！”他说。“马尔瓦莱（Malvallet）和你都将隶属西蒙麾下。这样你们不至分离，我也可放心：我的守将手下有两位忠心侍奉的臣子，他最细微的号令亦一体遵行。我便能安心挥师鲁昂（Rouen）了。不必谢我。你们欢喜，我便也欢喜——只可惜我要暂失我那三位美惠之臣了。”

“我不知如何谢您才好，陛下，”艾伦恳切地说。“我的言语道不尽心中感佩。”

亨利大笑。

“那我便高兴了，”他说着，挥手叫他退下。

---

===Original===

Ten days later Simon rode into Bayeux, the men of Beauvallet and some of Montlice behind him. As he came through the streets he was lustily cheered, and when he raised his hand in stiff salute, the cheers redoubled, and flowers were flung down before him, and caps tossed high in the air. So he came to Henry’s quarters, and straightway went to where Alan lodged.

Alan sprang up as he entered, and clasped his hands for a long minute.

“My Simon!”

Simon smiled, and his fingers gripped Alan’s. Then he released the slim hands in his.

“All is well with thee?”

“Very well. And with thee?” Alan asked affectionately.

===Chinese===

十天后，西蒙驰入巴约（Bayeux），身后跟着博瓦莱的兵马和一部分蒙利斯（Montlice）子弟。他一路穿过街衢，人们纵情向他欢呼；待他僵直地举手致意，欢呼声愈发震天，鲜花掷落在他马前，帽子抛上了半空。他就这样来到亨利的行辕，随即径直去寻艾伦的住处。

艾伦见他进来，一跃而起，双手紧紧攥住他的手，足足好一会儿。

“我的西蒙！”

西蒙微微一笑，手指扣紧了艾伦的手。随后才松开握在掌中的那双纤手。

“你一切都好？”

“好得很。你呢？”艾伦满怀挚情地问。

===Original===

“Gloucester carried all before him. Ye did hear that St. Lo fell to Hungerford?”

“Ay. Gloucester sent word. Domfront holds firm against Warwick still.”

“So I thought. But Domfront will fall before Cherbourg hath lost one stone from its walls. What doth the King want of me?”

“He told thee not?”

“Nay. His dispatch was as short as he says mine are wont to be.” Simon drew it from the leathern pouch at his belt, smiling. “ ‘To our well-beloved servant, Simon of Beauvallet: It is our pleasure that you make all haste to join us here in our town of Bayeux, bringing with you the men of Beauvallet and Montlice. Henry R.’ There is more, written on the back. ‘And thus have I my revenge on thee, my Soldier. Have I stirred thy curiosity?’ ”

===Chinese===

“格洛斯特一路所向披靡。圣洛（St. Lo）落入亨格福德（Hungerford）之手，你们听说了吧？”

“听说了。格洛斯特传了信来。多姆弗龙（Domfront）仍牢牢顶住沃里克（Warwick）呢。”

“我也这么料想。只是瑟堡城墙上还没掉一块石头，多姆弗龙就会先撑不住了。国王召我所为何事？”

“他没告诉你？”

“没有。他那道公文之简短，正如他平素数落我的一般。”西蒙笑着从腰间皮囊里把它抽了出来。“‘致我们的爱臣、博瓦莱的西蒙：我们甚愿你尽速率博瓦莱与蒙利斯人马，前来巴约城与我们相会。亨利王（Henry R.）’背面还写着字。‘如此，我也算报了你一箭之仇了，我的武人。可曾勾起了你的好奇？’”

===Original===

Alan laughed.

“Well? Hath he done so?”

Simon shrugged.

“I suppose I am to join Clarence. All ways are one to me. Alan, what ails the troops? As I rode hither they did cheer me as though I had accomplished some great emprise. What means it?”

===Chinese===

艾伦大笑。

“怎么样？他勾起了没有？”

西蒙耸耸肩。

“想是叫我归入克拉伦斯（Clarence）麾下。于我而言，哪条路都一样。艾伦，兵士们这是怎么了？我一路驰来，他们欢呼不止，倒像我建下了什么旷世壮举（emprise）。这是什么缘故？”

===Original===

“The King will tell thee,” Alan answered. “Hast thou had word from Geoffrey?”

“Ay. Belrémy is at peace. The﻿—the Lady Margaret did banish her cousin for seeking to slay me.” His eyes gleamed suddenly.

“So I did hear. What does that betoken, think you?”

Simon did not answer.

“The Lady Margaret’s hate is not so strong, perchance,” Alan said gently.

“She hates me not. I’ll go change my raiment before I see the King.” He went out heavily.

===Chinese===

“国王自会对你说，”艾伦答道。“杰弗里（Geoffrey）有消息给你么？”

“有。贝雷米（Belrémy）已然安定。那位——那位玛格丽特夫人（the Lady Margaret）把她的表弟逐了出去，只因他图谋刺杀我。”他的眼睛倏地亮了。

“我也听说了。依你看，这说明什么？”

西蒙没有作声。

“玛格丽特夫人的恨意，或许没那么深了，”艾伦温声说。

“她并不恨我。我先去换身衣裳，再去觐见国王。”他脚步沉沉地走了出去。

===Original===

Just before supper a page came to him, to command his attendance on the King. He went at once, and entering the audience chamber, found Henry seated on a dais, with his Council about him.

Simon paused on the threshold, and shot a quick look round. Then he went forward, and bowed low to Henry.

“Your lordship sent for me, sir?”

“Ay.” Henry held out his hand. “I have work for thee, Simon.”

===Chinese===

晚饭前，一名侍童来传他觐见国王。他即刻前往，一进觐见厅，只见亨利坐在高台上，枢密院（Council）诸臣环侍左右。

西蒙在门槛前略一驻足，目光飞快地扫视四周。随即上前，向亨利深深一躬。

“您传召我前来，陛下？”

“嗯。”亨利伸出手来。“我有差事给你，西蒙。”

===Original===

Simon kissed his hand and released it.

“That is good news, sire.”

“Arduous work, my Soldier,” Henry warned him.

“I desire naught better, sir.”

===Chinese===

西蒙吻过他的手，随即放开。

“这是好消息，陛下。”

“是桩苦差事呢，我的武人，”亨利提醒他。

“我再乐意不过了，陛下。”

===Original===

“Give me the order, Philip,” Henry said to Philip Morgan, standing beside him.

Morgan placed a long scroll in his hand, which Henry gave to Simon.

“Thou wert appointed to this office three days since, Simon, by vote of Council and my will.”

Simon looked round again, slightly frowning. Then he bent his head over the parchment, and began to read. In grandiose terms it gave him to understand that it was the King’s most gracious command that he be appointed Lieutenant and Warden of the Lands and Marches of Normandy,1 to maintain the peace in the Duchy, and to have control over the troops that should be left therein, while the King went on to Rouen. Further, that it was the King’s most gracious will that he should have under him the following knights: A list of names met Simon’s eye, the first two of which were Sir Geoffrey of Malvallet and Sir Alan of Montlice. There was much more besides, and at the bottom of the scroll was Henry’s seal and signature with the signatures of each one of his Council beneath.

===Chinese===

“把诏令呈来，菲利普。”亨利对立在身侧的菲利普·摩根（Philip Morgan）说。

摩根把一卷长长的诏书放到他手中，亨利转手递给了西蒙。

“西蒙，三日之前，经枢密院议决并我的意旨，你已受任此职。”

西蒙又环顾一周，微微蹙起眉头。随即俯首就着羊皮纸读了起来。诏书以堂皇的辞令晓谕他：奉国王陛下至仁至慈之命，任命他为诺曼底领地与边地的副王兼守将（Lieutenant and Warden of the Lands and Marches of Normandy）1，以维持公国境内之安宁，并统辖国王挥师鲁昂后留驻该地之各路人马；又，奉国王陛下至仁至慈之意旨，下列骑士俱受其节制：一列名字映入西蒙眼帘，头两名便是马尔瓦莱的杰弗里爵士与蒙利斯的艾伦爵士。此外尚有诸多条款，卷末钤着亨利的御玺与亲笔签署，其下是他的枢密院每一位臣工的签押。

===Original===

Simon read on to the end. Then he looked up, straight into Henry’s eyes. A long breath he drew, and there was a wondering look on his face.

“Is this﻿—indeed your Majesty’s pleasure?” he asked quietly.

Henry bowed his head.

“Sir﻿—” Simon stopped, at a loss for words. “I﻿—think I have done little to deserve this great honour.”

===Chinese===

西蒙一气读到末尾。而后他抬起头来，直直望进亨利的双眼。他长长吸了一口气，脸上现出怔忡的神色。

“这——当真是陛下的圣意么？”他低声问道。

亨利点了点头。

“陛下——”西蒙住了口，一时找不出话来。“我——自觉功绩微薄，当不起这般殊恩。”

===Original===

A low murmur of dissent came from the Council. Henry nodded towards Luttrell, who rose. One by one the Council filed out, so that Simon was left alone with the King.

“Thou canst not refuse the task,” Henry said, and came down from the dais. “It is sealed and done. A man must I leave behind me, so I leave thee.”

“Refuse!” Simon laughed shortly. “I cannot tell you, sir, what this means to me. If you do indeed think me worthy of this command, I can only thank you from the bottom of my heart.”

Henry laid a hand on his arm.

“Thank me not. I serve myself. One thing I would suggest to thee.”

“What is it, sir?”

===Chinese===

枢密院中响起一阵低低的异议。亨利朝勒特雷尔颔首示意，勒特雷尔便起身。诸臣一个接一个鱼贯而出，厅中只剩下西蒙与国王相对。

“这差事你推不得，”亨利说着，走下高台。“诏书已钤印定案。我总得留下一个人替我镇守，留下的便是你。”

“推不得！”西蒙短促地一笑。“陛下，我说不尽这于我意味着什么。您若当真认为我堪当此任，我唯有自肺腑深处拜谢。”

亨利伸手按住他的手臂。

“不必谢我。我是为自己打算。只有一件事要提点你。”

“什么事，陛下？”

===Original===

“That ye dispose your lieutenants how ye will, but that you yourself make some central spot your headquarters. Belrémy seems a right good town, and one that is large and of important standing. Get thee to it, my Soldier.”

Simon looked sharply round at him, and his eyes narrowed.

“This is Alan’s doing,” he said.

===Chinese===

“你的副将如何部署，悉听尊便；但你本人，须择一处居中之地驻节坐镇。贝雷米便是座上好的城池，地方广大，地位冲要。就到那儿去，我的武人。”

西蒙霍然逼视着他，双眼眯细了。

“这是艾伦的主意，”他说。

===Original===

Henry shook his head.

“Nay, nay, fear not that I seek to favour thee, thou proud lord! It is my will. Further it is my will that ye espouse the Lady Margaret with all speed. Simon, thou rogue! Never was I more amazed than when I heard that love had come to thee! Love for the tigress!”

“Nay, sire!” Simon answered forcefully. “She is no tigress, but a brave lady!”

“An Amazon!”

“Nay, a babe, for all her years and stateliness.”

===Chinese===

亨利摇了摇头。

“不，不，你这骄傲的老爷，别怕我有意偏袒你！这是我的旨意。再者，我还望你从速迎娶玛格丽特夫人。西蒙，你这促狭鬼！听说爱情也降临到你头上，我还从未那样惊诧过呢！爱的还是那头母虎！”

“不，陛下！”西蒙断然答道。“她不是母虎，是位刚勇的夫人！”

“一个亚马逊悍女（Amazon）！”

“不，是个娃娃罢了，任凭她年岁不小、仪态威严。”

===Original===

Henry laughed at him.

“When I return from Rouen, I will see thy babe. Umfraville called her not that.”

“He knew her not,” Simon said, and smiled to himself.

Henry grasped his hand.

“God grant thee happiness, Simon. May thy lady be kind and gentle.”

===Chinese===

亨利笑他。

“我从鲁昂回来，倒要见识见识你的娃娃。乌姆弗拉维尔（Umfraville）可不这么叫她。”

“他不了解她，”西蒙说，暗自莞尔。

亨利紧紧握住他的手。

“愿上帝赐你幸福，西蒙。愿你的夫人仁厚温柔。”

===Original===

Again Simon laughed.

“Gentle she is not, sire, kind I will make her. She is wilful and fierce, and swift with her dagger. It is a fighting maid that I will take to wife, not easily won. I would not have it otherwise.”

“Thou must ever choose the hardest task,” Henry said in amusement. “As soon as may be thou shalt go to Belrémy, but there is work yet to be done. May shall see thee in thy lady’s arms. Wilt thou write to Geoffrey?”

“Nay, sir. I will take them by surprise, so that my lady shall have no time to remember her stubborn pride. And, as your lordship doth know, mine is no able pen.”

===Chinese===

西蒙又是一笑。

“温柔，她是不肯的，陛下；仁厚，我会让她学会。她任性，刚烈，匕首出手又快。我娶的是一位能征善战的烈性姑娘，可不是轻易赢得的。我偏愿意如此。”

“你呀，偏拣最难的差事，”亨利忍俊不禁。“你尽早便可以去贝雷米，只是眼下还有活计要办。到了五月，你就能把你夫人搂在怀里了。你要不要写信给杰弗里？”

“不必，陛下。我要给他们来个措手不及，好叫我的夫人来不及记起她那倔强的傲气。再者，您是知道的，我这笔头可不灵光。”

===Original===

Henry’s eyes twinkled.

“I have served thee out for thy curt dispatches, Simon.”

“I was not curious at all, sir,” Simon replied. “I thought your dispatch to me long enough. It told me that ye had need of me. What more should I wish to know?”

“God’s my life! Are ye turned courtier?” Henry exclaimed.

“Nay, I but spoke the truth,” Simon said, rather surprised.

===Chinese===

亨利眼中闪着狡黠的光。

“你那些简慢的公文，我这回也算回敬过你了，西蒙。”

“我压根儿没有好奇，陛下，”西蒙答道。“我只觉得您那道公文已经够长的了。它告诉我，您用得着我。我还想知道什么呢？”

“上帝的性命！你改行做朝臣了不成？”亨利失声嚷道。

“不，我不过是说了实话，”西蒙答道，颇为诧异。

## XXI / 第二十一章

===Original===
How He Came to His Own

===Chinese===
他如何得到属于他的一切

===Original===
The Lady Margaret stood by the sundial in her pleasaunce, gazing wistfully down at it. It was May now, and all about her flowers bloomed, while the trees in the orchard, beyond the hedge, were laden with blossom. The sun shone warmly down upon the garden, and the birds sang, but the Lady Margaret was sad.

For a long time she stood motionless, thinking of one day in February when she had come running to this spot to warn Simon of danger. And as she thought, she smiled a little, drearily, and brushed her hand across her eyes. No word had come from him since March, and although Geoffrey made light of it, saying that Simon would never write unless he were forced to do so, Margaret felt the silence ominous, and feared she knew not what.

Today she was strangely nervous, jumping at every sound, as though she expected something to happen. Even now she lifted her head, listening, for it seemed to her that far away in the town some excitement was on hand. The faint noise died, but it came again presently, and she heard the echo of Fulk’s great voice, wafted to her by the gentle breeze. A deep breath she drew, and stood very still, hands clenched at her sides until the knuckles gleamed. She looked towards the entrance to the pleasaunce, lips slightly parted, and in her eyes were dread and hope.

===Chinese===
玛格丽特夫人（Margaret）伫立在游憩园（pleasaunce）的日晷旁，怅然俯望着它。时已五月，她四周繁花盛开，树篱之外果园里的树也都缀满了花朵。暖阳和煦，照着园林，鸟儿啼啭，玛格丽特夫人却满心愁绪。

她一动不动地立了许久，想着二月里的那一天——她曾奔到这处来向西蒙（Simon）示警。想着想着，她凄然一笑，抬手拭过眼角。自三月以来，他音信全无；杰弗里（Geoffrey）虽把这事看得轻淡，说西蒙不逼到份上是绝不肯提笔的，玛格丽特却觉得这沉默不祥，怀着一种连自己也说不清的惧怕。

今日她异样地心神不定，稍有声响便是一惊，仿佛等着要出什么事似的。这会儿她又抬起头来侧耳倾听——在她听来，远处的城里似乎正起一阵骚动。那隐约的喧声歇了下去，不多时却又响起，富尔克（Fulk）那洪亮嗓音的回声乘着微风飘飘荡荡送到她耳中。她深深吸了一口气，站定不动，双手在身侧攥得紧紧的，直到指节泛出白光。她望向游憩园的入口，双唇微启，眼里交织着惶惧与盼望。

===Original===
And at length a soft tread reached her straining ears, and her knees seemed suddenly to shake. Round the bend in the alley that led to the pleasaunce, Simon came, and paused some few yards from her, looking at her from under his jutting brow.

The Lady Margaret stood very still; only her bosom rose and fell quickly, and her eyelids flickered. She gazed in dumb longing at the fair giant before her, but she could not speak.

Simon’s deep voice reached her, and she quivered with a kind of fearful joy.

===Chinese===
终于，一记轻缓的脚步声传到她凝神细听的耳中，她的双膝猛然发软。通向游憩园的小径转过弯处，西蒙来了，在离她几步远的地方停住，从那突出的眉骨底下注视着她。

玛格丽特夫人静静立着，只有胸口急促地起伏，眼帘不住地颤。她痴痴望着眼前这位俊美的巨人，一个字也说不出。

西蒙低沉的声音传到她耳中，她不由得一阵战栗——那是掺杂着惧意的欢喜。

===Original===
“Willingly shalt thou come to me, and willingly give thy heart,” he said, and held out his arms.

The Lady Margaret took a faltering step forward, impelled by some invincible force. Her hands flew out, trembling.

“Milor’!” she whispered. “Thou hast﻿—come back!”

===Chinese===
“你必会心甘情愿地到我身边来，心甘情愿地交出你的心，”他说着，张开了双臂。

玛格丽特夫人被一股不可抗拒的力量催动，踉跄着向前迈了一步。她伸出双手，抖个不住。

“英国老爷（milor’）！”她低唤，“你——回来了！”

===Original===
“Ay, I have come as I swore I would. To lead thee to the altar.”

A sob broke from her, but it was a glad sob. She came to him, swiftly, stumblingly, her eyes full of tears.

“My heart﻿—was thine﻿—long since!” she said brokenly. “Willingly﻿—do I﻿—come!”

===Chinese===
“是啊，我践誓而归。要引你到圣坛跟前。”

一声呜咽从她喉头迸出，却是欢喜的呜咽。她朝他奔去，急急切切，跌跌撞撞，两眼蓄满了泪。

“我的心——早就是——你的了！”她语声破碎，“我心甘情愿——来——到你身边！”

===Original===
Then she was caught in a great embrace, swept off her feet, and crushed against Simon’s breast. She gripped the folds of his tunic with her slender hands, face upturned, half-crying and half-laughing.

“Thou art﻿—with me again! Ah, Simon, Simon, I knew not what to think! I feared﻿—Simon, milor’!”

His arms tightened ruthlessly about her. For one moment he looked down into her brimming eyes, his own ablaze with some newborn passion, then he bent and kissed her fiercely, on her eager mouth. And now, at last, the Lady Margaret returned his kisses, her pride dead, and all her fighting instincts flown.

===Chinese===
随即她被一条有力的臂膀拦腰拢住，双脚离地，紧紧贴上西蒙的胸膛。她纤细的手指攥住他上衣的褶皱，仰起脸，又哭又笑。

“你又——回到我身边了！啊，西蒙，西蒙，我都不知道该怎么想才好！我怕——西蒙，英国老爷！”

他的手臂不由分说地收紧。他低头凝望她盈盈欲泪的双眼，自己的眸子里燃着某种新生的情焰，随即便俯下身去，狠狠吻上她热切的双唇。而这一回，玛格丽特夫人终于回吻了他——她的傲气已然死灭，一身的斗志尽数散尽。

===Original===
So for a while they stayed thus, locked in each other’s arms, till the grip about Margaret’s shoulders slackened, and she was set upon her feet, breathless and quivering.

“My﻿—queen!” Simon said huskily, and knelt suddenly to kiss the hem of her gown.

The Lady Margaret looked down at him, and in her face was all the wonder of love. Gently she laid a hand on the bent head, and put her other into his, drawing him to his feet.

===Chinese===
两人就这样相拥了半晌，直到箍在玛格丽特肩头的手臂缓缓松开，她才被放回地上，气喘吁吁，浑身微颤。

“我的——王后！”西蒙喑哑地说道，蓦地屈膝跪下，去吻她的裙裾。

玛格丽特夫人低头望着他，脸上写满爱的惊奇。她温柔地把一只手覆在那低垂的头颅上，另一只手递入他的掌中，扶他站起。

===Original===
“Simon, oh, milor’, kneel not to me! It is I who am ’neath your heel!” She sank against his shoulder, and laughed unsteadily. “I swore vengeance on thee! Undying vengeance!” she whispered. “I said that I would make thee rue the day thou didst cross my path. Ah, Simon, Simon!”

His arms were round her once again, holding her close.

“Mayhap I shall live to rue that day,” he said, and his rare humour peeped out. “Undying thy vengeance shall be, and on our marriage-day it will be complete.”

===Chinese===
“西蒙，哦，英国老爷，别向我下跪！该伏在你脚边的才是我！”她软软倚上他的肩窝，笑声发颤。“我曾对你立誓复仇！不死不休的复仇！”她低语，“我说过，要叫你懊悔挡了我道的那一天。啊，西蒙，西蒙！”

他的手臂再度环拢她，紧紧抱住。

“没准儿我真会活着懊悔那一天，”他答道，难得一露的诙谐探出头来。“你的复仇自会不死不休；到我们成婚那日，便告圆满了。”

===Original===
“Oh, ungallant!” she cried, and put up her hand to touch his lean cheek. “Thou most cruel of lovers! Was﻿—was ever a maid so harshly wooed?”

“Was ever a maid so hardly won?” he retorted, and carried her hand to his lips. “Thou tigress! Wilt thou stab me, I wonder, if ever I gainsay thee?”

“Never again!” she said softly. “I could not do it﻿—that day in January, though I hated thee then. How should I stab thee now that my hate has turned to love? I would follow thee barefoot across the world!”

===Chinese===
“哦，好个不解风情的！”她嚷道，抬手抚上他清瘦的面颊。“你这情人里头最狠心的一个！可曾——可曾有哪位姑娘，被人这样狠巴巴地求过？”

“可曾又有哪位姑娘，赢得这般艰难？”他回敬道，把她的手引到唇边。“你这母虎（tigress）！倘若哪天我违逆了你，不知你会不会捅我一刀？”

“再也不会了！”她柔声说。“那一日我就下不了手——一月里的那一天，虽说那时我还恨着你。如今恨既化作了爱，我又怎么下得去？你便赤着脚走遍天涯，我也跟着你！”

===Original===
“Nay, for if I walked across the world, thou wouldst lie in mine arms, Margot. Never again shalt thou flee from me.”

“Thy strong arms﻿ ﻿… !” she murmured. “Even as thou didst bear me from Raoul’s palace. Stern, merciless conqueror! Simon, *mon maître et mon seigneur*!”

It was a long time before they left the pleasaunce, and then they went slowly, Simon’s arm about his lady’s waist, her head resting back against his shoulder, and her hand in his.

===Chinese===
“不必，我要是真走遍天涯，你也只会躺在我的臂弯里，玛戈（Margot）。你再也休想从我身边逃开。”

“你那有力的臂膀……！”她喃喃道。“就像你把我从拉乌尔（Raoul）的宫邸里劫出来时那样。严厉无情的征服者！西蒙，*我的主上与我的君侯*（mon maître et mon seigneur）！”

好久之后两人才离开游憩园，走得很慢：西蒙的手臂环着夫人的腰，她的头向后倚在他肩上，手握在他的掌心里。

===Original===
“I never thought to be so happy!” she sighed. “I never dreamed that I would bend to your will!”

“I must have loved thee from the moment I set eyes on thee,” Simon answered.

“What! Was it love then, that made thee mar my skin?” she pressed his hand to the scar on her breast.

===Chinese===
“我从没想过竟能这般幸福！”她喟叹，“做梦也没想到，我竟会屈从你的意志！”

“打从第一眼瞧见你，我就必定是爱上你了，”西蒙答道。

“什么！原来那竟是爱？爱得你来毁我的皮肉？”她把他的手按在自己胸前的疤上。

===Original===
“I know not. Thou wert a statue made of ice.”

“An Amazon thou didst call me! But oh, thy sword hurt!”

He bent to kiss the scar.

===Chinese===
“我不知道。那时你是一座冰雕的像。”

“你还骂我是亚马逊悍女（Amazon）！可是哎哟，你的剑真疼！”

他俯下身去，吻那道疤。

===Original===
“An Amazon thou wert, who flinched not nor cried out. How could I have treated thee so?”

“Ah, no, I am glad! I said that for as long as the scar remained I would remember thy cruelty, and so I will, and with it mine own attempted treachery. Simon, that shame will never die!”

“My shame is greater, Margot, for I threatened a woman, a child.”

===Chinese===
“你那时确是位亚马逊悍女，不退缩，也不喊出声。我那时怎能那样待你？”

“啊，不，我倒是庆幸！我说过，这疤还在一日，我便记得你的狠心一日——我会记着的，连同我自己那桩未遂的背信。西蒙，那份耻辱至死也不会消！”

“我的耻辱更深，玛戈，因为我威胁的是一个女子，一个孩子。”

===Original===
“No child am I, milor’. Just﻿—just an Amazon.”

He laughed down into her pleading eyes.

“That rankles still, my queen. I would not have thee aught but that. I did tell my King that the lady I love is a tigress, beautiful beyond words, swift with her dagger, proud and indomitable to her foes, but with a great heart, and a brave spirit.”

===Chinese===
“我才不是孩子，英国老爷。只是——只是一介亚马逊悍女罢了。”

他低头笑着，望进她那双恳求的眼眸。

“这话还扎在你心里呢，我的王后。我不要你有半点更改，就要你这样。我确实对国王说了：我所爱的夫人是一只母虎，美得难以言表，匕首出手如飞，对仇敌骄傲不屈，却怀一颗宽厚的心，一副无畏的胆魄。”

===Original===
Margaret blushed.

“Nay. I am not so fine. I have failed in all that I meant to do, and only succeeded in one thing. And that I did not mean to do. I stole what men thought was not there to steal. Thy cold heart, monseigneur. I swore to bring an army about your ears, and behold, I was foresworn. I tried to keep my hatred for thee alive, but it withered. See how thou hast humbled me!”

Simon drew her closer.

===Chinese===
玛格丽特红了脸。

“不。我没有那么好。凡我存心要做的，全都落了空，只有一件事成了，而那件偏偏不在我的盘算之内。我偷走了世人以为无从偷起的东西——你那颗冷心，大人（monseigneur）。我赌咒要引一支大军杀到你耳朵根子底下，看哪，我却自食其言。我竭力要让我对你的恨活下去，它却枯萎了。瞧瞧，你把我这傲气挫成了什么样！”

西蒙把她搂得更紧了。

===Original===
“One mistake didst thou make, dear heart. Thou didst set thy will against mine, for I had sworn to vanquish and to wed thee.”

“How vain my fight hath been!” she sighed. “In everything was I beaten, till thou hadst me at thy feet. And even then I would not realise, though Jeanne knew, and my Lord Fulk roared at me for a pert, wilful baggage. A silly maid, he called me, and bade me know that Simon of Beauvallet was not one to be worsted by an obstinate woman.”

Simon smiled.

===Chinese===
“你只错了一桩，心肝。你拿你的意志来抗我的意志，因为我早已立誓：既要降服你，又要娶你为妻。”

“我这一场抗争何其枉然！”她喟叹，“事事我都败了，直到你教我拜倒在你脚下。便是到了那时我还不肯醒悟，虽然让娜（Jeanne）早已看穿，富尔克老爷还冲我咆哮，骂我是个不知礼数、任性妄为的小贱货（baggage）。他管我叫傻丫头，要我认清：博瓦莱（Beauvallet）的西蒙，可不是会被一个犟女人挫败的人。”

西蒙微微一笑。

===Original===
“If my lord hath called thee names, then doth he love thee indeed.”

“Oh, he hath not a good word to say for me, but bellows at me until I tell him that he is wrongly named, and should be the Bull, not the Lion. There is only one Lion.” She drew his hand to her cheek. “Thy King will let thee stay with me? Thou wilt not go forth again?”

“My King hath made me lieutenant of the troops he leaves in Normandy, Margot. Thou wilt never be rid of me again, but when he returns from his campaign I will show him a gentle, docile English wife.”

===Chinese===
“父亲大人既肯这样骂你，那他便是真心疼你了。”

“唉，他嘴里就没一句我的好话，只顾冲我吼，直到我回敬他说，他这名号起错了，该叫‘公牛’才是，不兴叫‘狮子’。真正的狮子只有一个。”她把他的手拉到自己颊边。“你的国王会让你留在我身边么？你不会再出征了吧？”

“国王已命我做他留驻诺曼底（Normandy）之师的统帅，玛戈。你这辈子再也甩不脱我了；等他远征归来，我要让他看看一位温婉驯顺的英国妻子。”

===Original===
“Nay, ’tis I who will show him a tamed husband. Thou shalt be Count of Belrémy, and rule my land﻿—thy land now.”

“And when I take thee to England thou shalt be the Lady Baroness of Beauvallet, for all I have is thine.”

They had come now to the castle, and went into the great hall, hand in hand. Geoffrey and Jeanne were there, waiting for Simon to bring his lady in, and Fulk was standing by Alan, one arm flung around his son’s shoulders. He and Jeanne came forward, Jeanne running to her friend, Fulk waving his stick at Simon.

===Chinese===
“不，该是我领一位驯服了的丈夫给他看。你要做贝雷米（Belrémy）的伯爵，统我的领地——如今是你的领地了。”

“等我带你回英格兰（England），你就是博瓦莱的男爵夫人（Lady Baroness of Beauvallet），因为凡我所有，尽皆是你的。”

两人这时已到城堡，携手走进大厅。杰弗里和让娜已在厅中等着西蒙引夫人进来；富尔克站在艾伦（Alan）身旁，一条胳膊搂着儿子的肩膀。他和让娜迎上前来：让娜奔向她的好友，富尔克则朝西蒙挥着手杖。

===Original===
“So there thou art!” he roared. “First it is Geoffrey and his Jeanne, kissing and fondling until I am made sick by the sight of it, and now thee, thou good-for-naught, and Margaret, the graceless lass! Hadst thou no more sense than to thrust thy head into the halter, thou silly lad? Let me get hold on thy hand, I say!” He wrung it vigorously, his little blue eyes twinkling ferociously. “Always thou must conquer! I could weep when I think how none hath ever withstood thee! Small wonder is it that thou art a conceited coxcomb. Margot, thou rogue, come to me!” He embraced her noisily, shaking her to and fro. “What did I tell thee? Did I not say that my lion-cub would master thee? I warrant he will tame thy hot blood, saucy maid!” He rounded on Simon again, smiting him fondly on the shoulder. “Now I do say that if she sticks the dagger into thee, it will be but thy just deserts, lad! We will see what a slip of a girl may do to thee! Oh, thou art well-matched! A pair of fools, by my troth!”

===Chinese===
“好啊，你总算来了！”他吼道。“先是杰弗力和他的让娜，又亲又搂，看得我大倒胃口；如今又添上你这没出息的玩意儿，还有玛格丽特这个没规没矩的丫头！你这傻小子，难道就不长点脑子，偏要把脑袋往笼头里钻？把手伸过来，听见没有！”他攥住那只手使劲摇撼，一双蓝色的小眼睛凶巴巴地直闪。“你偏是什么都要赢！一想到从没有人能挡得住你，我简直要落泪了！难怪你成了个自负的纨绔子（coxcomb）。玛戈，你这小淘气，过来我这边！”他咋咋呼呼地搂住她，前后直摇。“我当初怎么说的？我不是说过，我这只小狮子（lion-cub）早晚收服得了你？我担保，他准能驯服你那身火性子，辣丫头！”他又转过身来对着西蒙，亲热地在他肩上擂了一拳。“我说，往后她要是把匕首捅了你，那也是你罪有应得，小子！咱们走着瞧，看一个小丫头片子能把你怎么样！哦，你们俩真是天造地设！凭真心起誓（by my troth），一对傻瓜！”

===Original===
“Shouting and blustering again!” Margaret said severely. “Thy gout will plague thee more than ever, and that will be *thy* just deserts!”

Fulk laughed delightedly, never so pleased as when Margaret chided him.

“Oh, she will school thee, Simon! Never was there so determined a lass! God’s Body, I never thought to get me a daughter so much after mine own heart!”

===Chinese===
“又在大呼小叫、吹胡子瞪眼了！”玛格丽特正色道。“你的痛风会比你从前犯得更凶，那才是*你*罪有应得！”

富尔克乐呵呵放声大笑——玛格丽特抢白他的时候，正是他最高兴的时候。

“哦，她会来管教你的，西蒙！从没有哪个姑娘这么有主意！上帝圣体（God’s Body），我从没想到能得一个这么合我心意的女儿！”

===Original===
Margaret pushed him into a chair, dropping a kiss upon his brow.

“A Bull and a Lion,” she said. “What will my life be betwixt you? What with thy passions and my lord’s obstinacy﻿—oh, Jeanne, am I not beset?”

Simon was kissing Jeanne’s hand, in congratulation on her marriage. She dimpled, looking mischievously into his eyes.

===Chinese===
玛格丽特把他按进椅子里，在他额上落下一吻。

“一头公牛，一头狮子，”她说。“夹在你们两个中间，我这日子可怎么过？一边是你的火暴性子，一边是我这位老爷的犟劲——哦，让娜，我岂不是四面受敌？”

西蒙正吻着让娜的手，贺她新婚。她脸上绽出笑靥，眼中含着促狭望进他的眼睛。

===Original===
“I shall warn Margot to have none of thee, milor’﻿—I will tell her﻿—oh, terrible things about a husband’s tyranny!”

Geoffrey laid his hand on Simon’s arm.

“Simon, mark well my words! Wives are the devil﻿—and I know!”

===Chinese===
“我要提醒玛戈，可别沾你的边，英国老爷——我要告诉她——哦，把丈夫如何专横的可怕故事讲给她听！”

杰弗里把手搭上西蒙的手臂。

“西蒙，记牢我的话！妻子个个是魔鬼——这我可有切肤之知！”

===Original===
“In truth,” Alan sighed, “I am the only wise one amongst us all.”

“Art a silly lad!” Fulk rumbled, and cast him an affectionate though fiery glance.

“Alan speaks sooth for once,” Simon said, and placed his finger on Margaret’s indignant lips. He had her in his arms again, and like a needle to the magnet, Jeanne had drawn near to her Geoffrey. “For Alan throughout hath known that needs must I fall, and at Margot’s feet.”

===Chinese===
“说实在的，”艾伦叹道，“咱们这伙人里，就数我一个明白人。”

“你是个傻小子！”富尔克闷雷似的吼了一声，斜他一眼，火辣辣的，却满含疼爱。

“艾伦这一回可说了实话，”西蒙说着，把手指按在玛格丽特怫然撅起的唇上。他已再度把她拥进怀里；让娜也像针为磁石所引，早挨到了她的杰弗里身旁。“因为艾伦自始至终都明白：我横竖免不了要栽跟头，而且是栽在玛戈的脚下。”

===Original===
“Ah, and he knew that I loved thee, even before I knew it myself,” Margaret cried. “Methinks he hath worked very quietly to bring about our happiness. And yet he will not seek his own.”

“I observe thy folly,” he said, “and know mine own wisdom. That is happiness.”

Jeanne looked at Geoffrey, and a smile passed between them, of boundless conceit. Margaret stole her hand into Simon’s, smiling also. Not one of them answered Alan, and he laughed, leaning on his father’s shoulder, and surveying his two friends with soft, satisfied eyes.

===Chinese===
“啊，他还早在我自己明白之前，就知道我爱着你呢，”玛格丽特嚷道。“依我看，他一直在不动声色地撮合我们的好事。可轮到他自己那份儿，他偏不去求。”

“我冷眼旁观你们的痴愚，”他说，“亦深知我自身的明智。这便是幸福。”

让娜望向杰弗里，两人之间递过一个自负无比的微笑。玛格丽特悄悄把手探进西蒙的掌心，也同样笑着。没有一个人答理艾伦，他倚着父亲的肩头笑起来，目光温柔而餍足，打量着他的两位朋友。

===Original===
“Are my sage words beneath contempt?” he asked.

“Ay,” Simon answered simply, and looked down into Margaret’s face for a long moment. A deep breath he drew, and glanced again at Alan. “Beneath contempt,” said Simon the Coldheart.

===Chinese===
“我这番金玉良言，竟不值一哂么？”他问。

“正是，”西蒙坦然答道，低头凝望玛格丽特的脸，久久不移。他深深吸进一口气，又瞥了艾伦一眼。“不值一哂，”‘冷心’西蒙如是说。

===Original===
To

			the memory of

			**my father﻿—

			this,

			his favorite**.

===Chinese===
献给

			**亡父——

			谨以此书，

			这是

			他生前最钟爱的作品**。

## Endnotes / 尾注

===Original===
- 
					

As a matter of stern, historical fact this post was held successively by the Earls of March and Salisbury. —﻿Author’s note ↩️

===Chinese===
- 
					

就严峻的史实而言，此职先后由马奇伯爵（Earl of March）与索尔兹伯里伯爵（Earl of Salisbury）担任。——作者注 ↩️
